### [1] [Seeing Time: Benchmarking Chronological Reasoning and Shortcut Biases in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05702)
*Haoyu Zhou,Qing Qing,Caichong Li,Qixin Zhang,Yongcheng Jing,Ziqi Xu,Juncheng Hu,Xikun Zhang,Renqiang Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoVision基准，专门评估视觉语言模型（VLMs）的时序推理能力，通过三个专门构建的数据集（长期历史相似物体、多样化事件类型、跨模态时间敏感文本）进行测试，发现VLMs常依赖灰度/颜色等表面线索而非真正的时序特征进行判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLMs在理解复杂视觉语义方面取得了显著进展，但其在时序推理（chronological reasoning）方面的能力尚未得到充分探索，缺乏专门评估VLMs跨图像时序感知与推理能力的基准。

Method: 构建三个专门数据集：1）长期历史跨度中视觉相似的物体；2）按不同事件和物体类型分类的数据；3）图像与时间敏感新闻文本配对用于跨模态对齐。通过广泛实验分析模型在不同类别上的性能差异，并检测模型是否依赖"错误捷径"（如图像颜色而非真实时序特征）。

Result: VLMs表现出一定潜力，但频繁利用表面线索（如灰度与彩色滤镜）来绕过真实的时序推理过程，而非基于真正的时序特征进行判断。

Conclusion: 通过提供高质量数据集和严格评估框架，该研究为识别当前VLMs在时序推理方面的局限性提供了诊断工具，并引导开发更稳健、逻辑基础更强的多模态模型。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly enhanced their ability to interpret complex visual semantics, yet their capacity for chronological reasoning remains under-explored. In this paper, we introduce a novel benchmark specifically designed to evaluate how VLMs perceive and reason about chronological information within and across images. Unlike existing video-based benchmarks that focus on frame sequencing, our work delves into the underlying logic of chronological judgment and the expansion toward multimodal integration. To facilitate this, we construct three specialized datasets: one containing visually similar objects spanning long historical durations, another categorized by diverse event and object types, and a third pairing images with time-sensitive news text for cross-modal alignment. Through extensive experiments, we analyze whether models exhibit performance disparities across categories and, crucially, explore whether they rely on ``incorrect shortcuts'', such as image color rather than genuine chronological features. Our results reveal that while VLMs show promise, they frequently exploit superficial cues like grayscale versus color filters to bypass authentic chronological reasoning. By providing these high-quality datasets and a rigorous evaluation framework, we offer a diagnostic tool to identify current limitations and guide the development of more robust, logically grounded multimodal models. The source code is shown in https://github.com/LuoRenqiang/ChronoVision.

</details>


### [2] [Learning Visual Spatial Planning from Symbolic State via Modality-Gap-Aware Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06076)
*Haocheng Luo,Jiahui Liu,Ruicheng Zhang,Zhizhou Zhong,Jiaqi Huang,Zunnan Xu,Quan Shi,Jun Zhou,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MGSD，一种两阶段模态差距感知自蒸馏框架，通过冷启动接地阶段和特权教师蒸馏阶段，解决视觉语言模型在视觉空间规划中存在的感知-推理模态差距问题。实验表明，该方法在4B和8B骨干网络上分别提升19.3%和18.4%的宏观平均性能，显著缩小了与符号输入上界的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在通用多模态理解上表现优异，但在视觉空间规划任务中仍存在困难。作者将其归因于感知-推理模态差距：视觉规划需要模型从像素中推断潜在状态结构并进行多步推理，而符号规划直接利用明确的物体和约束。这造成了视觉状态恢复和多步规划的双重瓶颈。

Method: MGSD包含两个阶段：1) 冷启动接地阶段，为视觉学生模型提供可靠的状态表示，减少早期感知噪声；2) 特权教师蒸馏阶段，利用显式符号状态通过在线策略蒸馏监督学生模型自身的视觉 rollout 前缀，从而传递规划能力。符号数据仅用于训练，推理时完全基于视觉输入。

Result: 在视觉规划基准测试中，MGSD在4B和8B骨干网络上分别将宏观平均性能提升19.3%和18.4%，缩小了与符号输入上界的差距。消融实验和诊断分析证实，性能提升来源于视觉状态恢复和最优路径推理两方面的改进。

Conclusion: 模态差距感知的自蒸馏方法不仅改善了模型对可行动作状态的感知能力，也提升了其在推断结构上进行规划的能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: While vision-language models excel at general multimodal understanding, they still struggle with visual spatial planning. We attribute this to a perception-reasoning modality gap: visual planning requires models to infer latent state structures from pixels and then reason over the recovered structure to produce valid actions, whereas symbolic planning directly leverages explicit objects and constraints. This creates dual bottlenecks in visual state recovery and multi-step planning. To address this, we propose MGSD, a two-stage modality-gap-aware self-distillation framework. First, a cold-start grounding stage equips the visual student with reliable state representations, minimizing early perception noise. Second, a privileged teacher transfers planning capabilities via on-policy distillation, using explicit symbolic states to supervise the student's own visual rollout prefixes. Crucially, symbolic data is used strictly during training, leaving inference purely visual. Experiments on visual planning benchmarks show that MGSD consistently improves visual planning across both 4B and 8B backbones, raising the macro average by 19.3% and 18.4%, respectively. The resulting models narrow the gap to symbolic-input upper bounds, while ablations and diagnostics confirm that the improvement comes from both visual state recovery and optimal-path reasoning. These results suggest that modality-gap-aware self-distillation improves not only how models perceive actionable states, but also how they plan over the inferred structure. Code is available at https://github.com/Oranger-l/MGSD.

</details>


### [3] [GuardNet: Ensemble Strategies of Shallow Neural Networks for Robust Prompt Injection and Jailbreak Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05566)
*Paulo Ricardo Ferreira Neves,Edson Rodrigues da Cruz Filho,Paulo Henrique Eleuterio Falsetti,João Vitor Pavan,Ian Degaspari,Henrique Vieira Laturrague,Patrick Vieira Laturrague,Guilherme Nielsen Dias,Marccello Wilson Perez Berto,Gustavo Voltani Von Atzingen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GuardNet是一个基于BiLSTM集成（约4700万参数）的防护系统，用于检测Prompt Injection和Jailbreak攻击。研究证明对抗鲁棒性更多依赖于样本覆盖多样性和阈值校准而非模型规模。GuardNet在盲测集上AUROC达0.747，专有基准上F1达0.92，CPU延迟约50ms，适合低算力生产部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM面临Prompt Injection和Jailbreak攻击威胁，且基准评测存在数据污染和信息泄露问题。现有大模型方案成本高、延迟大，需探索轻量级防护方案。

Method: 构建基于BiLSTM的浅层神经网络集成系统（GuardNet），共约4700万参数。通过多样本覆盖和阈值校准提升鲁棒性，而非依赖模型规模。

Result: 盲测JBB-Behaviors基准上AUROC达0.747（n=200），专有基准上F1达0.92（n=50）。CPU平均延迟约50ms。但Mistral-7B和Llama-3.1-8B在F1和AUROC上仍更优。

Conclusion: 对抗场景下鲁棒性更依赖样本覆盖多样性和阈值校准而非模型规模；GuardNet在轻量级方案中竞争力强，适合资源受限的生产环境部署。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed natural language processing, but they remain vulnerable to Prompt Injection (PI) and Jailbreak (JB) attacks. In addition, benchmark evaluations may be affected by contamination and partial information leakage, compromising performance estimates. This work presents GuardNet, a guardrail system based on an ensemble of shallow neural networks (BiLSTMs) with approximately 47 million parameters. We investigate the hypothesis that robustness in adversarial scenarios depends more on the diversity of example coverage and threshold calibration than on model scale. The results indicate that GuardNet achieves competitive performance compared with lightweight detectors and high efficiency at low latency, although larger LLMs such as Mistral-7B and Llama-3.1-8B still achieve superior performance in terms of F1 score and AUROC on the blind JBB-Behaviors benchmark. Nevertheless, GuardNet achieves an AUROC of 0.747 on the blind dataset (n = 200) and an F1 score of 0.92 on a proprietary benchmark (n = 50), under threshold calibration and evaluation with declared partial information leakage. The system operates with an average latency of approximately 50 ms on CPU, making it suitable for deployment in production environments with cost and infrastructure constraints.

</details>


### [4] [LeanMarathon: Toward Reliable AI Co-Mathematicians through Long-Horizon Lean Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05400)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Yuekai Sun,Taiji Suzuki,Jason D. Lee,Fanghui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeanMarathon 是一个用于研究级 Lean 自动形式化的多智能体框架。其核心是演化蓝图——一个同时充当形式化证明骨架、自然语言证明图和共享系统记录的 Lean 文件。四个契约范围的智能体进行审计、证明和修复，由两阶段编排器协调。在四个 Erdős 问题的评估中，它成功形式化了全部 7 个目标定理，证明了 258 个引理和定理，无任何 sorry。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长跨度研究数学的自动形式化不仅难以处理困难引理，更面临规模性问题：语句漂移、依赖纠缠、上下文衰减、局部修复破坏远端工作。现有方法缺乏在长时间形式化过程中保持目标保真度的耐用框架。

Method: 提出 LeanMarathon 框架：(1) 演化蓝图作为核心抽象，同时承载形式化证明骨架、自然语言证明图和系统记录；(2) 四个契约范围智能体（构造、审计、证明、修复）负责不同任务；(3) 两阶段编排器：先通过对抗性审查稳定目标保真度，再以并行 CI 门控轮次从动态叶子节点向上处理证明 DAG。

Result: 在两个研究论文的四个 Erdős 问题（#1051, #1196, #164, #1217）上评估，三次自主运行均成功形式化全部 7 个目标定理，证明 258 个引理和定理，无任何 sorry 遗留。

Conclusion: 可靠的 AI 协同数学不仅需要更强的证明器，更需要能够跨长数学发展维护目标保真度的耐用编排框架。LeanMarathon 将脆弱的单次长时间运行分解为多个局部的、可恢复的、并行的交易。

Abstract: Long-horizon autoformalization of research mathematics fails not only at hard lemmas, but at scale: statements drift, dependencies tangle, context decays, and local repairs corrupt distant work. We present LeanMarathon, a multi-agent harness for reliable research-level Lean autoformalization. Its core abstraction is an evolving blueprint: a Lean file that serves simultaneously as formal proof skeleton, natural-language proof graph, and shared system of record. Four contract-scoped agents construct, audit, prove, and repair this blueprint. These agents are coordinated by a two-stage orchestrator that first stabilizes target fidelity through adversarial review and then discharges the proof directed acyclic graph (DAG) from its dynamic leaves upward in parallel CI-gated rounds. LeanMarathon turns one brittle multi-hour run into many local, recoverable, parallel transactions. We evaluate LeanMarathon on two recent research papers spanning four Erdős problems (#1051, #1196, #164, #1217). Across three autonomous runs, it formalizes all seven target theorems with no sorry, proving 258 lemmas and theorems. These results show that reliable AI co-mathematics requires not only stronger provers, but durable harnesses that preserve target fidelity across long mathematical developments. The code can be found at https://github.com/YuanheZ/LeanMarathon.

</details>


### [5] [Harnessing Generalist Agents for Contextualized Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05404)
*Zihao Li,Kaifeng Jin,Yuanchen Bei,Jiaru Zou,Avaneesh Kumar,Xuying Ning,Yanjun Zhao,Mengting Ai,Baoyu Jing,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeClaw是一个面向时间序列的智能体框架，为通用LLM智能体提供原生运行时支持，使其能够执行上下文化的时序推理。该框架集成了可执行时序工具、经验驱动的能力进化机制和 episodic 多模态记忆，在多个真实领域基准上取得了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列常嵌入丰富的上下文中，且实际工作流中预测等任务仅是更广泛解决方案的一环。现有通用AI智能体主要在文本空间操作，未能充分对齐结构化时序信号，缺乏针对时序数据原生运行时支持。

Method: TimeClaw包含三个核心组件：1) 可执行时序工具，提供可溯源的分析能力；2) 经验驱动的能力进化机制，用于创建可复用的分析例程；3) episodic 多模态记忆，用于检索相关推理轨迹。三者协同实现开放式的上下文化时序推理。

Result: 在能源、金融、天气、交通等多个真实领域基准上的评估显示，TimeClaw相比基线方法取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: TimeClaw通过为通用LLM智能体提供时序原生运行时支持，有效弥合了通用AI与结构化时序信号之间的鸿沟，为端到端的时序分析工作流提供了一种可行的智能体框架方案。

Abstract: Time series are often embedded in rich contexts that are essential for holistic modeling. Moreover, real-world practitioners often require end-to-end workflows for analyzing temporal dynamics, where widely studied tasks such as forecasting are only one step in a broader solution loop. While generalist AI agents offer a promising interface for such workflows under complex contexts, they still operate primarily in textual spaces that are not fully aligned with structured temporal signals. In this work, we introduce TimeClaw, an agentic harness framework for time series that equips generalist LLM agents with the time series-native runtime support needed for contextualized temporal reasoning. TimeClaw integrates executable temporal tools for grounded and auditable analysis, experience-driven capability evolution for creating reusable analytical routines, and episodic multimodal memory for retrieving relevant reasoning traces. Together, these components unlock harnessed open-ended temporal reasoning with contextual information. Extensive evaluation on multiple benchmarks covering diverse tasks across energy, finance, weather, traffic, and other real-world domains demonstrates improved performance of TimeClaw. Code is available at https://github.com/iDEA-iSAIL-Lab-UIUC/TimeClaw.

</details>

Guo,Erika J Schneider,Zhiyong Lu,Mark Mueller,Radha Poovendran,Somayeh Sojoudi,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agents' Last Exam (ALE)，一个由250+行业专家协作构建的基准测试，用于评估AI代理在长期、经济价值高的真实世界任务上的表现。ALE涵盖55个子领域、13个行业集群、1000+任务，目前最难层级平均完全通过率仅2.6%，旨在弥合基准测试成功与GDP相关影响之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在广泛基准测试上取得了优异成绩，但这些成果并未转化为许多专业领域中有经济意义的实际部署。作者认为这一差距主要是评估问题：现有基准测试缺乏对真实且经济价值高的工作流进行持续性能测量。

Method: 与250+行业专家合作，基于O*NET/SOC 2018美国联邦职业分类体系，构建覆盖非体力行业的长周期、经济价值高的真实世界任务基准ALE。采用任务分类法，包括55个子领域、13个行业集群和1000+任务，并设计为持续增长的动态基准。

Result: 在最难层级的任务上，当前主流框架和骨干配置的平均完全通过率仅为2.6%，表明该基准远未饱和。

Conclusion: ALE旨在作为一个动态基准，通过持续吸纳新工作流和行业，弥合基准测试成功与GDP相关经济影响之间的差距，而非单纯增加一个排行榜。

Abstract: Recent AI systems have achieved strong results on a wide range of benchmarks, yet these gains have not translated into economically meaningful deployment across many professional domains. We argue that this gap is largely an evaluation problem: widely used benchmarks lack sustained performance measurement on real and economically valuable workflows. This paper introduces Agents' Last Exam (ALE), a benchmark designed to evaluate AI agents on long-horizon, economically valuable, real-world tasks with verifiable outcomes. Developed in collaboration with 250+ industry experts, ALE covers non-physical industries defined with reference to O*NET / SOC 2018 (the U.S. federal occupational taxonomy). It is organized around a task taxonomy with 55 subfields grouped into 13 industry clusters covering 1K+ tasks. Current results show that the hardest tier remains far from saturated: across mainstream harness and backbone configurations, the average full pass rate is 2.6%. ALE is designed as a living benchmark: its task pool grows continuously as new workflows and industries are onboarded. More broadly, ALE is intended not merely as another leaderboard, but as an instrument for closing the gap between benchmark success and GDP-relevant impact.

</details>


### [7] [Fix the Mind, Not the Move: Interpretable AI Assistance via Knowledge-Gap Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05602)
*Ayano Hiranaka,Ya-Chuan Hsu,Stefanos Nikolaidis,Erdem Bıyık,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SENSEI是一个能从交互行为中推断用户误解并提供精准、最小化且充分建议的框架，在三个长周期任务中展现出零样本组合泛化能力，用户研究显示能纠正90%的学生误解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI助手通过行为反馈（如警报、触觉提示）纠正人类的次优操作，但这种方法只能缓解即时错误，无法解决导致重复错误背后的根本误解。因此需要一个能识别并纠正用户深层误解的框架。

Method: SENSEI框架基于结构化知识表示，从交互行为中推断用户误解，提供定位到错误根源的、最小且充分的针对性建议。它不同于传统的行动级或轨迹级干预，而是对知识表示层面进行操作。

Result: 在三个包含多种误解及对应行为的长周期任务中，SENSEI实现了零样本组合泛化，即使仅在单误解案例上训练，也能解耦多个重叠误解。用户研究表明该方法能识别真实的人类误解，成功纠正90%的学生误解，提升了长周期任务表现。

Conclusion: SENSEI通过推断并纠正用户深层误解的方式，比传统行为级干预更有效地促进了人机协作中的长期学习效果，并在泛化能力和实际有效性上取得了显著成果。

Abstract: AI assistants in human-AI collaboration often correct suboptimal human actions through behavioral feedback (e.g., alerts or steering-wheel nudges in assistive driving). Such interventions can mitigate immediate errors, but long-term improvement requires addressing the underlying misconceptions that cause repeated mistakes. We introduce SENSEI, a framework that infers user misconceptions from interaction behavior and provides targeted, minimal yet sufficient suggestions to correct them. Our approach departs from action- or trajectory-level interventions by operating over a structured knowledge representation to localize and correct the sources of erroneous behavior. Across three long-horizon tasks with diverse misconceptions and corresponding behaviors, SENSEI demonstrates zero-shot compositional generalization, disentangling multiple overlapping misconceptions despite training only on single-misconception cases. A user study further shows that our method identifies real human misconceptions and provides effective guidance that improves long-horizon task performance, successfully correcting $90\%$ of student misconceptions. Code and project page are available at https://misoshiruseijin.github.io/SENSEI/.

</details>


### [8] [Self-Commitment Latency: A Reward-Free Probe for Prompted Implicit Hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05625)
*Bonan Shen,Youting Wang,Dingyan Shang,Tao Ning*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"自我承诺延迟"指标，通过检测推理上下文过早承诺模型最终答案的行为来识别隐式奖励黑客行为，无需奖励模型或外部评判器，在GSM8K数据集上达到0.878 AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的隐式奖励黑客行为难以审计，因为其思维链看似良性，最终答案却可能被提示捷径锚定。现有验证器探头需要任务特定的奖励信号，限制了其通用性。

Method: 提出"自我承诺延迟"指标，测量提示推理上下文多早会承诺到模型自身的最终答案。在GSM8K数据集上使用Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct-4bit模型，对比普通提示和包含答案提示的提示，采用首次承诺延迟（阈值0.8）、承诺范围和平均未承诺质量等指标进行评估。

Result: 含提示的上下文比诚实上下文更早且更低不确定性地承诺。首次承诺延迟AUROC达0.878，承诺范围AUROC达0.926，平均未承诺质量AUROC达0.904。信号在两种提示条件都回答正确时更强，且在不同阈值下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 捷径可用的推理上下文会留下早期行为承诺签名，无需奖励模型、外部评判器或训练分类器即可检测。

Abstract: Implicit reward hacking is hard to audit when a language model's chain of thought appears benign: a final answer may be anchored by a prompt shortcut while the written reasoning still resembles ordinary problem solving. Verifier-based probes expose such behavior by measuring how early truncated reasoning contexts obtain high reward, but require a task-specific reward signal. This paper proposes a weaker-input alternative, self-commitment latency, which measures how early a prompted reasoning context commits to the model's own final answer. We evaluate the probe in a controlled paired GSM8K setting using Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct-4bit, comparing ordinary prompts with prompts that include an answer hint. Hinted contexts commit substantially earlier and with lower uncertainty than honest contexts. The primary latency metric, first-commitment latency at threshold 0.8, reaches AUROC 0.878; supporting whole-curve summaries reach AUROC 0.926 for commitment range and 0.904 for mean uncommitted mass. The signal is stronger when both prompt conditions answer correctly and remains stable across thresholds. These results show that shortcut-available reasoning contexts can leave an early behavioral commitment signature detectable without a reward model, external judge, or trained classifier.

</details>


### [9] [Beyond Output Matching: Preserving Internal Geometry in NVFP4 LLM Distillatio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05682)
*Fangbo Tu,Junhua Zhao,Chi Liu,Xin Chen,Haifeng Wu,Jian Wan,Srinivasan Manoharan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CKA-QAD方法，通过在NVFP4低精度量化感知蒸馏中引入CKA引导的表征对齐正则化项，解决传统KL散度输出匹配导致的层内表征退化问题，显著提升大语言模型在推理和编码任务上的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度推理（如NVFP4）在大模型部署中需求增长，但量化感知蒸馏（QAD）仅通过KL散度匹配输出分布会掩盖内部表征退化，导致推理和编码任务出现瓶颈，亟需保留内部几何结构的恢复方法。

Method: 首先通过CKA分析揭示KL-only QAD在层间表征相似性上的退化问题；提出CKA-QAD，在蒸馏过程中加入轻量级正则化项，通过对齐逐层Gram矩阵来保持内部表征几何结构。

Result: 在Nemotron 3 Nano和Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507上，CKA-QAD显著改善了表征对齐，提升了下游推理和编码任务的准确率，且训练开销适度。

Conclusion: CKA引导的表征对齐是输出匹配的有效补充，为低比特量化LLM恢复提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Demand for low-precision inference, including NVFP4-based approaches, has grown as large language models are increasingly deployed in latency and cost constrained production environments. Quantization-aware distillation (QAD) helps recover accuracy lost under low bit quantization by training a quantized student to match the output distribution of a frozen higher precision teacher via a KL-divergence loss. In this work, we first provide a representation level diagnosis of QAD: output matching alone can mask internal degradation, because many intermediate activation geometries can yield similar teacher-aligned logits. Using CKA, we show that KL-only QAD can reduce layerwise representational similarity relative to the BF16 teacher, with especially severe drift in RL-post-trained models. This drift correlates with downstream bottlenecks on reasoning and coding tasks, suggesting that low bit recovery requires preserving internal geometry rather than matching outputs alone. Motivated by this finding, we propose \textbf{CKA-QAD}, a CKA-guided representational alignment method for NVFP4 QAD and low bit LLM accuracy recovery. The method adds a lightweight regularizer that preserves internal representational geometry during distillation by aligning layerwise Gram matrices through CKA. Across Nemotron 3 Nano and Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507, CKA-QAD substantially improves representational alignment and improves downstream reasoning and coding accuracy with modest training overhead. Our findings position CKA-guided representational alignment as a practical complement to output matching for quantized LLM recovery.

</details>


### [10] [Critic-Guided Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Reasoning for Reliable Mathematical Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05704)
*Muhammad Talha Sharif,Abdul Rehman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于评判的异构多智能体框架，通过生成器-验证器结构和评判驱动的自适应学习系统，提升大语言模型在复杂数学推理中的可靠性和准确性，在GSM8K基准上实现了最高13%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在复杂数学推理中仍存在幻觉、中间推理错误以及结果不可靠的问题，亟需提升推理过程的可靠性和可解释性。

Method: 采用异构多智能体协作框架，集成多个不同专长的LLM智能体；构建生成器-验证器架构，验证器不仅判断正确性还提供批判性反馈以指导重新生成；通过评判驱动的自适应学习系统基于中间反馈评估和引导推理过程，实现自适应错误修正和防止错误级联。

Result: 在GSM8K基准上，该方法相比单次推理和非评判模型实现了最高13%的准确率提升；消融实验表明性能提升主要来自评判反馈循环而非模型规模；异构性和评判机制使小模型也能达到大模型的性能水平。

Conclusion: 结合异构多智能体协作与批判性反馈机制能够构建更可靠、可解释的数学推理系统，且该方法可降低对大型模型的依赖。

Abstract: Recent Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown impressive reasoning abilities; but they are still susceptible to hallucinations, intermediate reasoning mistakes, and unreliable reasoning results in complex mathematical reasoning problems. In this study, we introduce a critic-based heterogeneous multi-agent approach to improve the dependability of mathematical reasoning. This framework incorporates several LLM agents of different specialties and employs a critic-driven adaptive learning system to assess and guide the reasoning process based on intermediate feedback. The system adopts a generator-validator framework, with the validator not only determining correctness but also offering critiques to guide regeneration of solutions. This allows for adaptive error correction and prevents error cascading. Our experiments on the GSM8K benchmark show that the proposed method achieves up to 13% accuracy improvement over single-shot and non-critic models. Additionally, findings suggest that heterogeneity and critique reduce the need for large models, allowing smaller models to perform on par. Ablation studies reveal the main performance gains are due to the critic-based feedback loop and not model size. In summary, the proposed approach showcases the benefits of combining heterogeneous multi-agent collaboration and critique to obtain reliable and interpretable reasoning systems.

</details>


### [11] [Can LLMs Write Correct TLA+ Specifications? Evaluating Natural-Language-to-TLA+ Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05792)
*Arslan Bisharat,Brian Ortiz,Eric Spencer,Khushboo Bhadauria,TaiNing Wang,George K. Thiruvathukal,Konstantin Laufer,Mohammed Abuhamad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个系统性评估LLM生成TLA+规范的研究。30个LLM在205个规范数据集上测试，句法正确率最高26.6%，语义正确率仅8.6%。模型规模不预测质量，DeepSeek r1:8b反超70B版本。代码专用模型因负迁移表现不佳。识别出五类幻觉，均源于训练数据偏差。结论：当前LLM无法在没有专家监督下生成可靠TLA+规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLA+已在亚马逊、微软等公司用于工业验证，但从自然语言编写正确TLA+规范仍需大量时间和专业知识，限制了该技术的广泛采用。LLM虽有潜力，但此前缺乏对LLM生成语义正确的TLA+规范的系统性评估。

Method: 系统评估30个LLM（8个系列），包括25个开源模型在4种提示策略下（2600次运行）和5个闭源模型在少样本提示下（130次运行），使用205个TLA+规范的数据集。所有生成结果经SANY解析器和TLC模型检查器验证。

Result: LLM句法正确率达26.6%，语义正确率仅8.6%。渐进式提示是成功的关键。模型规模不预测质量，DeepSeek r1:8b在所有策略上优于70B版本。代码专用模型因主流语言训练的负迁移持续表现不佳。识别出五类可追溯至训练数据偏差的幻觉。

Conclusion: 当前LLM无法在没有专家监督的情况下生成可靠的TLA+规范。推理对齐对形式化语言的重要性高于模型规模。代码专用模型的负迁移现象值得关注。发布了评估框架、代码和数据集以促进可重复性和未来研究。

Abstract: TLA+ has supported industrial verification at companies such as Amazon and Microsoft, yet writing correct TLA+ specifications from natural language still requires time and expertise, which limits adoption. LLMs show promise, but no prior study measures whether they produce semantically correct TLA+ specifications from natural language. This paper presents the first systematic evaluation of LLM-based TLA+ specification synthesis from natural language. Our study evaluates 30 LLMs across eight families on a curated dataset of 205 TLA+ specifications: 25 open-weight models across four prompting strategies (2,600 runs) and 5 proprietary models under few-shot prompting (130 runs), all validated by the SANY parser and TLC model checker. LLMs achieve up to 26.6% syntactic correctness but only 8.6% semantic correctness, with successes exclusive to progressive prompting. Results show that model size does not predict quality, e.g., DeepSeek r1:8b outperforms its 70B variant across all strategies, which suggests the importance of reasoning alignment for formal languages. Code-specialized models consistently underperform due to negative transfer from mainstream language training. We identify five recurring hallucination categories, all traceable to specific training data biases. These results suggest that current LLMs do not generate reliable TLA+ specifications without expert oversight. We release the evaluation framework, code, and dataset to support reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [12] [Retrospective Harness Optimization: Improving LLM Agents via Self-Preference over Trajectory Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05922)
*Wenbo Pan,Shujie Liu,Chin-Yew Lin,Jingying Zeng,Xianfeng Tang,Xiangyang Zhou,Yan Lu,Xiaohua Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种自监督方法RHO，通过利用历史轨迹优化AI智能体的技能/工具/工作流（称为harness），无需真实标注数据即可提升智能体性能。在SWE-Bench Pro上单轮优化将通过率从59%提升至78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体优化方法依赖带真实标签的验证集，但在实际部署场景中难以获取此类标注数据。因此需要一种无需外部标注、仅利用历史轨迹就能持续改进智能体能力的方法。

Method: RHO包含三个关键步骤：(1) 从历史轨迹中选取具有挑战性的多样化核心任务集；(2) 并行重新求解这些任务；(3) 通过自我验证（self-validation）和自我一致性（self-consistency）分析新轨迹，生成候选harness更新方案，并利用成对自偏好（pairwise self-preference）选择最优方案。

Result: 在软件工程（SWE-Bench Pro从59%→78%）、技术工作和知识工作三个领域均有效。优化的harness能有效针对历史失败模式，改变智能体行为模式，并在长周期会话中保持更高准确率。

Conclusion: RHO证明了仅利用历史轨迹进行自监督优化可以显著提升AI智能体性能，无需外部标注数据。该方法能针对性修复历史失败模式，实现持续改进。

Abstract: AI agents rely on a harness of skills, tools, and workflows to solve complex problems. Continually improving this harness is essential for adapting to new tasks. However, existing optimization methods typically require ground-truth validation sets, yet such labeled data is difficult to acquire in practical deployment settings. To address this problem, we introduce Retrospective Harness Optimization (RHO), a self-supervised method that optimizes the agent harness using only past trajectories. Specifically, RHO selects a diverse coreset of challenging tasks from past trajectories and re-solves them in parallel. The agent analyzes these rollouts using self-validation and self-consistency, then generates candidate harness updates and selects the most effective one by its own pairwise self-preference. We evaluate RHO across three diverse domains, spanning software engineering, technical work, and knowledge work. Notably, a single optimization round improves the pass rate on SWE-Bench Pro from 59% to 78% without any external grading. Furthermore, our analysis demonstrates that RHO effectively targets prior failure modes. As a result, the optimized harness alters the agent's behavior patterns and sustains higher accuracy during long-horizon sessions.

</details>


### [13] [A Pre-Registered Causal Partition of Self-Consistency Elicitation and Reward Design in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05932)
*Yuze Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明RLVR中常用的naive估计量（准确率差）存在系统性偏差，因为它混淆了自我一致性激发效应和真正的奖励设计信号。作者通过tabular-GRPO模拟器推导出精确的 telescoping 分解，并通过预注册因子实验和已发表论文的再审计验证了这一分解的诊断价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践者常用 naive = acc(TRUE) - acc(RANDOM) 作为奖励设计效应的估计量，但该估计量存在系统性偏差，因为它将自我一致性激发效应（通过多数伪奖励将策略向模态答案收紧）与真正的奖励设计信号混为一谈。需要一种更精确的分解方法来分离这两种效应。

Method: 使用受控的tabular-GRPO模拟器推导出精确的 telescoping 分解：total = null + elicit + rd；通过五个先验强度水平测量每个项；进行预注册的2x2x2因子实验验证非加性；对两篇已发表论文进行再审计。

Result: 奖励设计分数在弱先验（ps=0.20）时为0.139，在强先验（ps=0.80）时降至0.05，激发项在自我一致性交叉点翻转符号。预注册因子实验确认非加性（交互比0.385；AxC效应-0.089）。再审计结果分别判定为"激发主导"（激发份额0.98）和"奖励设计主导"（rd份额1.18）。

Conclusion: naive估计量不可靠，需使用telescoping分解进行诊断。强先验区域可点识别，近交叉区域只能有界识别。无论翻转结果如何都提交论文（非翻转同样是重要发现）。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves reasoning even when the reward signal is spurious -- assigning credit to the group-plurality answer rather than a ground-truth verifier. Practitioners commonly interpret naive = acc(TRUE) - acc(RANDOM) as the reward-design effect. We prove this estimand is systematically biased: it conflates self-consistency elicitation (sharpening the policy toward its modal answer via majority pseudo-reward) with genuine reward-design signal. Using a controlled tabular-GRPO simulator we derive an exact telescoping decomposition total = null + elicit + rd and measure each term across five prior-strength levels. The reward-design fraction of the naive estimator ranges from 0.139 at weak prior (ps=0.20) to 0.05 at strong prior (ps=0.80), with the elicitation term flipping sign at the self-consistency crossover. A pre-registered 2x2x2 factorial confirms non-additivity (interaction ratio 0.385; AxC effect -0.089). A points-vs-bounds pilot gate shows strong-prior regimes are point-identified while near-crossover regimes are only bounded. Re-audits of two named published results yield ELICITATION DOMINATED (elicitation share 0.98) and REWARD DESIGN DOMINATED (rd share 1.18) verdicts respectively, demonstrating the diagnostic value of the partition. We pre-commit to submit regardless of flip outcome; a non-flip is a finding of equal standing. We release a reusable one-command harness for any alignment paper to run the same audit.

</details>


### [14] [RedditPersona: A Modular Framework for Community-Conditioned LLM Adaptation from Reddit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06027)
*Amirhossein Ghaffari,Ali Goodarzi,Huong Nguyen,Simo Hosio,Lauri Lovén,Ekaterina Gilman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了RedditPersona框架，标准化了社区条件语言模型适配中的数据收集、社区定义和评估流程。通过对112个子版块（30万+用户、1600万+评论）的实验，发现五种分组策略的行为可识别性与其对子版块基线的内在一致性相关，且在所有策略中可识别性与分布相似性之间存在一致的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社区条件语言模型适配研究在数据收集、社区定义和评估方面各自独立做出选择，导致难以比较不同研究的假设或复用已有成果，亟需一个标准化的框架来统一这些流程。

Method: 构建RedditPersona模块化框架，包含：1）收集Reddit帖子和评论；2）对活跃用户进行画像；3）采用五种分组策略（基于子版块、图结构、语义、混合和基于交互）划分用户；4）通过QLoRA为每种策略训练参数高效适配器；5）在统一的指标集（涵盖流畅性、忠实度、分布对齐和社区可识别性）下进行评估。

Result: 在112个子版块（301,429个用户画像、1600万+条评论）上的实验表明，适配器的行为可识别性反映了每种策略与子版块基线的内在一致性，且在所有五种策略中，可识别性与真实文本的分布相似性之间存在一致的权衡关系。

Conclusion: RedditPersona框架成功标准化了社区条件语言模型适配的关键环节，揭示了不同分组策略下可识别性与分布相似性之间的系统权衡，为后续社区语言模型研究提供了可复用的基础设施和比较基准。

Abstract: Community-conditioned language model adaptation requires choices about data collection, community definition, and evaluation that are currently made independently in each study, making it hard to compare assumptions or reuse artifacts. We present RedditPersona, a modular framework that standardizes these choices: it collects Reddit posts and comments, profiles active users, partitions them under five grouping strategies (subreddit-based, graph-structural, semantic, hybrid, and interaction-based), trains a parameter-efficient adapter per strategy via QLoRA, and evaluates them under a shared metric suite spanning fluency, fidelity, distributional alignment, and community identifiability. Applied to 112 subreddits in the urban well-being domain (301,429 user profiles, 16M+ comments), we find that adapters' behavioral identifiability tracks each strategy's intrinsic agreement with the subreddit baseline, and that a consistent trade-off between identifiability and distributional similarity to real text holds across all five strategies. The code and configuration files are available at: https://github.com/Ahghaffari/redditpersona.

</details>


### [15] [Integrating Mechanistic and Data-Driven Models for Neurological Disorders through Differentiable Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06094)
*Shah Pallav Dhanendrakumar,Saikat Pal,Sitikantha Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了混合建模策略（将深度学习与基于物理的求解器结合）在神经系统疾病建模中的应用，重点介绍了残差建模、神经常微分方程（NODE）和求解器循环三种方法，并论证了其在脑肿瘤、阿尔茨海默病和中风等疾病诊断、预后和治疗规划中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机制模型虽能提供科学洞见，但常因简化假设或计算开销大而受限；纯数据驱动方法虽快速可扩展，但依赖高质量数据且缺乏可解释性和泛化能力。混合建模旨在结合两者优势，克服各自局限性。

Method: 采用视角论文形式，系统梳理了混合建模策略的结构化分类（并行、串联、并行-串联架构），重点阐述了三种主要方法：残差建模（补充缺失或不完整的物理过程）、神经常微分方程（NODE，用于连续时间动力学近似）和求解器循环（用神经近似加速传统求解器）。

Result: 混合模型将基于微分方程的公式化建模与深度学习相结合，能够更准确地刻画神经系统疾病的演化过程，在诊断准确性、疾病进展预测和治疗策略制定方面均优于单独的机制模型或纯数据驱动方法。

Conclusion: 混合建模在脑肿瘤、阿尔茨海默病和中风等神经系统疾病的进展建模和治疗反应预测中展现出强大潜力，是实现个性化神经建模的有力工具。

Abstract: Advances in computational modeling, neuroimaging, and artificial intelligence are revolutionizing the modeling of neurological disorders for improved diagnostics, prognosis, and treatment planning. Mechanistic models provide valuable scientific insight into the disorders, but in practice they are often simplified with assumptions or computationally expensive and slow to solve. However, while purely data driven approaches provide speed and scalability, they require large, high quality data to train and generally suffer from interpretability and generalization issues. This perspective paper presents a structured overview of hybrid modeling strategies, which combine deep learning models with physics based solvers, and are categorized into parallel, series, and parallel-series architectures. Three main approaches that have been emphasized are residual modeling for missing or incomplete physics, Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (NODEs) for continuous time dynamics approximation, and solver in the loop that accelerates traditional solvers with neural approximations. These hybrid models integrate the governing differential equation based formulations and deep learning to characterize the evolution of neurological disorders, and promise advanced personalized neurological modeling. In addition, the study explores and proposes different hybrid configurations to improve diagnosis accuracy, predict disease progression, and inform treatment strategies across a range of neurological disorders. These capabilities outperform standalone mechanistic or purely data driven approaches, making hybrid modeling a powerful tool, especially in applications involving modeling the progression and treatment responses in neurological conditions such as brain tumors, Alzheimer's disease, and stroke.

</details>


### [16] [Step-adaptive multimodal fusion network with multi-scale cloud feature learning for ultra-short-term solar irradiance forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06102)
*Jingxin Zhang Xiaoqin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对超短期太阳辐照度预测中现有方法无法捕捉云层空间动态、多尺度云特征表示不足以及固定低频补偿策略不适应不同预测步长的问题，提出了一种融合地基云图多尺度空间特征、步长自适应低频补偿与气象时序特征的多源数据融合模型，在公开数据集和实际光伏电站数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超短期太阳辐照度预测方法存在三个关键缺陷：单一时间序列模型无法捕捉复杂条件下的云层空间动态；标准卷积对多尺度云特征的表示能力不足；固定的低频补偿策略无法适应不同预测步长的需求。因此需要提出一种能融合多源数据、自适应调节特征的新模型。

Method: 提出多源数据融合模型：首先使用InceptionNeXt从地基云图中提取多尺度、多方向的空间特征；然后引入步长自适应低频补偿单元，根据预测步长动态调节全局低频信息；最后将增强后的图像特征与气象时间序列特征结合，通过TempAttnLSTM网络捕获全局时间依赖关系实现多步预测。

Result: 在公开NREL数据集和山东实际光伏电站数据上的实验表明，所提方法相较于多种当前最优方法具有更好的预测性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的多源数据融合模型通过结合InceptionNeXt空间特征提取、步长自适应低频补偿和TempAttnLSTM时序建模，有效解决了现有超短期太阳辐照度预测中的空间动态捕捉、多尺度特征表示和自适应补偿等关键问题，提升了预测精度。

Abstract: Ultra-short-term solar irradiance prediction is critical for photovoltaic system dispatch and power grid stability. Existing approaches suffer from three key shortcomings: single time-series models cannot capture the spatial dynamics of clouds under complex conditions, standard convolutions inadequately represent multi-scale cloud features, and fixed low-frequency compensation strategies fail to adapt to different prediction steps. To address these issues, this proposes a multi-source data fusion model for ultra-short-term irradiance prediction. The model first employs InceptionNeXt to extract multi-scale, multi-directional spatial features from ground-based cloud images. A step-adaptive low-frequency compensation unit is then introduced to dynamically modulate global low-frequency information based on the prediction step. Eventually, the enhanced image features are combined with meteorological time-series features, and a TempAttnLSTM network captures global temporal dependencies for multi-step prediction. Experiments on the public NREL dataset and practical photovoltaic stations in Shandong illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed method compared with several state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [17] [Unsupervised Pattern Analysis in Japanese Veterinary Toxicology: A Regulatory-Compliant Framework for Cross-Species Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06207)
*Yukiko Kawakami,Mohammad Shirazi,Ryo Shimizuwa,Saito Shinoda,Alireza Mortazavi,Matsumoto Kawahara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种融合日本监管框架（MAFF）的无监督学习框架，利用NVAL数据库中的4,120份高置信度ADE报告（9,080个药物-ADE组合），通过器官系统编码、物种偏差校正、聚类与降维，识别出具有统计学意义的物种特异性毒性模式（如伴侣动物肝脏毒性、反刍动物肾毒性、绵羊皮肤敏感性），药物聚类与药理分类对齐率达83%，余弦相似性表现最优（轮廓系数0.48，聚类精度87%），并经监管验证证实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有兽药警戒系统未能捕获由当地生物学和监管环境决定的区域特异性毒性模式，且多数研究依赖预测模型，缺乏机制可解释性。

Method: 基于NVAL数据库，将ADE编码为器官系统对齐表示，针对物种报告偏差进行校正，应用相似性度量的聚类与降维方法识别潜在毒性结构。

Result: 识别出三个显著物种簇（p<0.01）：伴侣动物肝脏主导模式（0.42±0.06）、反刍动物肾毒性（0.39±0.07）、绵羊皮肤敏感性（0.35±0.07）；药物聚类与药理分类对齐率达83%；余弦相似性优于其他度量（轮廓系数0.48，聚类精度87%）。

Conclusion: 监管对齐的无监督分析能够揭示具有生物学意义的区域特异性毒性模式，为兽药安全性评估提供一个可解释、可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Veterinary pharmacovigilance systems are essential for monitoring adverse drug events (ADEs), yet existing approaches often fail to capture region-specific toxicity patterns shaped by local biological and regulatory contexts. In Japan, these challenges are amplified by species-specific metabolic differences and reporting practices defined by the Ministry of Agriculture, Forestry, and Fisheries (MAFF). Most prior work relies on prediction-oriented models, limiting mechanistic interpretability. This study proposes a regulatory-integrated unsupervised framework for pattern discovery using the National Veterinary Assay Laboratory (NVAL) database. ADEs are encoded into organ system-aligned representations and adjusted for species-specific reporting biases, enabling cross-species comparison. Similarity-based clustering and dimensionality reduction are applied to identify latent toxicity structures. Analysis of 4,120 high-confidence ADE reports (9,080 drug-ADE combinations) identified three significant species clusters (p < 0.01), including hepatic-dominant patterns in companion animals (0.42 $\pm$ 0.06), renal toxicity in ruminants (0.39 $\pm$ 0.07), and dermatological sensitivity in sheep (0.35 $\pm$ 0.07). Drug-level clustering achieved 83% alignment with pharmacological classes, while cosine similarity outperformed alternative metrics (silhouette score: 0.48; cluster precision: 87%). Regulatory validation showed strong agreement with established classifications. These findings demonstrate that regulation-aligned unsupervised analysis can uncover biologically meaningful, region-specific toxicity patterns, providing an interpretable and scalable framework for veterinary drug safety assessment.

</details>


### [18] [Boosting Brain-to-Image Decoding with TRIBE v2 Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06345)
*Yohann Benchetrit,Marlène Careil,Simon Dahan,Hubert Banville,Stéphane d'Ascoli,Jean-Rémi King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究使用预训练的fMRI响应大模型TRIBE v2生成合成数据，增强小样本fMRI数据集以提升脑解码性能。在两个数据集上，合成数据增强使Top-10图像检索准确率提升高达68%，甚至在纯合成数据训练下也能实现零样本脑到图像解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑解码受限于标记神经数据的稀缺性，在低数据场景下表现困难。本文旨在探索能否通过预训练的fMRI响应模型生成的合成数据来增强小样本fMRI数据集，从而提升图像解码性能。

Method: 使用TRIBE v2（在超过1000小时视听语言fMRI响应上预训练的大规模编码模型）生成合成fMRI数据。在Natural Scenes Dataset（7T fMRI）和BOLD5000（3T fMRI）两个数据集上，系统评估不同合成数据量对图像解码器性能的影响，并分析数据来源对最佳合成数据比例的影响。

Result: 合成数据增强使Top-10图像检索准确率提升高达68%（相比仅使用真实数据）。不同数据集需要调整合成数据与真实数据的比例以达到最佳性能。纯合成数据训练的解码器在某些设置下仍能超过随机水平，实现零样本脑到图像解码。

Conclusion: 大规模fMRI响应模型（如TRIBE v2）生成的合成数据可以有效提升小样本脑解码的数据效率，为低数据场景下的脑解码提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Brain decoding is limited by the availability of labeled neural data, and remains challenging in low-data regimes. To address this issue, we investigate whether and when brain decoding can be boosted by augmenting small fMRI datasets with synthetic data generated by a pretrained model of fMRI responses to stimuli. We use TRIBE v2, a large encoding model pretrained on more than 1000 hours of fMRI responses to video, audio and language. For each dataset, we evaluate systematic grids that show how the performance of image decoders varies with the amount of synthetic data used for training. Our results, based on two datasets (the 7T fMRI Natural Scenes Dataset and 3T fMRI BOLD5000), show up to 68% improvement in Top-10 image-retrieval accuracy compared to decoders trained only on real data. Importantly, the proportion of augmented data required to reach a given image decoding performance needs to be adjusted depending on the data source. Surprisingly, image decoders trained exclusively on synthetic fMRI can perform above chance in some settings, suggesting that TRIBE v2 can support zero-shot brain-to-image decoding. Together, these results show how large-scale models of the fMRI responses to sight, sound and language may provide a foundation to improve the data efficiency for image decoding.

</details>


### [19] [Unsupervised Skill Discovery for Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06416)
*Zhisong Qiu,Kangqi Song,Shengwei Tang,Shuofei Qiao,Lei Liang,Huajun Chen,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DataCOPE提出了一种无监督的验证器引导技能发现框架，通过从探索轨迹中提取验证信号，迭代协调数据智能体、无监督验证器和技能管理器，从无标注数据中自动发现可复用的数据分析技能。在报告式分析（提升9.71%）和推理式分析（提升32.30%）两类任务上均显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时技能增强可以轻量级地提升数据分析智能体性能，但有效技能的发现面临两大挑战：可靠的监督信号昂贵，且不同分析格式的成功标准差异大。核心问题是如何仅从无标注探索中发现可复用的数据分析技能。

Method: DataCOPE采用无监督验证器引导的技能发现框架，包含三个迭代协调的组件：1) Data-Analytic Agent生成探索轨迹；2) Unsupervised Verifier从轨迹中提取验证信号，报告式分析采用Adaptive Checklist Verifier（自适应生成任务标准并迭代优化检查表），推理式分析采用Answer Agreement Verifier（按答案一致性分组轨迹并利用自一致性作为辅助信号）；3) Skill Manager进行对比式技能蒸馏。

Result: 在Deep Data Research（报告式分析）和DABStep（推理式分析）两个基准上，DataCOPE一致地提升了留出集性能。跨四种模型设置平均，报告式分析提升9.71%，推理式分析提升32.30%。

Conclusion: DataCOPE验证了从无标注探索中自动发现可复用数据分析技能的可行性，两种验证器实例化分别适配不同分析范式，且无需更新模型参数即可显著提升智能体性能。

Abstract: Inference-time skill augmentation provides a lightweight way to improve data-analytic agents by injecting reusable procedural knowledge without updating model parameters. However, discovering effective skills for data analysis remains challenging, as reliable supervision is expensive and success criteria vary across analytical formats. This raises the key question of how to discover reusable data-analysis skills from unlabeled exploration alone. We propose DataCOPE, an unsupervised verifier-guided skill discovery framework for data-analytic agents. DataCOPE derives verifier signals from the exploration trajectories and uses them to characterize relative quality or aggreement among trajectories. It iteratively coordinates a Data-Analytic Agent for trajectory generation, an Unsupervised Verifier for signal extraction, and a Skill Manager for contrastive skill distillation. For report-style analysis, we instantiate the verifier as an Adaptive Checklist Verifier that derives task-specific criteria, scores reports by verifiable coverage, and iteratively refines the checklist. For reasoning-style analysis, we instantiate it as an Answer Agreement Verifier that groups trajectories by answer agreement and uses self-consistency as an auxiliary signal. We evaluate DataCOPE on report-style analysis from Deep Data Research and reasoning-style analysis from DABStep. Across both settings, DataCOPE consistently improves held-out performance over baselines. Averaged across four model settings, DataCOPE improves the mean score by 9.71% and 32.30% on report-style and reasoning-style tasks respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

### [20] [VideoKR: Towards Knowledge- and Reasoning-Intensive Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05259)
*Lin Fu,Zheyuan Yang,Yang Wang,Tingyu Song,Arman Cohan,Yilun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoKR，首个针对知识密集型视频理解的大规模训练语料库，包含315K个视频推理示例和145K个专家领域视频，通过人机协作的技能导向生成流程构建，并配套专家标注的评估基准VideoKR-Eval。实验表明，基于VideoKR的SFT→GRPO训练显著提升知识密集型视频推理能力，同时保持通用视频推理竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解数据集缺乏对知识推理密集型场景的覆盖，且存在文本捷径问题（模型可凭文本线索作答而非真正理解视频），亟需大规模、高质量的训练语料来增强视频模型的深层推理能力。

Method: 1) 构建包含145K个CC许可专家领域视频、315K个推理示例的大规模语料VideoKR；2) 设计人机协作的技能导向示例生成流程，逐步提升推理难度、多样性和可靠性；3) 构建专家标注的评估基准VideoKR-Eval，避免文本捷径；4) 采用SFT→GRPO标准训练管线进行后训练。

Result: 基于VideoKR后训练的模型在知识密集型视频推理任务上优于现有后训练方法，同时保持通用视频推理任务的竞争力；消融实验验证了VideoKR各组件贡献。

Conclusion: 数据设计是视频推理进步的关键驱动力；VideoKR为知识密集型视频推理提供了有效的数据基础，且为未来工作提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: We introduce VideoKR, the first large-scale training corpus specifically designed to strengthen knowledge- and reasoning-intensive video understanding. It comprises 315K video reasoning examples over 145K newly collected, CC-licensed, expert-domain videos. We develop a human-in-the-loop, skill-oriented example generation pipeline that targets progressively deeper video reasoning capabilities while ensuring the difficulty, diversity, and reliability of both the examples and their CoT rationales. We also curate VideoKR-Eval, a new expert-annotated benchmark where questions require genuine video understanding and knowledge-intensive reasoning rather than textual shortcuts. Our experiments show that, under a standard SFT$\rightarrow$GRPO pipeline, models post-trained on VideoKR outperform prior post-training approaches on knowledge-intensive video reasoning while remaining competitive on general video reasoning, highlighting data design as a key driver of progress in video reasoning. We further conduct comprehensive ablations to isolate the contributions of VideoKR, providing actionable insights for future work.

</details>


### [21] [NIV: Neural Axis Variations for Variable Font Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05261)
*Nadav Benedek,Ariel Shamir,Ohad Fried*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NIV是一种自动将静态字体转换为可变字体的方法，通过预测字形轮廓上的逐点位移，支持多轴连续变化，并在大规模Google Fonts数据集上训练，泛化到未见字体、CJK字符甚至手写输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可变字体目前需要专家级的排版设计和手动指定字形变化数据，过程劳动密集且效率低下。NIV旨在自动化这一过程，降低可变字体的制作门槛。

Method: NIV直接在矢量字形几何上操作，采用新型Property Embedding机制捕获多个设计轴之间的交互，预测每个点的位移，从而在统一框架内实现一致的多轴变化。模型在从可变Google Fonts构建的包含百万级变体元组的数据集上训练。

Result: 模型能够泛化到未见过的码点、未见过的字体风格、高复杂度CJK字形，甚至分布外的手写输入。输出为标准可变字体文件，支持现有渲染引擎的连续插值。

Conclusion: NIV成功实现了从静态字体到可变字体的自动转换，超越了传统手工流程。该工作还展示了利用神经变形合成具有连续参数化变体的结构化几何对象的通用能力，对排版学以外的领域也有启示意义。

Abstract: Variable fonts enable continuous variation of glyph geometry along semantic design axes such as weight, width, slant, and optical size. However, constructing a variable font from a static font remains a labor-intensive process requiring expert typographic design and manual specification of glyph variation data. We introduce NIV (Neural Axis Variations), a method that automatically converts a static font into a fully functional variable font. Given glyph outlines and a set of desired design axes, NIV predicts per-point displacements. The model operates directly on vector glyph geometry and employs a novel Property Embedding mechanism that captures interactions between multiple axes, enabling consistent multi-axis variation within a unified framework. We train NIV on a newly constructed dataset derived from variable Google Fonts, comprising over one million variation tuples. The resulting model generalizes across unseen code points, unseen font styles, high-complexity CJK glyphs, and even out-of-distribution handwriting inputs. The generated outputs are standard variable font files supporting continuous interpolation via existing rendering engines. To facilitate research, we release the dataset, the complete training and inference implementation, and trained models at https://github.com/ndvbd/NIV. Beyond typography, our approach demonstrates how structured geometric objects with continuous parametric variation can be synthesized using neural deformations.

</details>


### [22] [Personal AI Agent for Camera Roll VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05275)
*Thao Nguyen,Krishna Kumar Singh,Donghyun Kim,Yong Jae Lee,Yuheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究个人相机胶卷视觉问答任务，构建了包含50位用户、31,476张图像和2,500个QA对的camroll数据集，并设计了配备分层记忆和工具集的camroll-agent对话智能体，实验表明该代理在长程个性化视觉记忆推理上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI助手缺乏对用户个人相机胶卷这种长时序、高度个性化的视觉内容流进行高效导航和定位的能力，需要一种能理解长期视觉记忆并回答从简单事实到开放性问题的新方法。

Method: 收集并人工标注了模拟真实使用场景的QA对，构建camroll数据集；设计了camroll-agent，该代理配备分层记忆结构（hierarchical memory）和最简工具集，用于在大量个性化视觉记忆上进行高效导航。

Result: camroll-agent在多个基线方法和长上下文理解AI代理系统中表现最优，揭示了AI代理在长程推理上的差距。

Conclusion: 个性化视觉记忆需要不同于标准长上下文文本记忆的方法，特别是在一致性、视觉细节和用户特定上下文方面存在显著差距。

Abstract: We study the personal camera roll visual question answering setting. In this setting, a conversational AI assistant can access a user's personal camera roll and retrieve relevant photos to answer queries, ranging from simple factual questions (e.g., ``Name of the food I tried yesterday?'') to more open-ended ones (e.g., ``Recommend some dishes I have never eaten before''). Given the vast nature of the personal camera roll (i.e., multiple years, hundreds to thousands of photos), a successful AI assistant needs to understand a long-horizon, highly personalized visual content stream in order to navigate and locate the correct and/or relevant information. To support this, we collect and manually annotate questions that mimic real-world usage. The final dataset, camroll, contains 50 users, 31,476 images, and 2,500 QA pairs. We further design camroll-agent, a conversational AI agent equipped with hierarchical memory and a minimal set of tools for efficient navigation over large, personalized visual memory. Experimental results show that camroll-agent outperforms numerous baselines and methods for long-context understanding AI agents system. Together, the camroll dataset and camroll-agent highlight the gap in AI agents' long-context reasoning: personalized visual memory requires different approaches from standard long-context textual memory, especially when consistency, visual details, and user-specific context are present.

</details>


### [23] [Do Models Share Safety Representations? Cross-Model Steering for Safe Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05290)
*Tobia Poppi,Silvia Cappelletti,Sara Sarto,Florian Schiffers,Garin Kessler,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi,Rita Cucchiara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个跨模型安全引导框架，通过源LLM学习可迁移的安全方向向量，仅需轻量对齐即可应用于异构目标生成器（文本到图像/视频），无需目标侧不安全数据即可实现安全控制，且不牺牲生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型的安全控制方法高度依赖模型特定设计，每次新架构出现都需要重新训练或定制干预，缺乏可复用性。

Method: 从源LLM中通过成对安全-不安全提示估计安全方向，通过仅基于良性数据的轻量对齐传输到目标生成器，在推理时应用；还提出了多向量扩展以捕获类别特定安全行为。

Result: 在文本到图像和文本到视频生成任务中，跨模型转移的安全方向实现了与目标模型本地学习（使用不安全数据）相当的ASR降低和CLIP-Score/FID权衡，且无需目标侧不安全数据。

Conclusion: 安全相关行为并非纯模型局部的，而是可以通过跨模型保持的潜在方向进行控制；指向了轻量级、可复用的安全机制新路径。

Abstract: Recent progress in generative modeling has made safety control a central challenge, yet existing approaches remain largely model-specific, requiring retraining or tailored interventions for each new architecture. In this work, we ask whether safety can be represented as a portable latent direction, learned once and reused across heterogeneous generators. We introduce the first framework for cross-model safety steering, in which a safety direction is estimated in a source LLM from paired safe-unsafe prompts, transported to a target generator through a lightweight alignment fitted on benign data alone, and applied at inference time. Crucially, our pipeline never accesses unsafe data on the target side, isolating whether safety can be transferred through shared representation geometry. Beyond a single global direction, we also identify a multi-vector extension that captures category-specific safety behaviors, enabling more selective control. We evaluate our approach in text-to-image and text-to-video generation across diverse source-target model pairs. Across models, transferred safety directions achieve ASR reduction and CLIP-Score/FID trade-offs comparable to directions learned natively on the target model using unsafe data, while requiring no target-side unsafe data. This indicates that safety improvements do not come at the expense of generation quality. Our results point to a modular view of safety: safety-relevant behavior is not purely model-local, but can be controlled through latent directions that persist across models. This suggests a new path toward lightweight, reusable safety mechanisms that do not require target-side unsafe data.

</details>


### [24] [TopoPult-SSL: Gland-Mask-Free Cross-Device Meibomian Gland Segmentation via Self-Distilled Weak Clinical Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05347)
*Nicolò Savioli,Luca Del Tongo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TopoPult-SSL两阶段框架，利用廉价临床信号（眼睑轮廓、Pult等级等）实现跨设备睑板腺分割，无腺体掩码的Stage-1变体在精度上显著优于SAM/MedSAM，蒸馏后的紧凑模型在MGD-1k→CAMG基准上达到Dice 0.716。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新型临床成像设备带来域偏移问题，腺体密集掩码标注昂贵，而廉价临床信号（眼睑轮廓、Pult等级、形态比等）可常规获取，亟需一种无需目标域腺体掩码即可实现跨设备分割的方法。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage 1利用四个弱先验锚点（由目标眼睑掩码和临床元数据驱动），在没有目标腺体掩码参与训练损失的情况下适配源域训练模型；Stage 2在有目标腺体掩码时，通过有监督自蒸馏将互补的Stage-1教师模型提炼为单个紧凑学生模型。

Result: 在MGD-1k→CAMG跨设备基准上，蒸馏模型Dice 0.716±0.006（最佳0.726），超越UA-MT（0.710）和集成教师（0.720）；无腺体掩码的Stage-1变体精确率0.694，显著优于SAM/MedSAM的0.30-0.34（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 证明了利用廉价临床信号替代昂贵腺体掩码实现跨设备域适应的可行性，无掩码变体可直接部署而无需密集腺体轮廓标注。

Abstract: Every new clinical imaging device creates a domain shift where dense gland masks are expensive yet cheap clinical signals -- eyelid outlines, Pult grades, morphometric ratios -- are routinely recorded. We present TopoPult-SSL, a two-stage framework for cross-device meibomian gland segmentation. Stage 1 adapts a source-trained model without target gland masks in the training loss, using four weak-prior anchors driven by target eyelid masks and clinical metadata only. Stage 2, when target gland masks are available, distils complementary Stage-1 teachers into a single compact student via supervised self-distillation. We develop and validate the technique on the public MGD-1k to CAMG research benchmark (1,000 to 100 images, different device), where the distilled model achieves Dice 0.716+/-0.006 (best 0.726), surpassing UA-MT (0.710) and the ensemble teacher (0.720) -- with a single pass. The gland-mask-free Stage-1 variant reaches Precision 0.694 vs. 0.30-0.34 for SAM/MedSAM (p<0.001), enabling deployment without dense gland contouring. Code and reproducibility scripts are released.

</details>


### [25] [LightVesselNet: An Ultra-Lightweight Sub-100K Parameter Network for Retinal Blood Vessel Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05354)
*Shadman Sobhan,Farhana Jalil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightVesselNet是一个仅含75K参数的轻量级视网膜血管分割网络，采用紧凑编解码架构融合通道-空间注意力、多尺度特征聚合和子像素上采样策略，在五个公开数据集上取得与大规模模型相当的性能，适合资源受限的临床部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习视网膜血管分割模型虽精度高但计算资源需求大，难以在边缘设备上部署，需要一种兼顾效率与性能的轻量化方案。

Method: 采用紧凑型编码器-解码器架构，在瓶颈处引入通道-空间注意力机制和多尺度特征聚合模块，解码器使用子像素上采样策略，并设计专用边缘残差连接以保留细小微血管细节。

Result: 在DRIVE、STARE、CHASEDB1、FIVES和HRF五个数据集上，灵敏度分别为0.8189/0.8499/0.8640/0.8634/0.8096，Dice系数分别为0.8070/0.8072/0.8181/0.8649/0.7686。在参数效率和计算效率上均优于现有SOTA模型，跨数据集评估验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: LightVesselNet以极低参数量实现了有竞争力的分割性能，是低资源临床环境和移动筛查工具的理想候选方案。

Abstract: Retinal blood vessel segmentation plays a vital role in the early detection of diabetic retinopathy and glaucoma. While recent deep learning models have achieved great segmentation accuracy, they typically require heavy computational resources, making real-world deployment on edge devices difficult. In this paper, we propose LightVesselNet, an efficient neural network designed for retinal vessel segmentation in a resource-constrained environment. Despite containing only 75K parameters, LightVesselNet performs competitively with much larger models. The network employs a compact encoder decoder architecture enhanced with channel and spatial attention mechanisms, a multi-scale feature aggregation module at the bottleneck, and a subpixel upsampling strategy in the decoder. A dedicated edge residual connection preserves fine vessel detail throughout decoding. Extensive experiments on five publicly available datasets: DRIVE, STARE, CHASEDB1, FIVES, and HRF, yield sensitivity scores of 0.8189, 0.8499, 0.8640, 0.8634, 0.8096, and Dice coefficients of 0.8070, 0.8072, 0.8181, 0.8649, and 0.7686, respectively. LightVesselNet shows improved efficiency (Performance vs Parameter or GFlops) compared to State-of-the-Art models. Cross-dataset evaluation confirms the model's generalisation capability. Overall, LightVesselNet is a strong candidate for deployment in low-resource clinical settings and mobile screening tools.

</details>


### [26] [Recovering Physically Plausible Human-Object Interactions from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05359)
*Dingbang Huang,Etienne Vouga,Qixing Huang,Georgios Pavlakos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RePHO，一种从单目视频重建物理合理的人-物交互（HOI）的方法。该方法从运动学估计出发，通过强化学习（RL）训练策略在物理模拟器中优化交互，并引入自适应采样策略和双自更新机制以处理运动学估计的噪声，最终生成物理一致的HOI序列，在标准基准上显著提升了物理合理性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于运动学的方法虽然能产生视觉上合理的运动，但常常导致物理上不合理的伪影（如相互穿透、物体漂浮），因此需要一种物理引导的重建框架来解决这些问题。

Method: 首先进行运动学估计，然后通过强化学习训练一个策略，该策略经过优化以在物理模拟器中再现交互。为解决运动学估计噪声问题，提出自适应采样策略和双自更新机制，识别信息量最大且最可靠的运动学重建帧，逐步提升重建质量。

Result: 在两个标准HOI基准上进行了验证，在物理合理性指标上相比现有最先进方法取得了明显改进。

Conclusion: RePHO能够有效从单目视频重建物理合理的人-物交互序列，解决了运动学方法常见的物理不合理伪影问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose RePHO, a method to reconstruct physically plausible human-object interactions (HOI) from monocular videos. While existing kinematic-based approaches produce visually plausible motion, they often result in physically implausible artifacts such as interpenetration and object floating. To overcome these issues, we introduce a physics-guided reconstruction framework. We begin with a kinematic estimate and then refine it by training a policy with reinforcement learning (RL). This policy is optimized to reproduce the interaction in a physics simulator. Because kinematic estimates are typically noisy, naive RL training can fail. Therefore, we propose an adaptive sampling strategy with a dual self-updating mechanism that can identify the frames with the most informative and reliable kinematic reconstruction. Our process progressively improves reconstruction quality and yields physically consistent HOI sequences. We demonstrate our approach on two standard HOI benchmarks and achieve clear improvements in physical plausibility metrics over state-of-the-art methods. Project Page: https://dingbang777.github.io/RePHO/

</details>


### [27] [Biomazon: A Multimodal Dataset for 3D Forest Structure and Biomass Modeling in the Amazon Basin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05368)
*Sayan Mandal,Rocco Sedona,Simon Besnard,Mikhail Urbazaev,Morris Riedel,Ehsan Zandi,Gabriele Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Biomazon，一个面向亚马逊盆地的20米分辨率多模态基准数据集，支持联合预测GEDI RH剖面（有序垂直结构）和AGBD，并建立了标准化评估协议与基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML方法大多将冠层顶部高度（如RH95/RH98）或AGBD作为独立标量预测，缺乏对森林垂直结构（有序RH百分位剖面）的学习；社区缺少支持联合预测完整RH剖面与AGBD的多模态基准数据集，以及评估方法是否强制保持RH百分位间物理一致性的标准。

Method: 构建Biomazon多模态基准数据集（20m分辨率），包含GEDI RH和AGBD目标，配准多传感器预测因子（Sentinel-1/2、ALOS-2 PALSAR-2、Copernicus DEM、Dynamic World LULC、AlphaEarth嵌入）；设计共享编码器-解码器带任务特定头的基线框架；进行消融实验（backbone/模型规模、模态贡献、辅助嵌入的独立与融合设置）；开展区域对齐比较。

Result: 在统一训练协议下报告了单目标和联合目标的结果以量化权衡；通过区域对齐比较，将基线性能与现有网格化产品（GEDI L4D RH10-RH98和AGBD）进行对比。

Conclusion: Biomazon建立了首个支持结构一致RH剖面预测和结构-生物量建模的参考基准，为未来相关研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Accurate, spatially explicit characterization of tropical forest structure is essential for carbon accounting and ecosystem monitoring, yet most ML pipelines predict canopy-top height proxies (e.g., RH95/RH98) or AGBD as separate scalar targets, rather than learning the forest vertical structure as an ordered profile. The community lacks a ML-ready multimodal benchmark for predicting the entire GEDI RH profile jointly with AGBD, or for evaluating methods that enforce physically consistent ordering across RH percentiles. We address this with Biomazon, a 20 m multimodal benchmark dataset over the Amazon Basin that pairs GEDI RH and AGBD targets with multi-sensor predictors (Sentinel-1/2, ALOS-2 PALSAR-2, Copernicus DEM, Dynamic World LULC, and AlphaEarth embeddings) under standardized spatial splits and evaluation protocols. Using a shared encoder-decoder with task-specific heads as a baseline framework, we conduct a comprehensive ablation study of (i) backbone/model scale, (ii) modality contributions, and (iii) the use of auxiliary embeddings under standalone and fusion settings, and we report both single-target and joint-target results to quantify tradeoffs under a unified training protocol. Finally, we contextualize baseline performance through regionally aligned comparisons against existing gridded products, including GEDI L4D RH10-RH98 and AGBD, at matching temporal scale. Biomazon, together with the accompanying protocols and baseline results, establishes a reference benchmark for future work on structurally consistent RH-profile prediction and structure-biomass modeling in tropical forests.

</details>


### [28] [Three-Dimensional Retinal Microvasculature Restoration in OCT Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05375)
*Yukun Guo,Min Gao,Tristan T. Hormel,Steven T. Bailey,Thomas S. Hwang,Yali Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习（EfficientNet-B5编码器+SCSE模块解码器）的算法，通过单次OCTA体数据恢复毛细血管解剖结构，利用相邻三帧B-scan预测中间帧，显著提升了图像质量（PSNR: 26.16 vs 22.23, SSIM: 0.91 vs 0.72）和微血管保真度（2D Dice提升≥3.8%, 3D Dice提升≥51.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCTA在定量分析视网膜血流和无灌注区域时易受伪影干扰，现有方法多聚焦于2D/横截面层面的噪声抑制、投影伪影去除或信号增强，忽视了OCTA数据固有的三维血管结构信息。

Method: 构建深度学习网络：采用EfficientNet-B5作为编码器，解码器引入并发空间和通道挤压激励模块（scSE），通过跳跃连接保留空间分辨率；以三个相邻B-frame为输入预测中间帧，输出为恢复后的毛细血管解剖血管结构。

Result: 相比原始单次OCTA体数据，模型PSNR从22.23±0.78提升至26.16±1.26，SSIM从0.72±0.03提升至0.91±0.02（均p<0.001）；二维Dice系数提升≥3.8%，三维Dice系数提升≥51.2%（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 所提出的深度学习方法能有效从单次OCTA体数据中恢复毛细血管解剖结构，显著提升图像质量和微血管保真度，优于传统仅关注2D/横截面处理的方法。

Abstract: Optical coherence tomographic angiography (OCTA) is a powerful technique for imaging retinal microvasculature. However, acquiring reliable quantification of retinal blood flow and areas of retinal nonperfusion is challenging because of imaging artifacts. Existing methods primarily focus on noise suppression, projection artifact removal, or signal enhancement to improve the image quality of OCTA in cross-sectional or two-dimensional (2D) en face projections, while neglecting the intrinsic three-dimensional vascular architecture. In this study, we propose a deep learning-based algorithm for restoring capillary anatomical vasculature from a single OCTA volume. The network consists of an EfficientNet-B5 encoder and a decoder incorporating concurrent spatial and channel squeeze-and-excitation modules, connected via skip connections to preserve spatial resolution. Three adjacent B-frames are used as input to predict the restored middle B-frame. We evaluated the performance of the model using the peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) and structural similarity index measure (SSIM) against ground truth generated from averaging multiple scans. The results show that the proposed model significantly (both p < 0.001) improved image quality compared with the original single OCTA volume, with a PSNR of 26.16 +/- 1.26 vs. 22.23 +/- 0.78 and an SSIM of 0.91 +/- 0.02 vs. 0.72 +/- 0.03. The proposed model also significantly (p < 0.001) improved microvascular fidelity, measured by the Dice coefficient overlap between the model output and ground truth, in both 2D and 3D by at least 3.8% and 51.2%, respectively, across several different vascular slabs.

</details>


### [29] [Deep Learning-assisted AMD Staging based on OCT and OCT Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05379)
*Yukun Guo,Tristan T. Hormel,An-Lun Wu,Liqin Gao,Min Gao,Steven T. Bailey,Yali Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究开发并评估了基于OCT/OCTA数据的深度学习模型，用于自动分级年龄相关性黄斑变性（AMD）严重程度。共分析271名参与者（351只眼）的2030个OCT/OCTA体积数据。比较了三种输入模态：生物标志物映射图、二维en face投影和三维体积。所有模型表现良好（QWK≥0.83），其中基于生物标志物的模型综合性能最佳（QWK=0.85），尤其在早期AMD检测方面表现突出（F1=0.59）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现AMD严重程度的自动化、客观分级，减少人工评估的主观性和耗时性，特别是提升早期AMD的检测能力。

Method: 基于EfficientNet架构，分别以三种模态作为输入：(1) 由分割病理特征（视网膜积液、玻璃膜疣、地理萎缩、黄斑新生血管）生成的生物标志物映射图；(2) 二维en face OCT和OCTA投影；(3) 三维OCT/OCTA体积。采用五折交叉验证、数据增强和标准化输入进行训练。

Result: 生物标志物模型QWK=0.85±0.03，早期AMD F1=0.59±0.14，整体最优；3D模型QWK=0.83±0.04，与2D模型（QWK=0.83±0.09）性能相当；2D模型精度最高（0.79±0.06），对无AMD眼识别最准确。

Conclusion: 基于OCT/OCTA数据的深度学习模型能够准确、自动地对AMD严重程度进行分级，其中生物标志物驱动的方法综合性能最均衡，对早期AMD检测具有特别价值。

Abstract: To develop and evaluate deep learning models for automated grading of age-related macular degeneration (AMD) severity using optical coherence tomography (OCT) and OCT angiography (OCTA) data. Two hundred seventy-one participants aged >= 50 years with varying AMD severities. Central macular 6 x 6 mm OCT/OCTA volumes were acquired using a swept-source OCTA system (SOLIX; Visionix/Optovue Inc., CA). AMD severity was graded into four stages (No AMD, Early AMD, Intermediate AMD, and Advanced AMD) according to the AREDS simplified severity scale. Three deep learning models were developed using different input modalities: (1) biomarker maps derived from segmented pathological features, including retinal fluid, drusen, geographic atrophy (GA), and macular neovascularization (MNV); (2) two-dimensional (2D) en face OCT and OCTA projections; and (3) three-dimensional (3D) OCT/OCTA volumes. EfficientNet-based architectures were trained using normalized inputs, data augmentation, and five-fold cross-validation. A total of 2,030 OCT/OCTA volumes from 351 eyes of 271 participants were analyzed. All models demonstrated strong AMD staging performance with substantial agreement with the reference standard (QWK >= 0.83). The biomarker-based model achieved the highest overall performance (QWK = 0.85 +/- 0.03, mean +/- standard deviation) and the best detection of early AMD (F1-score = 0.59 +/- 0.14). The 3D model achieved performance comparable to the 2D OCT/OCTA model (QWK = 0.83 +/- 0.04 vs. 0.83 +/- 0.09), while the 2D OCT/OCTA model showed the highest precision (0.79 +/- 0.06) and most accurately identified eyes without AMD. Deep learning models using OCT/OCTA data can accurately and automatically grade AMD severity. Among the evaluated approaches, the biomarker-based model provided the most balanced performance and showed particular value for early AMD detection.

</details>


### [30] [UniPixie: Unified and Probabilistic 3D Physics Learning via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05399)
*Qilin Huang,Quynh Anh Huynh,Long Le,Chen Wang,Chuhao Chen,Ryan Lucas,Eric Eaton,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UNIPIXIE将物理属性预测从点估计重构为可控连续分布的学习任务，通过单一视觉输入沿软硬谱生成多样化的物理材料场，并在多个物理求解器上降低杨氏模量预测误差超50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈网络只能从视觉外观预测单一物理属性集合（点估计），无法捕捉现实世界固有的物理歧义性，需要将物理预测重构为学习可控的连续材料属性分布。

Method: 提出UNIPIXIE框架，在PIXIEMULTIVERSE数据集上学习物体从最软到最硬谱系的直接映射，通过单一直观参数实现可控的连续材料场生成；并设计统一架构输出适用于多种物理求解器（MPM、LBS、弹簧-质点系统）的仿真就绪参数。

Result: 不仅生成了丰富多样的合理动力学结果，还将杨氏模量预测误差相较于最强确定性基线降低了超过50%。

Conclusion: UNIPIXIE弥合了静态点估计与物理现实连续性之间的鸿沟，支持可控且物理合理的材料属性生成，并解决了跨求解器的参数可移植性问题。

Abstract: Existing feed-forward networks excel at predicting a single set of physical properties from visual appearance, but this point-estimate paradigm fundamentally fails to capture the real world's inherent physical ambiguity. We address this by reframing physics prediction as a task of learning a controllable, continuous distribution of material properties. We introduce UNIPIXIE, a framework trained to predict a continuous and parameterized path of physically plausible material properties from a single visual input. By learning a direct mapping along an object's softest-to-stiffest spectrum on our PIXIEMULTIVERSE dataset, UNIPIXIE allows for controllable generation of diverse, physically valid material fields via a single intuitive parameter. Crucially, UNIPIXIE introduces a novel unified architecture to produce simulation-ready parameters for diverse physics solvers, including continuum-based Material Point Method (MPM), reduced-order deformation based on Linear Blend Skinning (LBS), and anchor-based Spring-Mass systems, addressing a key portability issue in prior work. Experiments show our approach not only generates a rich variety of plausible dynamics but also reduces Young's Modulus prediction error by over 50% against the strongest deterministic baseline, bridging the gap between static point estimates and the continuous nature of physical reality. Project page: https://unipixie.github.io/

</details>


### [31] [Would you still call this Dax? Novel Visual References in VLMs and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05409)
*Ada Defne Tür,Gaurav Kamath,Joyce Chai,Siva Reddy,Benno Krojer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NVRD数据集（19,176张图像，90个视觉概念），研究视觉语言模型在上下文学习中将新视觉概念映射到语言的能力，尤其是当新概念与预训练知识冲突时。实验发现模型难以学习矛盾的新概念，且相比人类会过度泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类学习者经常接触新的视觉概念，但视觉语言模型如何将新视觉参考映射到语言（尤其是在与预训练知识矛盾时）尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建Novel Visual References Dataset (NVRD)，包含19,176张图像、90个视觉概念，每个概念有最多20个逐渐扰动的版本。评估3个开源和2个闭源模型，同时收集2,400个人类判断进行直接比较。

Result: (i) 当新概念与先验知识矛盾时，模型难以在上下文中学习新概念；(ii) 模型和人类对视觉扰动的敏感性相关，但模型显著过度泛化，将学习到的标签扩展到人类拒绝的刺激上。

Conclusion: NVRD可作为研究人类和机器视觉概念学习的语料库和基准。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs), like human learners, are frequently exposed to new visual concepts, but how they map novel visual references to language after exposure remains largely underexplored, particularly when those references contradict prior knowledge from pre-training. To study this, we present the Novel Visual References Dataset (NVRD): 19,176 images spanning 90 visual concepts across different levels of visual novelty, each with up to 20 increasingly perturbed versions of the original object to probe generalization. Unlike prior work on visual augmentations of familiar concepts, NVRD comprises entirely novel, open-ended stimuli constructed from scratch, mirroring how humans encounter genuinely new concepts. We evaluate 3 open- and 2 closed-source models alongside 2,400 human judgments for direct human-model comparison, and find that (i) models struggle to acquire novel concepts in-context when they contradict prior knowledge, and (ii) while models and humans show correlated sensitivity to visual perturbations, models significantly overgeneralize, extending learned labels to stimuli that humans reject. We contribute NVRD as a corpus and benchmark for research on visual concept learning in both humans and machines.

</details>


### [32] [Disentangled Fine-Grained Prototype Learning for Incomplete Image-Tabular Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05455)
*Feixiang Zhou,Jianyang Xie,Zhuangzhi Gao,Qinkai Yu,Fu Wang,Yuheng Fan,Jing Li,Zheheng Jiang,Yitian Zhao,Yanda Meng,He Zhao,Gregory Y. H. Lip,Yalin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DFPL框架，通过细粒度原型学习解决图像-表格多模态学习中缺失模态问题。该框架包含共享-特异原型建模（SSPM）、原型引导细粒度对齐（PFA）和类别感知多尺度聚合（CMA）三个模块，在三个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过全局token平均特征的解耦和对齐学习模态不变表示，只能捕捉粗粒度的跨模态一致性，忽略了细粒度的语义和分布错配，导致在缺失模态场景下难以充分利用模态间的互补信息。

Method: 提出DFPL框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) SSPM：提取紧凑且多样化的共享原型和模态特异原型，并在原型级别进行解耦以抑制冗余的模态内相关性；(2) PFA：在统一的原型空间中联合执行原型级别的分布匹配和原型到类别的语义对齐，保留细粒度的分布和语义一致性；(3) CMA：从全局和原型级别自适应聚合共享语义和模态特异特征，实现鲁棒预测。

Result: 在三个不同图像-表格基准数据集上的大量实验表明，在各种缺失模态设置下，所提方法均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: DFPL通过细粒度原型学习有效解决了高度异构的图像-表格模态在缺失场景下的跨模态对齐问题，代码将公开。

Abstract: The missing-modality problem poses a significant challenge in image-tabular multimodal learning across a wide range of multimedia applications, including product understanding, recommendation systems, and medical diagnosis. This challenge is particularly pronounced when the two modalities are highly heterogeneous, as images and tabular attributes differ substantially in their semantic granularity and data distributions. Existing methods learn modality-invariant representations through disentanglement and alignment over global token-averaged features, capturing only coarse cross-modal consistency and overlooking fine-grained semantic and distributional misalignment, which hampers the exploitation of complementary cues under missing modalities. To address this, we propose DFPL, a novel framework for fine-grained prototype learning. Specifically, Shared-Specific Prototype Modeling (SSPM) extracts compact and diverse shared and modality-specific prototypes, and further performs prototype-level disentanglement to suppress redundant intra-modality correlations. Additionally, we propose a Prototype-guided Fine-grained Alignment (PFA) module that jointly enforces prototype-level distribution matching and prototype-to-class semantic alignment within a unified prototype space, thereby preserving both fine-grained distributional and semantic consistency across modalities. We further introduce a Class-aware Multi-scale Aggregation (CMA) module to adaptively aggregate shared semantics and modality-specific characteristics from global and prototype levels for robust predictions. Extensive experiments on three diverse image-tabular benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of our method compared to the previous approaches under various missing-modality settings. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [33] [Horse Eye Blink Detection and Classification for Equine Affective State Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05458)
*João Alves,Signe Møller-Skuldbøl,Pia Haubro Andersen,Rikke Gade*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发并评估了三种自动化马匹眨眼分类方法（YOLOv12、光流阈值法、VideoMAE），在公开数据集上取得了0.898的宏F1分数（眨眼分类）和0.926的F1分数（二值眨眼检测），展示了细粒度面部动作单元检测在马匹福利监测中的潜力与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马匹面部动作单元（AU）的自动检测是评估马匹疼痛和情感状态的重要途径，其中半眨眼和全眨眼运动是疼痛和压力的已知指标，但由于其作为微表情的细微特性，肉眼难以捕捉，需要可靠的自动化检测方法。

Method: 开发了三种自动化眨眼分类方法：基于帧的YOLOv12检测器、光流幅度阈值法以及微调后的VideoMAE模型，并在公开数据集上进行测试。

Result: 眨眼分类任务宏F1分数达到0.898，二值眨眼检测任务F1分数达到0.926。

Conclusion: 结果表明自动化细粒度AU检测在马匹福利监测中具有潜力，但同时也面临固有的挑战。

Abstract: Automated detection of equine facial action units (AUs) is a promising yet under-explored avenue for pain and affective state assessment in horses. Half and full-blink movements are recognised indicators of pain and stress, but as micro-expressions, their subtle, fine-grained nature makes them easily missed by the naked eye and only discernible through frame-by-frame video inspection, making reliable automated detection from video a particularly demanding task. We develop and evaluate three methods for automated blink classification from horse videos: a frame-based YOLOv12 detector, an optical flow magnitude thresholding approach, and a fine-tuned VideoMAE model, tested on a publicly available dataset. We achieve a macro-F1 score of 0.898 when doing blink classification and 0.926 on binary blink detection. Our results highlight both the potential and the inherent challenges of fine-grained AU detection for equine welfare monitoring.

</details>


### [34] [ORACLE-CT: Anatomy-Aware Support Pooling for CT Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05460)
*Lavsen Dahal,Yubraj Bhandari,Geoffrey Rubin,Joseph Y. Lo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ORACLE-CT框架，通过多器官分割定义标签特定解剖支持区域，将注意力池化限制在相关区域，解决腹部CT疾病分类中全局聚合与局部证据不匹配的问题。在三个编码器家族上验证，支持掩码池化优于全局平均池化，提升内外部数据集性能，且保持预测与解剖证据的可审计关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹部CT疾病分类中，诊断证据通常局限于特定器官或解剖区域，而现有的研究级分类器使用与解剖无关的池化或注意力机制进行全局特征聚合，导致局部疾病证据与全局证据聚合之间的不匹配，影响了分类性能与可解释性。

Method: 提出ORACLE-CT框架，一种编码器无关的解剖感知聚合框架。利用多器官分割定义标签特定的解剖支持区域（支持单器官、多器官联合、比较性、局部和全局五种策略），将注意力池化限制在相关区域，实现解剖感知的特征聚合。

Result: 在MERLIN内部评估中，支持掩码池化相比全局平均池化，DINOv3的macro-AUROC/AUPRC从0.838/0.638提升至0.858/0.676，I3D-ResNet-121从0.829/0.617提升至0.848/0.659。在10标签外部评估中，DINOv3在Duke-Abdomen上从0.802/0.628提升至0.835/0.683，在AMOS上从0.742/0.313提升至0.762/0.350。对于Pillar-0编码器，主要提升来自学习到的注意力，解剖掩码带来额外较小收益。

Conclusion: ORACLE-CT通过解剖感知的聚合框架有效提升了腹部CT疾病分类的判别性能和外部鲁棒性，同时保持了预测与解剖证据之间的可审计关联，验证了解剖先验信息在医学影像分类中的价值。

Abstract: Abdominal CT disease classification is challenging because each scan is a large 3D volume with many possible findings, while diagnostic evidence is often confined to specific organs or anatomical compartments. Most study-level classifiers aggregate encoder features using anatomy-agnostic pooling or attention, creating a mismatch between localized disease evidence and global evidence aggregation. We propose ORACLE--CT, an encoder-agnostic anatomy-aware aggregation framework that uses multi-organ segmentation to define label-specific anatomical supports and restrict attention pooling to relevant regions. The framework supports single-organ, multi-organ union, comparative, localized, and global support strategies.
  We evaluate ORACLE--CT with three encoder families: DINOv3, I3D--ResNet-121, and the radiology-native Pillar--0 encoder. Models are trained end-to-end on MERLIN and evaluated internally and under frozen external transfer to Duke--Abdomen and AMOS. Compared with global average pooling, support-masked pooling improved MERLIN macro-AUROC/AUPRC from 0.838/0.638 to 0.858/0.676 for DINOv3 and from 0.829/0.617 to 0.848/0.659 for I3D--ResNet-121. On harmonized 10-label external evaluation, DINOv3 improved on Duke--Abdomen from 0.802/0.628 to 0.835/0.683 and on AMOS from 0.742/0.313 to 0.762/0.350, with similar gains for I3D--ResNet-121. For Pillar--0, most gains came from learned attention, with smaller additional benefit from anatomical masking. ORACLE--CT improves discrimination and external robustness while preserving an auditable link between predictions and anatomical evidence.

</details>


### [35] [Formal Concept Lattices are Good Semantic Scaffolds for Concept-Based Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05471)
*Deepika SN Vemuri,Sayanta Adhikari,Ankit Saha,Krishn Vishwas Kher,Vineeth N Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用形式概念分析中的概念格作为语义支架，指导神经网络在适当深度学习不同抽象层级的概念，使模型形成分层、语义化的表示，提升可解释性和干预效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念模型将所有概念视为同一层次的扁平集合，忽略了人类语义理解中概念从一般到特殊的分层组织特性，且深度网络虽能学习视觉特征层级，却很少与显式语义层级对齐。

Method: 借助形式概念分析中的概念格，根据概念的抽象程度确定其在网络中的学习位置，引导模型在深度方向上逐步学习从一般到具体的语义表征。

Result: 在真实数据集上，模型生成的嵌入更具可解释性，支持更有效的干预操作，且学习到的概念表示具有意义明确的分层结构。

Conclusion: 形式概念格能够提供原则性的语义支架，帮助神经网络在深度上对齐语义层级，提升模型的可解释性与语义理解能力。

Abstract: Learning semantics is essential for deep learning models to be interpretable and better aligned with human reasoning. Concept-based models approach this by representing classes through meaningful semantic abstractions, but typically treat all concepts as a flat, unstructured set learned at a single neural network layer. This overlooks a fundamental property of human semantic understanding: concepts being organized hierarchically, from general to specific. While deep networks do learn a hierarchy of visual features, this structure is rarely aligned with explicit semantic hierarchies. Drawing on Formal Concept Analysis, we demonstrate that formal concept lattices provide principled semantic scaffolds to guide neural network learning. These lattices naturally identify where in the network concepts should be learned based on their level of generality. This allows the model to develop staged, semantically grounded representations throughout its depth. Empirical results on real-world datasets show that our models produce more interpretable embeddings, support more effective interventions, and learn concept representations that are both meaningful and hierarchically structured.

</details>


### [36] [Can We Predict The Human Preference For Text-to-Image Content Prior To Generation And Is It Even Useful To Do So?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05478)
*Joong Ho Kim,Keith G. Mills*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散模型中随机噪声对生成质量的影响，提出了一种在生成前预测人类偏好指标（HPM）分数的方法，并利用该预测来提升生成图像质量，且硬件开销可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中的随机噪声显著影响生成质量，尤其是在小型本地部署模型中更为明显。现有研究多关注模型架构或训练策略，而忽略了初始噪声的可预测性及其对生成质量的直接影响。作者希望探索能否在投入计算资源前预测HPM分数，并利用该预测来改进生成结果。

Method: 首先研究能否在生成前预测标量HPM分数，然后探讨如何利用这种预测来提升生成图像质量，并对比不同HPM指标在该任务中的适用性，最后评估硬件开销。

Result: 实验表明，在生成前预测HPM分数不仅是可行的，而且硬件开销极小（可忽略不计）。

Conclusion: 通过预测扩散模型生成前的HPM分数，可以有效提升生成图像质量，且该方法在硬件效率上具有实用性。
