The perception module analyzes partners' multimodal behaviors from video, while the social reasoning module infers hidden mental states through Theory of Mind and selects responses via an ensemble mechanism. The expression module then generates emotion-controllable dual-agent videos synthesizing both speaker speech and expression alongside listener reactive behaviors, capturing bidirectional dynamics absent in prior work. We construct a hierarchical Persona-Scenario dataset with psychologically grounded personas and private social goals to support evaluation under information asymmetry. Experiments on this dataset demonstrate competitive or superior performance on both dialogue quality and video generation metrics. Notably, our method surpasses even the full-information Script mode on key dialogue quality dimensions, suggesting that explicit mental state inference under uncertainty can elicit more thoughtful dialogue than unrestricted information access.

</details>


### [74] [Self-Learning Expression Deformations for Data-Efficient Gaussian Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05912)
*Jiahao Yang,Xiaohang Yang,Qing Wang,Yilan Dong,Gregory Slabaugh,Shanxin Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE框架，通过自监督学习Gaussian形变，仅需极少量输入数据即可实现高保真、可驱动的3D面部化身重建与动画，数据需求减少数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D高斯化身管线需要大量多视角和序列化表情数据，限制了可扩展性和可访问性。解决动态面部表情建模中高斯表示的无结构特性带来的挑战，降低数据需求。

Method: 联合优化2D高斯surfels和符号距离场(SDF)以实现紧凑、表面对齐的高斯分布；采用自监督表情学习阶段，用几何和外观一致性约束替代长训练序列。支持多视角(单帧)、单目(仅需头部旋转)和单图(无需预训练或先验)三种重建模式。

Result: 在重建和动画质量上达到与最先进方法相当的水平，同时数据需求减少数个数量级。

Conclusion: 自监督高斯形变学习是实现数据高效、易于访问的化身创建的有效途径。

Abstract: Modeling dynamic facial expressions using 3D Gaussian representations remains challenging due to their unstructured nature. Conventional Gaussian avatar pipelines require extensive multiview and sequential expression data, limiting scalability and accessibility. In this work, we introduce Self-Adaptive Gaussian Expression (SAGE), a framework for self-learning expression-induced Gaussian deformations that enables high-fidelity, animatable avatars from minimal input data. Our method jointly optimizes 2D Gaussian surfels and a Signed Distance Field (SDF) to enforce compact, surface-aligned Gaussian distributions, while a self-supervised expression learning phase replaces long training sequences with geometric and appearance consistency constraints. This design allows flexible deployment across multiple reconstruction regimes: in the multiview setting, only a single frame (timestep) is required instead of thousands; in the monocular setting, only head rotations are needed without expression sequences; and in the one-shot setting, no pretraining or priors are necessary. Experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves reconstruction and animation quality comparable to state-of-the-art methods, while reducing data requirements by several orders of magnitude. Our results highlight the potential of self-supervised Gaussian deformation learning as a step toward accessible, data-efficient avatar creation.

</details>


### [75] [CamFlow+: Hybrid Motion Bases for 2D Camera Motion Estimation with Stabilization Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05915)
*Haipeng Li,Zhen Liu,Zhanglei Yang,Hai Jiang,Tianhao Zhou,Zhengzhe Liu,Ping Tan,Bing Zeng,Shuaicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CamFlow+提出了一种混合基础框架，将单应性物理基础、随机采样基础与深度-平移基础结合，直接在密集光流空间表示2D相机运动，并通过深度感知平滑项处理视差，在相机运动估计和数字视频稳定化任务上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于单应性的方法在处理平面场景或纯旋转时效果良好，但面对相机平移、深度变化和局部视差时表现不佳；局部单应性和网格模型虽然提升了灵活性，但仍依赖于分片平面假设。作者旨在提出一种不受平面约束、同时保持相机运动规律性的密集流空间表示方法。

Method: CamFlow+采用混合基础框架，包含三类基础：1）从单应性导出的物理基础；2）从单应性流中采样的随机基础；3）从深度和相机内参导出的深度-平移基础。同时引入深度感知平滑项，在连续深度区域正则化平移引起的视差，同时在深度边界附近保留运动变化。

Result: 在GHOF-Cam基准（通过在光流基准中掩蔽动态物体和不适定遮挡区域来隔离相机诱导运动）上，CamFlow+在稀疏和密集相机运动估计上均有提升。在数字视频稳定化任务中，CamFlow+同时改善了全局和局部稳定性，在盲测用户研究中取得了最佳top-1偏好率。

Conclusion: CamFlow+通过混合基础框架有效突破了单应性方法的平面约束限制，能够更准确地估计包含平移和深度变化的相机运动，并在下游任务（视频稳定化）中展现了实际应用价值。

Abstract: Estimating 2D camera motion is fundamental to computer vision and computational photography. Existing homography-based methods work well for planar scenes or pure rotation, but struggle with camera translation, depth variation, and local parallax; local homography and mesh-based models improve flexibility but still rely on piecewise planar assumptions. We introduce CamFlow+, a hybrid-basis framework that represents 2D camera motion directly in dense-flow space. CamFlow+ combines homography-derived physical bases, stochastic bases sampled from homography flows, and depth-translational bases derived from depth and camera intrinsics, relaxing the single-plane constraint while preserving camera-motion regularity. A depth-aware smoothness term further regularizes translation-induced parallax in continuous-depth regions while preserving motion changes near depth boundaries. We evaluate CamFlow+ on GHOF-Cam, a camera-motion benchmark that masks out dynamic objects and ill-posed occlusion regions in an optical-flow benchmark to isolate camera-induced motion. Experiments show that CamFlow+ improves sparse and dense camera-motion estimation. In digital video stabilization, CamFlow+ also improves global and local stability, achieving the best top-1 preference rate in a blind user study. Code and datasets will be available on the project page: https://lhaippp.github.io/CamFlow+.

</details>


### [76] [Unveiling the Unknown: Open Vocabulary Object Detection with Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05916)
*Yi Chen,Yinghao Lu,Zhehao Li,Chenchen Yan,Jiafei Wu,Chong Wang,Jiangbo Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Scene-guided Relational Modeling检测框架，通过场景图捕获对象间的结构化语义和空间关系，结合关系注意力模块和场景文本对齐分支，提升开放词汇目标检测中新颖类别的检测性能，在COCO和LVIS数据集上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识蒸馏式开放词汇目标检测方法忽略了对象间结构化的、图像特有的关系（如交互和空间布局），限制了新颖类别的检测效果。

Method: 1）利用场景图捕获候选区域与其上下文对象之间的结构化语义和空间关系；2）显式建模相邻区域间的交互，并设计关系注意力模块隐式增强从场景图提取的关键关系线索；3）提出基于场景的文本对齐分支，从字幕中蒸馏类别知识以引导关系对齐，实现视觉关系与语义信息的融合。

Result: 在COCO和LVIS数据集上，新颖类别的平均精度（AP）超越其他开放词汇目标检测方法。

Conclusion: 显式建模场景级关系能够有效提升开放词汇目标检测对新类别的泛化能力，关系注意力与场景文本对齐的联合设计是关键。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection seeks to identify novel object categories that were not part of the training data. Many knowledge distillation-based approaches have shown promising performance by transferring knowledge from pre-trained vision-language models to object detection. However, these methods often overlook structured, image-specific relationships between objects, such as interactions and spatial arrangements. This oversight can significantly restrict the effectiveness of detecting novel categories. To address this issue, we propose a Scene-guided Relational Modeling detection framework. This framework utilizes scene graphs to capture structured semantic and spatial relationships between candidate regions and their contextual objects. It explicitly models interactions among neighboring regions and incorporates a Relation Attention Module to implicitly amplify the key relational cues extracted from the scene graph. Furthermore, we present a scene-based textual alignment branch that distills category knowledge from captions to guide relational alignment. This approach facilitates a seamless integration of visual relations with semantic information for enhanced detection performance. Comprehensive experiments show that our model achieves superior performance compared to other OVOD methods, improving the AP for novel categories on COCO and LVIS datasets.

</details>


### [77] [MemoryCard: Topic-Aware Multi-Modal Clue Compression for Long-Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05917)
*Qing Yang,Pengcheng Huang,Xinze Li,Zhenghao Liu,Yukun Yan,Yu Gu,Ge Yu,Gang Li,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemoryCard是一种基于视频记忆的增强框架，通过将长视频分割为语义连贯的"记忆卡片"（Memory Cards），每个卡片对应一个事件或主题，生成事件级视频摘要并选择代表性视觉时刻，从而提升长视频问答性能，在可比视觉token预算下准确率相对提升最高达21.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于帧的方法将孤立、碎片化的帧作为基本证据单元，难以有效捕获连贯的事件级语义，限制了VLMs在长视频问答中处理稀疏、短暂且时间上分散的相关证据的能力。

Method: MemoryCard首先通过对视频和对齐文本进行自阅读（self-reading）过程，将视频分割为语义连贯的单元；对每个单元生成事件级视频摘要并选择代表性视觉时刻，渲染为统一的Memory Cards，用于检索和问答。

Result: 在可比视觉token预算下，MemoryCard持续提升长视频问答性能，准确率相对提升最高达21.8%。

Conclusion: MemoryCard通过将长视频组织为自包含的事件级记忆卡片，有效解决了现有帧级方法在捕获事件级语义方面的局限性，显著提升了长视频问答效果。

Abstract: Long-video question answering remains challenging for Vision-Language Models (VLMs), as answer-relevant evidence is often sparse, transient, and temporally dispersed across lengthy video contexts. Existing frame-centric approaches improve efficiency through uniform sampling, query-aware frame selection, visual-token compression, and adaptive resolution strategies. However, they still rely on isolated and fragmented frames as the fundamental evidence units, limiting VLMs' ability to effectively capture coherent event-level semantics. To address this limitation, we propose MemoryCard, a video-memory-based augmentation framework that organizes long videos into self-contained Memory Cards. Specifically, MemoryCard first performs a self-reading process over videos and aligned utterances to segment the video into semantically coherent units, each corresponding to a distinct topic or event. For each unit, it generates an event-level video gist and selects representative visual moments, which are then rendered into unified Memory Cards for retrieval and question answering. Experimental results demonstrate that MemoryCard consistently improves long-video QA performance under comparable visual-token budgets, achieving up to a 21.8% relative improvement in accuracy. All code is available at https://github.com/NEUIR/MemoryCard.

</details>


### [78] [Faithful, Enriched, and Precise: Benchmarking Natural-Science Illustration Generation by T2I models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05949)
*Yifan Chang,Jiaxin Ai,Jianwen Sun,Yuandong Pu,Siqi Luo,Liangliang Zhao,Yuchen Ren,Minghao Liu,Yunfei Yu,Yu Qiao,Kaipeng Zhang,Yihao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了FEPBench基准，用于系统评估文本到图像模型在科学插图生成中的精细粒度表现，涵盖指令忠实度、推理丰富性和语义精度三个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常从整体层面评估科学插图生成质量，忽略了细粒度元素；同时科学推理能力和输出简洁性未被充分量化。

Method: 从多学科高质量科学插图中构建FEPBench，借助多模态大语言模型和人类专家提供细粒度原子集标注，沿指令忠实度、推理丰富性和语义精度三个维度评估T2I模型，并分解视觉、文本、关系和布局元素的性能。

Result: 即使SOTA闭源模型（如GPT Image 2和Nano Banana Pro）仍存在文本渲染瓶颈、推理丰富性有限，且在生成丰富性与精度之间难以平衡。

Conclusion: 这些发现为改进和部署科学插图生成的T2I模型提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Scientific illustrations are essential tools for communicating research findings, especially in natural science, where they visualize complex concepts and processes. As Text-to-Image (T2I) models become increasingly capable, researchers have started to use them for scientific illustration generation. However, existing benchmarks often assess outputs at a holistic level, overlooking fine-grained elements, while scientific reasoning ability and output conciseness remain under-quantified. We introduce FEPBench, a benchmark built from carefully selected high-quality scientific illustrations across multiple disciplines and layout types. With the assistance of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and human experts, we provide fine-grained atom set annotations and systematically evaluate T2I models along three dimensions: instruction faithfulness, reasoning enrichment, and semantic precision. Our evaluation further decomposes model performance across visual, textual, relation, and layout elements. Results show that even state-of-the-art (SOTA) closed-source models, such as GPT Image 2 and Nano Banana Pro, still suffer from text-rendering bottlenecks, limited reasoning enrichment, and difficulty balancing generation richness with precision. These findings provide practical guidance for improving and deploying T2I models in scientific illustration generation. Benchmark data, atom set annotations, and evaluation code will be released by us.

</details>


### [79] [T-FunS3D: Task-Driven Hierarchical Open-Vocabulary 3D Functionality Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05975)
*Jingkun Feng,Reza Sabzevari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: T-FunS3D是一种任务驱动的分层开放词汇3D功能分割方法，通过构建场景图并利用视觉语言模型，在保持与现有方法相当性能的同时，实现了更快的运行速度和更少的内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D分割方法主要聚焦于物体级别识别，而场景级部分分割方法需要穷举分割整个场景，计算资源消耗大且耗时。如何在分割粒度、准确性和速度之间取得平衡是一个挑战。

Method: 输入室内场景的3D点云和带位姿的RGB-D图像，构建开放词汇场景图提取实例及其视觉嵌入；根据任务描述识别场景图中最相关的实例，并利用视觉语言模型定位其功能组件。

Result: 在SceneFun3D数据集上，T-FunS3D在开放词汇3D功能分割任务上达到了与最先进方法相当的性能，同时运行速度更快、内存使用更少。

Conclusion: T-FunS3D通过任务驱动的分层方法有效地平衡了分割粒度、精度和速度，为机器人应用提供了可行的感知方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D functionality segmentation enables robots to localize functional object components in 3D scenes. It is a challenging task that requires spatial understanding and task interpretation. Current open-vocabulary 3D segmentation methods primarily focus on object-level recognition, while scene-wide part segmentation methods attempt to segment the entire scene exhaustively, making them highly resource-intensive and time consuming. Balancing segmentation performance in terms of granularity, accuracy, and speed remains a challenge. As one step towards alleviating this, we introduce T-FunS3D, a task-driven hierarchical open-vocabulary 3D functionality segmentation method that provides actionable perception for robotic applications. Our method takes as input the 3D point cloud and posed RGB-D images of an indoor scene. We construct an open-vocabulary scene graph by extracting instances and their visual embeddings in the environment. Given a task description, T-FunS3D identifies the most relevant instances in the scene graph and locates their functional components leveraging a vision-language model. Experiments on the SceneFun3D dataset demonstrate that T-FunS3D is comparable to state-of-the-art in open-vocabulary 3D functionality segmentation, while achieving faster runtime and reduced memory usage.

</details>


### [80] [Video-Rate Streaming Stylization on a Vision-Aware MLLM-Conditioned Edit Diffusion: Asymmetric Batched Inference on a Distilled UNet + MLLM Text Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05981)
*Yoshiyuki Ootani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对扩散U-Net蒸馏后文本编码器成为瓶颈的问题，提出了一套流式推理管线：通过非对称CUDA流水线、编译友好的ControlNet-LLLite融合图以及周期性条件刷新调度，在消费级GPU上实现了实时视频编辑扩散速度（RTX 3090 Ti上512×512分辨率达到27.4-29.6 fps）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散U-Net经过激进蒸馏（4步或1步）后，每帧去噪瓶颈被消除，但文本编码器（尤其是视觉感知编辑中的MLLM）反而成为计算关键路径，限制了实时视频编辑管线的吞吐量。

Method: 1) 非对称侧流/主流CUDA流水线，结合批量化文本编码器摊销（可选静态提示缓存）；2) 编译友好的ControlNet-LLLite重公式化，将U-Net+适配器栈折叠为单一融合计算图；3) 周期性条件刷新调度，通过钩子子集摊销每帧条件计算成本。

Result: 在RTX 3090 Ti上512×512分辨率以批大小B=8达到27.4 fps、B=16达到29.6 fps，p50延迟约0.5秒和1.0秒；RTX 4090上54.9 fps，RTX 5090上74.1 fps。时序适配器在油画画风上泛化至19个DAVIS-2017序列和15个非DAVIS片段。

Conclusion: 提出的流式管线有效解决了蒸馏后文本编码器瓶颈问题，在消费级GPU上实现了视频速率流式吞吐（而非交互式低延迟），并提供了与StreamDiffusion的系统级对比基线。

Abstract: Aggressive distillation of the diffusion U-Net inverts the per-frame bottleneck of real-time text-to-image pipelines: once the denoiser is a 4-step or 1-step distilled student, the text encoder becomes the critical path. This inversion is most acute in vision-aware edit diffusion, where the encoder is a multimodal large language model (MLLM). We study the case of a 0.39B distilled edit U-Net paired with a 2.13B MLLM text encoder (Qwen3-VL) and present a streaming pipeline targeted at this regime built around three engineering mechanisms: asymmetric side-stream / main-stream CUDA pipelining with batched text-encoder amortisation (and optional static-prompt caching), a compile-friendly ControlNet-LLLite reformulation that folds the entire U-Net + adapter stack into a single fused graph, and a periodic conditioning-refresh schedule with a hook subset that amortises the per-frame conditioning cost. On a single consumer RTX 3090 Ti at 512x512 the pipeline sustains 27.4 fps over a 480-frame run at batch size B=8 and 29.6 fps at B=16, with end-to-end p50 latency of approximately 0.5 and 1.0 seconds respectively; the same operating point measures 54.9 fps on RTX 4090 and 74.1 fps on RTX 5090. We report video-rate streaming throughput rather than interactive low latency, and locate our numbers against same-stack StreamDiffusion re-runs as systems context, not as a benchmark superiority claim. For the trained oil-painting style, the released temporal adapter generalises within in-clip noise to 19 unused DAVIS-2017 sequences and 15 non-DAVIS clips from seven sources; prompt-level generalisation to unseen style families is bounded and reported separately.

</details>


### [81] [Multimodal Sexism Identification and Characterization using Large Language Models and Gradient Boosting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05997)
*Kyriakos Chaviaras,Maria Lymperaiou,Athanasios Voulodimos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了AILS-NTUA系统，采用特征工程与后期融合策略，结合梯度提升回归模型和层次化后处理，分别处理模因（Task 2）和短视频（Task 3）中的多模态性别歧视识别与特征化问题。实验表明，LLM-derived语义线索能有效提升模因识别性能，而短视频任务中特征选择在开发集上有效但泛化性不足，未筛选特征在测试集上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决模因和短视频中性别歧视内容的多模态识别与特征化问题，探索不同模态特征（视觉、文本、人口统计、生物特征、LLM语义、声学、传感器元数据）对性别歧视检测的有效性，并比较特征筛选策略在跨模态场景下的泛化能力。

Method: 采用特征工程驱动的后期融合流水线，核心为梯度提升回归模型与层次化后处理。对模因：融合视觉、文本、人口统计、生物特征及LLM-derived语义指标（捕捉刻板印象、物化、反讽、厌女等高级线索）。对短视频：探究特征选择、基于帧的视觉表示、OCR文本特征、声学描述符和传感器元数据的影响，比较了筛选特征与未筛选特征的泛化性能。

Result: 开发集上LLM-derived语义线索有效提升模因性别歧视识别性能；短视频任务中，开发集上紧凑特征选择表现更优，但官方测试结果相反——未筛选特征在未见数据上泛化更好。

Conclusion: 针对静态模因，定向语义特征工程是有效的；针对短视频噪声环境，需要更强的时间建模能力以提升鲁棒性和泛化性。

Abstract: We present the AILS-NTUA submission to the EXIST 2026 Lab at CLEF, addressing multimodal sexism identification and characterization in memes (Task 2) and short-form videos (Task 3). Our system follows a feature-engineered late-fusion pipeline built around gradient-boosted regression models and hierarchical post-processing. For memes, we combine visual, textual, demographic, biometric, and LLM-derived semantic indicators designed to capture high-level cues such as stereotyping, objectification, irony, and misogyny. For videos, we investigate the effect of feature selection, frame-based visual representations, OCR-based textual features, acoustic descriptors, and sensor-derived metadata. Development results show that focused LLM-derived semantic cues improve meme sexism identification, while video performance is highly sensitive to feature dimensionality and cross-modal noise. For videos, development results favor compact feature selection, but official test results show that this conclusion does not fully transfer to unseen data, where the unfiltered representation generalizes better. Overall, our findings highlight the usefulness of targeted semantic feature engineering for static memes and the need for more robust temporal modeling in noisy short-form video settings.

</details>


### [82] [Deep Learning-based 3D Oral Cavity Reconstruction Using 2D Intraoral Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05998)
*Jihun Cho,Soo-Yeon Jeong,Eun-Jeong Bae,Sun-Young Ihm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅需10张2D口内图像即可重建3D口腔模型的纯软件方法，采用MobileNetV2+Multi-head Attention架构，在Dental3DS数据集上达到77.49%的精度，但存在预测顶点分布不均的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统印模取模法存在患者不适、材料变形、存储运输困难等问题，口内扫描仪虽然效果好但设备成本高昂。为解决这些硬件相关局限，本文提出一种无需专用硬件的纯软件3D重建方案。

Method: 使用MobileNetV2作为图像编码器提取特征，结合Multi-head Attention进行多视角特征融合，仅需10张不同角度的2D口内图像，在Dental3DS数据集（950个上颌样本）上训练，实现3D口腔模型重建。

Result: 最近邻匹配（距离阈值0.035）准确率达到77.49%，但预测顶点倾向于集中在真实数据的高密度区域，导致重建模型上点分布不均匀。

Conclusion: 该方法有效降低了成本和患者不适感，无需物理扫描设备即可实现自动化3D重建，但点分布不均匀问题是后续需要改进的方向。

Abstract: Oral 3D modelling is one of the most essential stages in dentistry, and many different approaches, such as impression taking and intraoral scanning, are commonly used for this phase, each with notable limitations. Impression taking, which involves placing alginate or silicone material in a tray and inserting it into the patient's oral cavity to form a negative mold, suffers from significant patient discomfort, material deformation errors, and difficulties in storage and transportation. Intraoral scanners, which directly scan oral structures in real time using structured light or laser technology, produce state-of-the-art results but are associated with substantially high equipment costs. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a software-based approach that reconstructs a 3D oral model using only ten 2D intraoral images captured from different angles, requiring no dedicated hardware devices. The proposed method reduces cost, eliminates the need for physical scanning equipment, minimises patient discomfort, and enables automated 3D reconstruction. The model is trained on the publicly available Dental3DS dataset, comprising 950 upper jaw samples, and employs MobileNetV2 as the image encoder combined with Multi-head Attention for multi-view feature fusion. The proposed model achieves an accuracy of 77.49%, measured by nearest-neighbor matching with a distance threshold of 0.035. However, predicted vertices tend to concentrate in high-density regions of the ground truth, resulting in uneven point distribution across the reconstructed model.

</details>


### [83] [ATT-CR: Adaptive Triangular Transformer for Cloud Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05999)
*Yang Wu,Ye Deng,Pengna Li,Wenli Huang,Kangyi Wu,Xiaomeng Xin,Jinjun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自适应三角Transformer用于遥感图像去云（ATT-CR），通过三角注意力（TAN）实现O(N)计算复杂度，并利用特征选择门控模块（FSGM）区分云层与干净像素，在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的去云方法存在两个问题：1）自注意力的高计算复杂度限制了可扩展性；2）注意力计算中将云像素与干净像素同等对待，导致后续层引入干扰信息，性能次优。

Method: 提出ATT-CR模型，包含两个核心组件：1）三角注意力（TAN），利用下三角和上三角矩阵近似Softmax注意力，计算复杂度降至O(N)；2）特征选择门控模块（FSGM），与TAN协同，自适应区分云层特征与干净特征，减少无效信息传入后续层。

Result: 在多个去云基准数据集上的大量实验表明，ATT-CR性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ATT-CR通过降低计算复杂度和减少云像素干扰，有效提升了遥感图像去云的性能与可扩展性。

Abstract: Cloud removal aims to accurately reconstruct the ground objects obscured by clouds in remote sensing images. Existing Transformer-based methods utilizing self-attention have shown impressive results by effectively modeling long-range dependencies in cloudy images. However, they suffer from the following issues: 1) the high computational complexity of self-attention limits scalability; 2) treating both cloudy and clean pixels as valid within the attention computation brings disturbances in subsequent layers, leading to suboptimal performance. To address these challenges, we propose the Adaptive Triangular Transformer for Cloud Removal (ATT-CR), a model that effectively reduces computational costs and mitigates interference from cloudy pixels. Specifically, it consists of two core components: Triangular Attention (TAN) and Feature Selected Gating Module (FSGM). TAN employs lower and upper triangular matrices to approximate Softmax attention with O(N) computational complexity, significantly reducing the computational costs. The FSGM, on the other hand, integrates with TAN to adaptively distinguish between cloudy and clean features, which minimizes the introduction of invalid information into subsequent layers. Extensive experiments on cloud removal benchmarks demonstrate that ATT-CR delivers superior performance compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [84] [Global-Local Monte Carlo Tree Search in Vision-Language Models for Text-to-3D Indoor Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06002)
*Mengshi Qi,Wei Deng,Xianlin Zhang,Huadong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于树搜索的文本到3D室内场景生成方法，通过PRM引导的MCTS算法在全局树和局部树中分层搜索，解决了LVLM顺序决策导致的错误传播问题，并在新收集的大规模数据集3DTindo-bench上取得了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM方法采用链式顺序决策机制，无法修正早期决策，导致错误传播。文本到3D室内场景生成需要满足空间和布局常识约束，因此需要一种能够回溯和探索多种可能的规划方法。

Method: 将任务建模为树搜索问题，包含全局树和局部树。全局树中迭代放置物体并探索多种尝试，提出层次化场景表示（房间级→区域级→地面物体级→支撑物体级）和PRM引导的MCTS方法（PRM剪枝不必要分支，MCTS平衡探索与利用）。局部树将每个物体的放置分解为更细的子步骤。利用预训练扩散模型为场景中所有物体预测纹理。

Result: 收集了包含65种场景类型和3,250条指令的大规模多样化数据集3DTindo-bench。实验表明该方法生成的3D场景比现有最先进方法更真实。

Conclusion: 树搜索策略结合层次化表示和PRM-MCTS方法能有效解决文本到3D场景生成中的错误传播问题，生成更符合空间和布局常识的室内场景。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models have achieved significant reasoning performance in various tasks.However, there are few studies on text-to-3D indoor scene generation with LVLMs. The main challenge is that prevailing LVLM-based methods employ chain-of-thought sequential decision mechanisms that cannot revise earlier decisions, causing error propagation.In this paper, we consider the task as a planning problem constrained by spatial and layout commonsense.To solve this problem, we model it as a tree search problem with global and local trees, which differs from existing sequential decision-making approaches.In the global tree, we place each object iteratively and explore multiple attempts like humans furnishing a room, where the problem space is represented as a tree.To effectively search the tree, we propose a hierarchical scene representation and a PRM-guided MCTS method.The hierarchical representation abstracts a scene into room level, region level, floor object level, and supported object level.The PRM-guided MCTS method uses the PRM to prune unnecessary branches and the MCTS algorithm to balance exploration and exploitation to get an optimal solution with fewer attempts.In the local tree, it further decomposes the placement of each object into finer sub-steps, including the specific placement parameters.To make the whole appearance of the scene consistent, we leverage pre-trained diffusion image generative models to predict textures for all the objects in the scene.As existing benchmarks for text-to-3D indoor scene generation remain limited in scale and diversity, we collect a new large-scale diverse dataset that contains 65 scene types and 3,250 instructions with diverse sizes, layouts, and styles, named 3DTindo-bench, to better assess the capability of the state-of-the-art models. Our experiments show that our method generates more realistic 3D scenes than state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [85] [ReSAGE-PAR: Representational Similarity Assessment for Generative Expansion in Pedestrian Attribute Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06020)
*Pablo Ayuso-Albizu,Pablo Carballeira,Juan C. SanMiguel,Paula Moral*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReSAGE-PAR管道，利用扩散模型和属性引导提示生成行人图像以扩充数据集，通过LoRA图像到图像适配、视觉-语言对齐评分和贝叶斯伪标签分类器解决域差距和属性验证问题，在PAR任务上取得高达8.7%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人属性识别面临数据多样性和稀缺性限制，现有扩散模型生成的图像存在预训练高质量数据与低分辨率监控图像之间的域差距，以及生成幻觉导致的属性不可靠问题。

Method: 提出ReSAGE-PAR管道：(1) 基于LoRA的图像到图像方法将预训练扩散模型适配到原生PAR分辨率；(2) 提取生成图像与条件提示之间的视觉-语言对齐分数，采用包含标签一致和不一致互补的综合提示策略；(3) 构建贝叶斯分类器将连续分数转换为可靠的二值伪标签。

Result: 在标准骨干网络上实现高达8.7%的性能提升，推动最先进框架达到新的性能水平，验证了其作为架构无关的PAR可扩展增强方案的有效性。

Conclusion: ReSAGE-PAR有效弥合了域差距并实现了属性验证，是一种架构无关的可扩展PAR数据集扩充方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: To address the limited diversity and data scarcity in Pedestrian Attribute Recognition (PAR), we explore image synthesis using diffusion models guided by attribute-based prompts. While this enables the controlled generation of pedestrian images, it faces two critical challenges: (i) the domain gap between high-quality pre-training data and low-resolution, non-standard surveillance crops, and (ii) the need for reliable attribute verification to prevent generative hallucinations. In this paper, we introduce a robust generate-score-autolabel pipeline called ReSAGE-PAR (REpresentational Similarity Assessment for Generative Expansion in PAR) that bridges this domain gap and enables scalable, high-fidelity dataset expansion. First, we adapt pre-trained diffusion models to native PAR resolutions using a tailored LoRA-based Image-to-Image approach. Second, we extract vision-language alignment scores between the generated images and their conditioning prompts, utilizing a comprehensive prompting strategy that includes label-consistent and inconsistent complements. Finally, we formulate a Bayesian classifier that converts these continuous scores into reliable binary pseudo-labels. Extensive evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness of ReSAGE-PAR in preserving spatial priors and verifying attributes. When integrated into PAR training, ReSAGE-PAR consistently yields significant improvements-achieving gains of up to 8.7% on standard backbones and pushing state-of-the-art frameworks to new performance levels. This proves its value as an architecture-agnostic solution for scalable PAR enhancement. The complete codebase for ReSAGE-PAR is publicly available at http://www-vpu.eps.uam.es/publications/ReSAGE-PAR.

</details>


### [86] [HyperVis: Continuous Latent Visual Relational Graphs on the Lorentz Hyperboloid for Compositional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06100)
*Moshiur Farazi,Sameera Ramasinghe,Mahbub Ahmed Turza,Shafin Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HyperVis通过将稠密视觉关系张量投影到洛伦兹双曲面上，绕过场景图生成器的语义瓶颈，作为训练时正则化器和推理时关系编码器，显著提升了VLMs的 compositional reasoning 能力，并发现强曲率空间对连续视觉特征的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLMs在理解物体间关系的组合推理上表现不佳；直接注入离散场景图三元组反而因语义瓶颈导致性能下降（GQA从60.38%降至58.86%），亟需一种无需离散化、保留连续视觉特征的关系建模方法。

Method: (1) 从N个与类别无关的区域提议计算稠密O(N²)视觉关系张量（空间偏置交叉注意力）；(2) 投影到洛伦兹双曲面；(3) 通过IoA驱动的包含锥（entailment cones）和外角排斥（exterior-angle repulsion）执行空间物理层次约束；(4) 作为训练时正则化器（双曲关系损失塑造LoRA表示）和推理时关系编码器（双曲前缀token提升判别式组合评分）。

Result: 训练时正则化：GQA 61.03% vs. LoRA基线的57.21%（恢复并超越基线）；推理时编码：SugarCrepe 79.94%（+6.25pp）；曲率稳定在κ=4.0（此前双曲VLM通常趋近于零）；欧几里得消融：GQA 60.81%（正则化效果相近），但SugarCrepe增益仅+4.58pp（双曲特有增益），包含损失在欧氏训练中高出约6倍。

Conclusion: 连续视觉特征需要强曲率双曲空间提供的指数级体积；双曲空间对组合推理的增益是特有的，欧几里得空间无法替代；HyperVis在不依赖离散场景图标注的情况下，同时提升了生成式VQA和判别式组合评分。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) struggle with compositional reasoning that requires understanding inter-object relationships. A natural remedy is to inject explicit scene graph triplets $\langle s, p, o \rangle$ from an off-the-shelf scene graph generator (SGG), but we show this backfires: discrete text labels collide with the continuous visual modality, degrading GQA accuracy from 60.38\% to 58.86\%. We propose \textbf{HyperVis}, which bypasses the SGG semantic bottleneck entirely. From $N$ class-agnostic region proposals, we compute a dense $O(N^2)$ visual relation tensor via spatially-biased cross-attention, project it onto a Lorentz hyperboloid, and enforce hierarchy through spatial physics, namely IoA-driven entailment cones and exterior-angle repulsion. We discover that HyperVis contributes in two complementary ways: (1) as a \emph{training-time regularizer}, the hyperbolic relational losses shape LoRA representations that improve generative VQA (GQA 61.03\% vs.\ 57.21\% for LoRA fine-tuning without relational losses, recovering and surpassing the baseline); and (2) as an \emph{inference-time relational encoder}, hyperbolic prefix tokens boost discriminative compositional scoring (SugarCrepe 79.94\%, $+$6.25pp over baseline). The learned curvature stabilises at $κ{=}4.0$, an order of magnitude above prior hyperbolic VLMs where $κ$ typically collapses toward zero, indicating that continuous visual features genuinely require the exponential volume of strongly curved space. A controlled Euclidean ablation confirms this decomposition: the relational pipeline regularises LoRA comparably in flat space (GQA 60.81\%), but the compositionality gain is specifically hyperbolic (SugarCrepe $+$4.58pp over Euclidean), with entailment loss ${\sim}6{\times}$ higher in Euclidean training. Codes are available at TBA.

</details>


### [87] [MS-DKC: A Dataset Knowledge Card Framework for Designing and Adapting Medical Image Segmentation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06103)
*Tariq M. Khan,Syed Saud Naqvi,Thantrira Porntaveetus,Hamid Alinejad-Rokny,Shahzaib Iqbal,Imran Razzak,Mohammad AU Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出医学分割数据集知识卡（MS-DKC）框架，用于显式记录数据集特征（前景占比、形态、边界模糊性、拓扑敏感性、标注质量、采集变异、操作点），并将其映射到失败模式、设计先验和风险对齐标准，从而使分割模型设计比传统的架构优先比较更具可追溯性。在DRIVE、ISIC2018和ACDC三个数据集上的实验表明，不同数据集需要不同的设计先验、操作点和评估证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像分割研究过度聚焦于追求更强的网络架构，忽视了更根本的问题：数据集对模型提出了什么要求？这些要求包括前景占比、形态、边界模糊性、拓扑敏感性、标注质量、采集变异和操作点七个关键因素，需要显式化建模。

Method: MS-DKC框架通过图像/采集、形态、监督、上下文依赖和部署风险五个描述符记录数据集证据，并将其映射到对应的失败模式、设计先验和风险对齐标准，使分割设计过程可追溯。

Result: DRIVE（稀疏细支血管）：DKC-TNet-v2达到Dice 0.8044、IoU 0.6730，SA-UNetv2-DKC-AmbRef达Dice 0.8141、IoU 0.6865。ISIC2018（紧凑但外观多变病灶）：MS-DKC-AttNextTopo-VCSF-NoAug达Dice 0.8872、IoU 0.8214、Boundary F1 0.4878。ACDC（多类心脏）：推荐四类softmax分割、类别平衡Dice/CE监督和类别级表面评估。

Conclusion: 不同数据集需要不同的设计先验、操作点和评估证据才能判断模型是否合适，支持数据集条件化的设计范式，而非统一的架构优先比较。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation is often framed as a search for stronger architectures, but this can obscure a more fundamental question: what does the dataset require from the model? In medical imaging, this requirement is shaped by foreground occupancy, morphology, boundary ambiguity, topology sensitivity, annotation quality, acquisition variation, and operating point.
  This paper introduces the Medical Segmentation Dataset Knowledge Card (MS-DKC), a framework for making these factors explicit. MS-DKC records dataset evidence through image/acquisition, morphology, supervision, context-dependence, and deployment-risk descriptors. These descriptors are mapped to failure modes, design priors, and risk-aligned criteria, making segmentation design more traceable than architecture-first comparison.
  We evaluate MS-DKC on DRIVE, ISIC2018, and ACDC, representing distinct regimes. DRIVE contains sparse, thin, branching vessels, favoring detail-preserving models, sensitivity-aware optimization, threshold analysis, and topology-aware metrics. DKC-TNet-v2 achieved Dice 0.8044 and IoU 0.6730 with 35103 parameters, while SA-UNetv2-DKC-AmbRef reached Dice 0.8141, IoU 0.6865, sensitivity 0.8265, specificity 0.9804, and AUC 0.9853. ISIC2018 involves compact but appearance-variable lesions; validation-constrained score-function selection on Att-Next-Topo/ATTNext produced MS-DKC-AttNextTopo-VCSF-NoAug with Dice 0.8872, IoU 0.8214, precision 0.9173, Boundary F1 0.4878, and ASSD 4.13, while plausible additions failed to improve the risk-aligned profile. ACDC provides a multi-class cardiac case, where MS-DKC recommends four-class softmax segmentation, class-balanced Dice/CE supervision, and class-wise surface evaluation. Overall, the results support dataset-conditioned design: different datasets require different priors, operating points, and evidence before a model can be judged appropriate.

</details>


### [88] [Where, What, Why, and Importance: Structured Defect Grounding for Text-to-Image Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06113)
*Huaisong Zhang,Hao Yu,Yuxuan Zhang,Jiahe Wang,Xinrui Chen,Haoxiang Cao,Feng Lu,Wendong Zhang,Changqian Yu,Chun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构化缺陷基础（SDG），将T2I诊断建模为结构化集合预测，每个缺陷表示为（位置、类型、原因、重要性）元组，并构建SDG-30K数据集和SDG-Eval评估协议，最终形成从诊断到对齐的完整框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型存在局部、细微且结构复杂的失败模式，而当前的密集反馈方法（如热力图）本质上是像素场回归，难以定位可变基数的缺陷并将语义原因绑定到单个故障上。

Method: 1) 提出SDG，将缺陷诊断建模为结构化集合预测，每个缺陷表示为（位置、类型、原因、重要性）四元组；2) 构建SDG-30K数据集（30K图像，盒级标注，覆盖4种T2I生成器）和SDG-Eval评估协议；3) 设计诊断到对齐框架：VLM作为SDG检测器，BoxFlow-GRPO将预测的缺陷集合转换为基于盒的、重要性加权的空间奖励用于扩散模型对齐。

Result: SDG检测器在结构化缺陷基线上优于领先的专有VLM；SDG引导的奖励一致性提升T2I对齐，并支持局部图像细化。

Conclusion: SDG为诊断、评估和增强现代生成模型提供了统一的实例级接口。

Abstract: Despite generating increasingly photorealistic images, text-to-image (T2I) models still exhibit localized, subtle, and structurally complex failures. Diagnosing these failures requires instance-level feedback that answers where a defect occurs, what type it is, why it is defective, and its importance to overall image quality. While recent dense-feedback methods move beyond scalar supervision, their heatmap-centric representations still formulate diagnosis as pixel-field regression, making it difficult to localize variable-cardinality defects and bind semantic reasons to individual failures. To address this representation bottleneck, we propose Structured Defect Grounding (SDG), which casts T2I diagnosis as structured set prediction by modeling each defect as a (location, type, reason, importance) tuple. To make this formulation trainable and measurable, we introduce SDG-30K, a 30K-image dataset with box-grounded annotations across four modern T2I generators, together with a dedicated evaluation protocol, SDG-Eval. Building on this structured representation, we further present a diagnosis-to-alignment framework in which a Vision-Language Model (VLM) serves as the SDG detector, and BoxFlow-GRPO converts predicted defect sets into box-derived, importance-weighted spatial rewards for diffusion model alignment. Extensive experiments show that our SDG detector outperforms leading proprietary VLMs on structured defect grounding, while SDG-guided rewards consistently improve T2I alignment and support localized image refinement. These results establish SDG as a unified, instance-level interface for diagnosing, evaluating, and enhancing modern generative models.

</details>


### [89] [Diff-CA: Separating Common and Salient Factors with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06120)
*Michaël Soumm,Alexandre Fournier Montgieux,Yunlong He,Pietro Gori,Alasdair Newson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的条件化框架，在不牺牲生成质量的前提下实现对比分解，通过无提示的图像条件扩散模型和弱监督学习将条件分解为共同因子和显著因子，并证明了加性对比分解的可识别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比分析方法基于VAE或GAN等生成模型，存在重建质量和图像质量有限的缺陷，阻碍了有效的潜在因子分离，限制了在高保真图像生成和编辑中的应用。

Method: 首先训练一个无提示的、图像条件化的扩散模型，然后通过弱监督学习将条件化分解为共同因子和显著因子，利用加性对比分解实现因子分离。

Result: 在温和条件下证明了加性对比分解的可识别性，能够通过交换或插值仅显著因子实现目标操作。

Conclusion: 所提框架在保持扩散模型高生成质量的同时实现了有效的对比分解，为高保真图像生成和编辑提供了新途径。

Abstract: Contrastive Analysis aims to separate factors that are common between two data distributions from those that are salient to only one of them. Existing contrastive methods are based on generative models (e.g., VAEs or GANs) that often suffer from limited reconstruction and image quality, which hampers effective latent factor separation and limits their applicability to high-fidelity image generation and edition. We propose a novel conditioning framework for diffusion models that enables contrastive decomposition without compromising generation quality. We first train a prompt-free, image-conditioned diffusion model, and then learn to decompose the conditioning into a common and a salient factor, using weak supervision. We prove that the additive contrastive factorization, commonly assumed in prior work, is identifiable under mild conditions. This factorization enables targeted operations by swapping or interpolating only the salient factor.

</details>


### [90] [Computation-Aware Event-to-Frame Reconstruction via Selective Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06142)
*Jingqian Wu,Yunbo Jia,Edmund Y. Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向事件到帧重建的高效框架，采用循环编码器-解码器架构、选择性上下文融合和轻量级混合注意力机制，在保持重建质量的同时兼顾计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件到帧重建方法在重建质量与计算效率之间存在权衡，缺乏高效的因果时序建模与计算感知设计。

Method: 采用循环编码器-解码器增量聚合事件信息；提出选择性上下文融合策略整合事件特征与先验强度线索；引入轻量级混合注意力机制增强特征选择性。

Result: 在标准基准上实现了有竞争力的重建性能，在精度与模型复杂度之间取得了良好平衡。

Conclusion: 该框架有效缓解了重建质量与效率之间的权衡，验证了因果时序建模和计算感知设计的有效性。

Abstract: Event-to-frame (E2F) reconstruction bridges asynchronous event streams with frame-based vision pipelines, but existing methods often face a trade-off between reconstruction quality and computational efficiency. In this work, we propose an efficient E2F framework that emphasizes causal temporal modeling and computation-aware design. The architecture adopts a recurrent encoder-decoder to incrementally aggregate event information with compact hidden states. To improve robustness under fast motion and illumination variations, a selective context fusion strategy is introduced to integrate event-driven features with prior intensity cues. Within this fusion process, a lightweight hybrid attention mechanism enhances feature selectivity without relying on heavy attention operations. Experimental results on standard benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves competitive reconstruction performance while maintaining a favorable balance between accuracy and model complexity.

</details>


### [91] [Adaptive Tokenisation Via Temporal Redundancy Masking And Latent Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06158)
*Kevin Dave,Sai Aditya Patkuri,Chhaya Kumar Das,Gouranga Bala,R. Venkatesh Babu,Rajeshkumar SA*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需额外参数的视频自适应Token分配方法：利用冻结连续视频分词器隐空间中的时间残差（逐位置L1差异）直接识别并丢弃冗余Token，再通过轻量级Latent Inpainting Transformer（LIT）重建丢弃位置。该方法仅需单次编码器前向和一次LIT前向，在TokenBench和DAVIS上实现与先进方法相当的保真度，同时相比ElasticTok-CV获得31倍推理加速，相比InfoTok获得约2倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应视频Token化方法（连续型：迭代二值搜索或神经回归器；离散型：需全速率解码器估计信息量）计算开销大。作者认为这些开销并非必要，冻结连续分词器的隐空间本身就编码了可利用的时间冗余。

Method: 1) 对冻结的连续视频分词器隐空间中逐位置计算时间维L1差异；2) 用固定阈值判断冗余位置并丢弃（参数无关，压缩率由内容自然决定）；3) 提出Latent Inpainting Transformer（LIT），一种轻量级因子化时空注意力架构用于重建丢弃位置。整体推理仅需一次编码器前向+一次LIT前向，无需辅助路由网络。

Result: 在TokenBench和DAVIS基准上，该方法实现了有意义的、内容驱动的Token分配，重建保真度与现有方法竞争力相当；推理速度相比ElasticTok-CV提升31倍，相比InfoTok提升约2倍。

Conclusion: 证明了通过隐空间时间差直接判断冗余是可行的且高效，参数无关的自适应分配机制让压缩率自然地由输入内容决定，为视频Token化提供了一种极简且实用的替代方案。

Abstract: Adaptive video tokenisation seeks to dynamically allocate token budgets based on the underlying visual complexity of a sequence. Current continuous-regime approaches achieve this via iterative binarised searches or trained neural regressors, while discrete methods often require a full-rate decoder pass to estimate information content. We demonstrate that such computational overheads are not strictly necessary. We show that the latent space of a frozen continuous video tokeniser inherently encodes temporal redundancy that can be exploited directly: spatial positions whose latent representations change minimally between consecutive frames carry near-zero additional information.
  We introduce a parameter-free adaptive token allocation mechanism that applies a fixed threshold to per-position temporal-L1 differences, identifying and dropping redundant latent positions. Consequently, the compression rate emerges naturally from the input content rather than being enforced top-down: static scenes get compressed aggressively, while highly dynamic sequences retain more tokens. To reconstruct the dropped positions, we propose the Latent Inpainting Transformer (LIT), a lightweight factorised spatial-temporal attention architecture. The resulting inference pipeline is highly efficient, requiring only a single encoder pass and one LIT forward pass, eliminating the need for auxiliary routing networks. Evaluations across TokenBench and DAVIS, which are the standard benchmarks used by recent tokenisers~\cite{infotok, agarwal2025cosmos}, indicate that our framework yields meaningful, content-driven token allocation while maintaining competitive reconstruction fidelity, and delivers a $31\times$ inference-time speedup over the continuous adaptive baseline (ElasticTok-CV) and an $\approx2\times$ speedup over the discrete information-theoretic baseline (InfoTok)

</details>


### [92] [RQUL-UIE: Revitalizing Quality-Unstable Labels for Underwater Image Enhancement via In-Dataset Self-Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06176)
*Haochen Hu,Yanrui Bin,Chih-yung Wen,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩散模型的域内自监督水下图像增强策略，通过预训练扩散模型无训练地评估标签质量并量化为噪声级别，实现逐级去噪监督，避免低质量标签损害模型性能，同时引入傅里叶细化网络恢复高频分量，在多个基准上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的水下图像增强方法依赖配对数据集，但标签质量不稳定，成为模型性能的瓶颈。

Method: 1) 利用预训练扩散模型的语义感知嵌入无训练地评估标签质量；2) 将质量分数量化为噪声级别索引，指导多步去噪过程实现逐级监督；3) 引入基于傅里叶的细化网络显式重建高频分量。

Result: 在多个评估基准上一致优于当前最先进方法，恢复质量显著提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的域内自监督扩散策略能有效利用训练标签的质量分布，防止低质量标签退化模型，同时最大化其效用，结合傅里叶细化网络实现更优的水下图像增强效果。

Abstract: Underwater Image Enhancement (UIE) is essential for mitigating degradations caused by water medium. Although learning-based methods have advanced significantly, most rely on paired datasets with unstable label quality, which bottlenecks model performance. This paper proposes a diffusion-based, in-dataset self-supervised learning strategy designed to exploit the quality distribution of training labels. Specifically, we evaluate label quality via semantic perception embeddings from a pre-trained diffusion model in a training-free manner. These quality scores are subsequently quantized into noise-level indices, guiding a multi-step denoising process for level-wise supervision. This mechanism prevents low-quality labels from degrading the model while maximizing their utility during training. Furthermore, a Fourier-based refinement network is incorporated to explicitly reconstruct high-frequency components. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms SOTA approaches in restoration quality. The code and pre-trained model will be available once accepted in link.

</details>


### [93] [Adversarial Attacks Already Tell the Answer: Directional Bias-Guided Test-time Defense for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06186)
*Liangsheng Liu,Si Chen,Jiamin Wu,Weiwei Feng,Zhixin Cheng,Xiaotian Yin,Wenfei Yang,Tianzhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现对抗性图像在CLIP特征空间中沿主导方向偏移，并提出基于防御方向估计的双流重建测试时防御方法DBD，在15个数据集上实现SOTA鲁棒性，甚至出现对抗精度超越干净精度的反直觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM（如CLIP）虽具强零样本泛化能力，但对对抗扰动高度脆弱，现有测试时防御方法无需大规模重训练但效果有限。本文旨在揭示对抗扰动的结构化偏移特性并设计高效测试时防御。

Method: 提出Directional Bias-guided Defense (DBD)框架：1) 估计防御方向（Defense Direction），即对抗图像在特征空间中沿主导方向偏移的反向；2) 采用DB-score-based双流重建策略恢复鲁棒特征表示。

Result: 在15个数据集上，DBD达到SOTA对抗鲁棒性，同时保持干净精度；发现对抗精度可超越干净精度，表明对抗扰动编码了真实决策边界的先验方向信息。

Conclusion: 对抗扰动在CLIP特征空间中具有结构化方向先验，利用该先验可设计高效测试时防御；对抗精度超越干净精度这一反直觉结果揭示了对抗扰动的信息价值。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs), such as CLIP, have shown strong zero-shot generalization but remain highly vulnerable to adversarial perturbations, posing serious risks in real-world applications. Test-time defenses for VLMs have recently emerged as a promising and efficient approach to defend against adversarial attacks without requiring costly large-scale retraining. In this work, we uncover a surprising phenomenon: under diverse input transformations, adversarial images in CLIP's feature space consistently shift along a dominant direction, in contrast to the dispersed patterns of clean images. We hypothesize that this dominant shift, termed the Defense Direction, opposes the adversarial shift, pointing features back toward their correct class centers. Building on this insight, we propose Directional Bias-guided Defense (DBD), a test-time framework that estimates the Defense Direction and employs a DB-score-based two-stream reconstruction strategy to recover robust representations. Experiments on 15 datasets demonstrate that DBD not only achieves SOTA adversarial robustness while preserving clean accuracy, but also reveals the counterintuitive result that adversarial accuracy can even surpass clean accuracy. This demonstrates that adversarial perturbations inherently encode directional priors about the true decision boundary.

</details>


### [94] [SC-MFJ: A Simple Haptic Quality Metric for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06199)
*Souraj Adhikary,Negar Chabi,Andre Mastmeyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SC-MFJ指标，用于评估分割表面是否适合触觉渲染，发现简单高斯后处理相比二进制nnU-Net输出可提升147倍触觉质量，且优于学习的SDF回归方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割指标（Dice、Hausdorff距离）仅测量几何重叠，无法评估分割表面是否适合手术模拟中的触觉渲染。

Method: 提出SC-MFJ（表面约束平均力抖动）指标，通过虚拟触笔在分割表面采样短路径并测量接触力的抖动程度来量化触觉质量，只需约1分钟CPU时间。

Result: 在80例胰腺CT和131例LiTS肝脏数据集上，SC-MFJ显示二进制nnU-Net与高斯后处理间存在147-189倍的触觉质量差距，该差距完全不被Dice和HD95检测到；高斯平滑的触觉质量一致性优于学习型SDF回归。

Conclusion: 对于触觉仿真应用，简单的高斯后处理可能就足够；SC-MFJ这类低成本指标能发现几何指标遗漏的问题。

Abstract: Standard segmentation metrics such as Dice and Hausdorff distance measure geometric overlap but say nothing about whether a segmented surface is suitable for haptic rendering in surgical simulation. We propose SC-MFJ (Surface-Constrained Mean Force Jerk), a simple, inexpensive metric that samples a segmented organ surface with many short virtual stylus walks and measures how jerky the resulting contact forces are. The metric is computed from existing segmentation outputs and uses roughly one minute of CPU time per case. We evaluate three pancreas CT segmentation approaches-binary nnU-Net output, Gaussian-smoothed output, and learned signed distance function (SDF) regression-across 80 cases in five-fold cross-validation. SC-MFJ reveals a 147x gap in haptic quality between the raw binary baseline and simple Gaussian post-processing, a difference entirely invisible to Dice and HD95. It also shows that learned SDF regression, despite requiring full model retraining, produces more variable haptic quality than Gaussian smoothing, with a case-level standard deviation of 168 N/s2 compared with 22 N/s2 for Gaussian. A second evaluation on the LiTS liver dataset (131 cases) confirms the generality of these findings: the binary-to-Gaussian gap widens to 189x, and Gaussian smoothing again produces consistently low force jerk across all folds. Our results suggest that for haptic simulation applications, a one-line post-processing step may be sufficient, and that a cheap metric like SC-MFJ can flag problems that geometric metrics miss.

</details>


### [95] [DisasterBench: A Multimodal Benchmark for UAV-Based Disaster Response in Complex Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06217)
*Tan Zhang,Quanyou Li,Lu Zhang,Jun Liu,Xiaofeng Zhu,Ping Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DisasterBench是一个面向无人机灾害响应的多阶段多模态推理基准，涵盖14种灾害场景和9项关键任务；同时提出DisasterVL轻量级模型（2B参数），通过三阶段训练流程（领域指令微调、链式思维多模态对齐、强化学习策略优化），在21个主流MLLM上优于所有开源模型，达到与GPT-4o相当的推理精度且效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态基准多强调感知（识别/描述），覆盖灾害类型有限，且缺乏灾害响应所需的多阶段推理支持（因果归因、传播预测、损伤分析、决策推理）。

Method: 1) 构建DisasterBench：覆盖14种灾害类型、前/中/后3个阶段、9项任务，含细粒度灾害-任务映射。2) 提出DisasterVL（2B参数）：三阶段训练流水线——领域指令微调 → 链式思维引导的多模态对齐 → 基于强化学习的策略优化。

Result: 在21个主流MLLM上，2B参数的DisasterVL优于所有评估的开源模型，显著缩小了与GPT-4o等闭源模型的差距，达到可比的推理精度且效率更优。

Conclusion: 通过专门构建的基准和轻量级模型，有效提升了无人机灾害场景下的多阶段多模态推理能力，在边缘设备上实现了高效且准确的灾害响应。

Abstract: When a disaster unfolds, responders must answer not only what is happening, but also why it is happening, what will happen next, and what to do now, often from noisy low-altitude UAV views and under tight on-site compute constraints. However, most existing multimodal benchmarks emphasize perception (e.g., recognition/description), cover limited disaster types, and provide insufficient support for the multi-stage reasoning required in practical emergency response. We introduce DisasterBench, a multi-stage multimodal reasoning benchmark for UAV-Based disaster response in complex environments. DisasterBench spans 14 disaster-related scene types and 9 response-critical tasks across pre-, during-, and post-disaster stages, with fine-grained disaster-task mappings that explicitly test causal attribution, propagation prediction, damage analysis, and decision-oriented reasoning. To enable reasoning on the edge, we further propose DisasterVL, a lightweight multimodal model optimized with a three-stage pipeline combining domain instruction tuning, chain-of-thought-guided multimodal alignment, and reinforcement learning-based policy optimization. Experiments across 21 popular MLLMs show that our 2B-parameter DisasterVL outperforms all evaluated open-source models and substantially narrows the gap to state-of-the-art closed-source models, achieving GPT-4o-comparable reasoning accuracy with superior efficiency. The project page is available at https://github.com/TanmouTT/DisasterBench.

</details>


### [96] [Symb-xMIL: Symbolic Explanations for Multiple Instance Learning in Digital Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06224)
*Yanqing Luo,Julius Hense,Niklas Prenißl,Andreas Mock,Klaus-Robert Müller,Thomas Schnake,Mina Jamshidi Idaji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Symb-xMIL，一种基于符号逻辑规则（AND/OR/NOT）的MIL模型后验解释框架，将模型行为量化为与可读决策规则的对齐分数，在病理组织学任务中揭示异构决策模式、隐藏错误并改进生存分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIL解释方法主要依赖热图突出影响区域，但无法解释模型如何组合不同组织区域的特征证据进行预测，限制了可解释性，尤其是当决策依赖于组织特征间的交互时。

Method: 提出Symb-xMIL后验解释框架，通过量化MIL模型行为与人类可读决策规则（逻辑关系：AND、OR、NOT）的对齐程度，生成对齐分数，揭示模型预测背后的语义模式。

Result: 在合成MIL数据上可靠恢复真实逻辑规则；在临床肿瘤检测任务中发现异构决策模式并暴露隐藏模型错误；在TCGA-HNSCC的HPV预测任务中，框架在HPV状态之外进一步细化了患者生存分层，具有潜在临床相关性。

Conclusion: Symb-xMIL将MIL可解释性从视觉归因扩展到结构化、基于规则的推理，实现了更透明和语义化的模型预测解释。

Abstract: Explanations of multiple instance learning (MIL) models are widely used for validation and discovery in digital histopathology. Existing methods primarily rely on heatmaps that highlight influential regions but do not explain how evidence from different tissue regions is combined to produce a prediction. This limits interpretability, especially when decisions depend on interactions between tissue features. We introduce Symbolic explainable MIL (Symb-xMIL), a post-hoc explanation framework that quantifies how a MIL model's behavior aligns with human-readable decision rules, expressed as logical relationships (e.g., AND, OR, NOT) between input features. These alignment scores reveal semantic patterns underlying the model's predictions. We evaluate Symb-xMIL on synthetic and real-world histopathology datasets. On synthetic MIL data, Symb-xMIL reliably recovers ground-truth logical rules. In a clinical tumor detection task, the best-aligned rules uncover heterogeneous decision patterns and expose hidden model errors. On an HPV-prediction task on TCGA-HNSCC, a cohort of head and neck cancer, our framework refines patient survival stratification beyond HPV status with potential clinical relevance. Overall, Symb-xMIL extends MIL explainability beyond visual attribution toward structured, rule-based reasoning, enabling more transparent and semantically grounded interpretation of model predictions.

</details>


### [97] [SAM-Flow: Source-Anchored Masked Flow for Training-Free Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06228)
*Haowang Cui,Rui Chen,Tao Luo,Tao Guo,Zheng Qin,Jiaze Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAM-Flow提出了一种基于源锚定掩码流的无训练局部图像编辑框架，通过侦察图像和注意力图定位可编辑语义区域，仅在该区域进行差分速度更新，并引入时变源锚定投影机制（动态软掩码、过渡区域、时间掩码累积）来提升边界自然度和空间稳定性，实现了无需微调即可精准编辑目标区域并有效保护背景的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练图像编辑方法（基于反演或差分流）通常进行全局潜空间传输，导致编辑效果不可避免地扩散到非目标区域，造成背景泄漏问题。

Method: 1) 使用侦察图像和token锚定注意力图定位可编辑语义区域；2) 仅在定位区域内进行差分速度更新，非目标区域锚定到源图像潜轨迹；3) 引入时变源锚定投影机制，包含动态软掩码、过渡区域和时间掩码累积，提升空间稳定性和边界自然度。

Result: 定性和定量实验表明，SAM-Flow在实现精确语义编辑的同时显著提升了背景保持效果，且可即插即用地集成到Stable Diffusion 3和FLUX等主流流匹配骨干网络中。

Conclusion: SAM-Flow提供了一种简单、通用的局部化无训练图像编辑范式，有效解决了背景泄漏问题，且无需微调即可兼容主流扩散/流匹配模型。

Abstract: Training-free image editing has recently attracted increasing attention due to its ability to modify real images using powerful pre-trained diffusion and flow-matching models without additional training. However, existing inversion-based and differential-flow-based methods usually perform global latent transport, which inevitably propagates editing effects to non-target regions and leads to background leakage. To address this problem, we propose SAM-Flow, a source-anchored masked flow framework for localized training-free image editing. Instead of updating the whole latent representation, SAM-Flow first uses a scout image and token-grounded attention maps to localize the editable semantic regions. It then applies differential velocity updates only within these regions, while anchoring the remaining areas to the source-image latent trajectory. To further improve spatial stability and boundary naturalness, we introduce a time-varying source-anchored projection mechanism with dynamic soft masks, transition regions, and temporal mask accumulation. The proposed method is plug-and-play and can be integrated with mainstream flow-matching backbones such as Stable Diffusion 3 and FLUX without any fine-tuning. Extensive qualitative and quantitative experiments demonstrate that SAM-Flow achieves accurate semantic editing while significantly improving background preservation, providing a simple and general localized editing paradigm for training-free image editing. Code is available at: https://github.com/chwbob/Sam-Flow.

</details>


### [98] [GRAMformer: Any-Order Modality Interactions via Volumetric Multimodal Cross-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06249)
*Giordano Cicchetti,Eleonora Grassucci,Danilo Comminiello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多模态交叉注意力机制VMA，通过计算查询向量与多个模态键向量张成的体积来定义注意力分数，从而显式建模任意阶模态间的联合交互，并基于此设计了GRAMformer架构，在多种多模态学习任务上取得了更好的效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的多模态注意力机制要么通过逐对点积交互（复杂度随模态数量呈二次增长）要么通过拼接所有模态到键中（无法显式建模多种模态的联合配置依赖）来计算注意力分数，缺乏对多模态间高阶联合交互的显式建模能力。

Method: 提出Volumetric Multimodal cross-Attention (VMA)，将注意力分数定义为查询向量与多个模态键向量所张成的几何体积的函数，从而捕捉超越逐对相似性的联合多模态依赖。基于VMA设计了GRAMformer架构，可原生支持任意数量模态的集成。

Result: 在多模态学习任务上，GRAMformer相比现有方法在效果和效率上均取得了改进。

Conclusion: VMA通过体积几何建模实现了对多模态联合交互的显式表达，GRAMformer验证了该机制在多模态学习中的有效性和高效性。

Abstract: Transformer-based multimodal models rely on attention mechanisms to integrate information across heterogeneous modalities. Despite their success, existing multimodal attention formulations compute their scores through collections of pairwise dot-product interactions or by concatenating all the modalities into the keys, even when multiple modalities should be jointly involved. As a consequence, current approaches either incur quadratic complexity in the number of modalities or fail to explicitly model interactions that depend on the joint configuration of multiple representations. In this work, we introduce the Volumetric Multimodal cross-Attention (VMA), a novel cross-attention mechanism in which attention scores are defined as a function of the joint geometry of a query and multiple modality-specific keys. VMA computes the volume spanned by query and key vectors across multiple modalities, capturing joint multimodal dependencies beyond pairwise similarity, enabling native modeling of any-order modality interactions. We integrate VMA into our novel multimodal transformer architecture, named GRAMformer, explicitly designed to integrate any number of modalities. We evaluate the proposed model on multimodal learning tasks, demonstrating improved effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [99] [Geodesic Flow Matching on a Riemannian Degradation Manifold for Blind Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06278)
*Akshay Janardan Bankar,Ankita Chatterjee,Sayan Banerjee,Shreyas Pandith,Kalakonda Sai Shashank,Amit Satish Unde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于黎曼流形上的测地线流匹配方法，用于盲图像恢复，将退化显式建模为流形上的点，学习符合退化空间曲率的内在传输动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定性流方法通常采用欧几里得插值，隐含假设了线性退化几何，无法有效处理未知且可能混合的退化。

Method: 将退化显式建模为低维黎曼流形上的点，在联合图像-流形空间上通过测地线流匹配目标学习内在传输动力学，将恢复问题表述为测地线传输。

Result: 该框架推广了线性流匹配，对混合退化提供了基于测地线合成的原则性处理，并对超出观测退化的泛化能力给出了清晰的理论解释。

Conclusion: 通过几何化的退化建模，显著提升了盲图像恢复在复杂退化场景下的表现和泛化能力。

Abstract: Blind image restoration requires recovering clean images from observations corrupted by unknown and potentially mixed degradations. While recent deterministic flow-based methods model restoration as transport processes that map degraded images to clean ones, they typically rely on Euclidean interpolation, implicitly assuming linear degradation geometry. In this paper, we explicitly model degradations as points on a low-dimensional Riemannian manifold and formulate restoration as geodesic transport on the joint image-manifold space. Using a geodesic flow matching objective, we learn intrinsic transport dynamics that respect the curvature of degradation space. This framework generalizes linear flow matching, provides a principled treatment of mixed degradations as geodesic compositions, and yields a clean theoretical interpretation for generalization beyond observed degradations.

</details>


### [100] [Synthetic Data Generation and Vision-based Wrinkle and Keypoint Detection for Bimanual Cloth Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06292)
*Ariel Herrera,Xueyang Kang,Atal Anil Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Blender合成数据训练的视觉感知框架，结合CNN关键点检测与YOLOv8-OpenCV褶皱抓取点提取，实现了双臂机器人对完全折叠衣物的展开与熨烫操作，关键点模型平均位置误差1.7615像素，无需微调即可迁移至真实织物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纺织品操作因连续形变和自遮挡导致视觉状态估计困难，且缺乏带标注的真实世界数据，现有方法在高遮挡或严重褶皱状态下易失败。

Method: 1) 基于Blender的合成数据管线，自动标注关键点；2) CNN实现排列不变性关键点检测；3) YOLOv8-OpenCV管线从结构褶皱中提取抓取点；4) 双臂算法：通过褶皱展开折叠衣物，角点出现后切换至基于关键点的熨烫操作。

Result: 关键点模型平均位置误差(MPE)为1.7615像素，感知系统无需微调即可直接迁移至真实物理织物，在高遮挡和严重褶皱状态下优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 合成数据训练的视觉系统可有效应对纺织品操作中的变形与遮挡挑战，实现从完全折叠到展开熨烫的全流程自动化。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation of textiles remains challenging because continuous deformation and self-occlusions hinder the robust visual perception required to estimate the cloth's state. To address the lack of annotated real-world data, we developed a Blender-based synthetic pipeline exporting auto-annotated keypoints, and combined manually labeled renders with real-world data to train a wrinkle detector. We present a perception framework integrating a CNN for permutation-invariant keypoint detection and a YOLOv8-OpenCV pipeline to extract grasping points from structural wrinkles. A proposed bimanual algorithm uses this system to stretch fully folded garments via wrinkles, transitioning to keypoint-based ironing once corners emerge. The keypoint model achieves a Mean Position Error (MPE) of 1.7615 pixels. The perception system transfers to physical fabrics without fine-tuning, outperforming baselines that fail in high-occlusion states or yield false positives on severe folds.

</details>


### [101] [Towards One-to-Many Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06294)
*Qi Xu,Yue Tan,Shihao Chen,Jiahao Meng,Anna Wang,Shunping Ji,Hao Fei,Jason Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套针对“一对多时间定位”（OMTG）问题的系统性解决方案，包括建立首个OMTG基准、构建5.6万样本的高质量数据集、设计专门的时间与字幕奖励函数，最终在OMTG Bench上取得43.65%的EtF1，超越Gemini 2.5 Pro和Seed-1.8约15.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间定位研究主要关注单片段检索，而现实场景中常需为单个查询定位多个不连续的视频片段（OMTG）。现有最先进的MLLMs因缺乏事件基数感知能力，在此场景下表现极差（近乎零分），亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 1）建立OMTG基准并引入Count Accuracy (C-Acc)和Effective Temporal F1 (EtF1)作为评估指标；2）通过精细化构建流程，整理出包含5.6万样本的高质量OMTG数据集；3）设计专门针对OMTG的时间奖励函数和字幕奖励函数，其中字幕奖励利用密集视频字幕上的思维链推理，显式引导策略优化朝向精确性和完整性。

Result: 在OMTG Bench上，所提模型实现了43.65%的EtF1，分别超过Gemini 2.5 Pro和Seed-1.8达15.85%和15.61%，达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过基准、数据集和奖励函数的系统性设计，有效解决了OMTG任务中MLLMs的事件基数感知缺失问题，显著提升了一对多场景下的时间定位性能。

Abstract: Temporal Grounding (TG) aims to localize video segments corresponding to a textual query. Prior research predominantly focuses on single-segment retrieval. Real-world scenarios, however, often require localizing multiple disjoint segments for a single query -- a setting we term One-to-Many Temporal Grounding (OMTG). Previous state-of-the-art MLLMs, optimized for one-to-one settings, struggle in this context, often yielding near-zero scores due to a lack of event cardinality perception. To bridge this gap, we present a systematic solution with three key contributions. First, we establish the first comprehensive OMTG benchmark, introducing Count Accuracy (C-Acc) and Effective Temporal F1 (EtF1) as evaluation metrics. Second, we curate a high-quality OMTG dataset comprising 56k samples through a sophisticated construction pipeline. Third, we develop novel temporal and caption reward functions specifically designed for OMTG. In particular, the caption reward leverages Chain-of-Thought reasoning over dense video captions to explicitly guide policy optimization toward both preciseness and completeness. Extensive experiments show our model achieves a new state-of-the-art EtF1 of 43.65\% on OMTG Bench, outperforming Gemini 2.5 Pro and Seed-1.8 by 15.85\% and 15.61\%, respectively.

</details>


### [102] [RhymeFlow: Training-Free Acceleration for Video Generation with Asynchronous Denoising Flow Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06309)
*Chensheng Dai,Shengjun Zhang,Yifan Li,Zhang Zhang,Zheng Zhu,Yueqi Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RhymeFlow提出了一种无需训练的框架，通过解耦不同帧的去噪轨迹来加速DiT视频生成。该方法仅对关键帧进行密集去噪，非关键帧逐步跳过去噪步骤，并引入潜在轨迹投影模块保持时序一致性，在提升推理速度的同时保持视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于DiT的视频生成模型由于3D注意力的二次复杂度导致高延迟和高计算成本。现有加速方法虽然降低了单步计算量，但严格遵循标准扩散管线的固有约束——所有帧必须在所有扩散时间步上经历完整的密集去噪过程。然而相邻帧之间内容与运动高度相关，这种均匀密集去噪对自然视频数据本质上是冗余的。

Method: (1) 识别少量主导潜在语义演化的关键帧；(2) 仅对关键帧进行密集逐步去噪以保证结构完整性，非关键帧逐步跳过去噪步骤以降低计算成本；(3) 引入潜在轨迹投影模块，使关键帧能够与完整且时序一致的序列表示进行交互，解决非关键帧跳过步骤导致的时序连贯性问题。

Result: 在当前的DiT视频生成模型上，RhymeFlow在推理速度和视觉质量上均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: RhymeFlow通过解耦不同帧的去噪轨迹，有效打破了标准扩散管线中所有帧必须经历相同密集去噪过程的约束，在无需训练的条件下实现了视频生成加速，为DiT视频生成模型的高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Video generation models based on Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have achieved remarkable performance in video synthesis, yet they suffer from high inference latency and computational costs due to the quadratic complexity of 3D attention. Existing acceleration methods primarily reduce computational complexity within each individual denoising steps through techniques such as sparse attention and KV-caching. However, they rigidly adhere to the inherent constraint of the standard diffusion pipeline: every frame in the target video sequence must be subjected to a complete, dense denoising process across all diffusion timesteps. We observe that due to the corresponding contents and motions among adjacent frames, when keyframes with critical semantic transitions are anchored, the intermediate states of others often follow more predictable trajectories, which indicates that such uniform, dense denoising process is inherently redundant for natural video data. To this end, we introduce \textbf{RhymeFlow}, a training-free framework that decouples the denoising trajectories of different frames. Specifically, we first identify a sparse set of pivotal key frames that dominate the latent semantic evolution. Then, only these keyframes undergo dense, step-by-step denoising to ensure structural integrity, while non-keyframes progressively skip denoising steps to minimize computational cost. Since skipped intermediate states of non-keyframes break the temporal coherence in keyframe denoising steps, leading to visual degradation, we further introduce a latent trajectory projection module, which enables keyframes to interact with a complete and temporally consistent sequence representation. Extensive experiments on current DiT-based video generation models demonstrate our method outperforms existing baselines with higher inference speed and better visual quality.

</details>


### [103] [StoryVideoQA: Scaling Deep Video Understanding with a Large-Scale, Multi-Genre and Auto-Generated Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06338)
*Zhengqian Wu,Zhixian Liu,Aodong Chen,Jingyang Zhang,Ruizhe Li,Hanlin Ge,Zhongyuan Wang,Chunxia Xiao,Chao Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StoryMindv2多智能体协作框架，自动构建大规模深度视频理解数据集StoryVideoQA（363K问答对，393.2小时视频），并设计PlotTree视频理解智能体，通过层次化情节结构组织长视频内容，显著提升复杂故事线推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VideoQA方法在事实类问答上表现良好，但在深度视频理解（DVU）任务中，由于长视频内容、多类型问题及实例级故事要素的复杂性，手动构建的DVU数据集规模受限、多样性不足，且现有方法难以维持长程角色关联和连贯故事线理解。

Method: 提出StoryMindv2增强型多智能体协作框架，集成监督引导生成机制和多审稿人投票策略，自动构建大规模DVU数据集StoryVideoQA；并提出PlotTree视频理解智能体，将长视频内容重组织为层次化情节结构以支持高效故事线推理。

Result: 构建了目前最大的DVU数据集StoryVideoQA，包含363K+问答对，覆盖393.2小时影视内容（电视剧平均1635秒，电影平均7878秒）；对20种SOTA VideoQA方法的评估表明它们无法充分维持长程角色关联和连贯故事线理解；PlotTree在该基准上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 自动构建大规模DVU数据集能有效缓解数据瓶颈问题；层次化情节结构组织长视频内容是实现深度故事线理解的有效途径。

Abstract: Video question answering (VideoQA) aims to answer questions about given videos. While existing approaches excel on factoid VideoQA, they struggle with deep video understanding (DVU), which requires the comprehension of complex storylines. This challenge arises from the inherent long-range video content, multi-faceted question types, and instance-level story elements, all of which constrain the scale and diversity of manually constructed DVU datasets. These difficulties constrain the scale and diversity of manually-constructed DVU dataset. To address these, we previously introduced StoryMind to automatically construct DVU datasets with balanced fine-grained topics. Though it can generate high-quality question-answer pairs (QAs) for TV series, it suffers significant performance degradation when handling longer and more complex movies. In this paper, we further design StoryMindv2, an enhanced multi-agent collaboration framework to generate high-quality DVU datasets for both TV series and movies. By integrating a novel supervisor-guided generation mechanism and a refined multi-reviewer voting strategy, the framework is utilized to construct StoryVideoQA, the largest DVU dataset to date, featuring over 363K QAs on 393.2 hours diverse story videos including TV series (avg. 1,635 seconds) and movies (avg. 7,878 seconds). Comprehensive evaluations of 20 state-of-the-art VideoQA methods on this large-scale benchmark reveal that they cannot fully maintain long-range character associations or construct a coherent understanding of complex storylines. To bridge this gap, we propose PlotTree, a novel video understanding agent, re-organizing long-range video content into a hierarchical plot structure, enabling efficient storyline reasoning on StoryVideoQA. Project page: https://github.com/nercms-mmap/StoryVideoQA/

</details>


### [104] [Comparison of Deep Learning Frameworks For Rice Disease Mapping From UAV Multispectral Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06359)
*Yadav Raj Ghimire,Jagrati Talreja,Tewodros Syum Gebre,Timothy Agboada,Shikha V. Chandel,Leila Hashemi Beni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究利用无人机多光谱影像，结合CNN和Transformer模型对水稻细菌性叶枯病（BLB）严重程度进行分割。在多种架构中，U-Net++（EfficientNet-B3编码器）在mIoU达97.62%表现最佳，轻量级CNN骨干在田间病害监测中比Transformer更可靠，植被指数的加入可带来微小但稳定的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用无人机多光谱影像和深度学习模型（CNN与Transformer）对水稻细菌性叶枯病严重程度进行精确分割，评估不同架构和输入配置的性能，为田间病害监测提供可靠方法。

Method: 使用公开BLB数据集，评估U-Net（ResNet-101编码器）、U-Net++（EfficientNet-B3/B7）、DeepLabV3+和SegFormer等模型，在三种输入配置（仅多光谱、多光谱+NDVI、多光谱+NDRE）下进行训练和测试，采用mIoU、mF1、mAcc、精确率和召回率作为评价指标。

Result: U-Net++ with EfficientNet-B3取得最佳性能，mIoU达97.62%。SegFormer分割精度较低但推理速度相当。植被指数的加入带来微小但一致的性能提升。轻量级CNN骨干在操作层面比Transformer更可靠。

Conclusion: 轻量级CNN骨干在BLB田间监测中比Transformer模型更可靠实用，植被指数可提供辅助提升，标准化的无人机数据集对比较病害映射方法具有重要价值，推荐在实际田间部署中使用CNN架构。

Abstract: In this study, UAV multispectral imagery is used to segment the severity of bacterial leaf blight (BLB) in rice using convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and transformer-based models. The evaluated architectures include U-Net with a ResNet- 101 encoder, U-Net++ with EfficientNet-B3 and EfficientNetB7, DeepLabV3+, and SegFormer, all trained under a common pipeline with three input configurations (multispectral only, multispectral+NDVI, and multispectral+NDRE). Experiments are conducted using the publicly available BLB dataset with performance reported using mean IoU (mIoU), mean F1 (mF1), mean accuracy (mAcc), precision, and recall. U-Net++ with EfficientNet-B3 achieved the highest performance, with an mIoU of 97.62%. SegFormer obtained lower segmentation accuracy but comparable inference speed. Overall, the results indicate that lightweight CNN backbones remain more reliable for operational BLB monitoring while integration of vegetation indices provides small and consistent improvements. The study also highlights the value of standardised UAV datasets to compare disease mapping methods and encourages the use of CNN architectures for field implementation.

</details>


### [105] [Physics in 2-Steps: Locking Motion Priors Before Visual Refinement Erases Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06361)
*Woojung Han,Seil Kang,Youngjun Jun,Min-Hung Chen,Fu-En Yang,Seong Jae Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现图像到视频扩散模型在少步生成(2步)时物理一致性优于多步生成(50步)，通过频谱分析将原因归结为去噪过程中的相位侵蚀。据此提出无需训练的PhaseLock框架，通过从2步提取运动先验并利用Latent Delta Guidance施加到高保真生成中，有效缓解相位退化，平均提升物理一致性6.2分，仅增加轻微开销(1.06倍时间,1.02倍内存)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像到视频扩散模型生成的视频常违反物理规律，现有方法依赖昂贵的外部引导，亟需高效且无需额外训练的物理一致性改进方案。

Method: 提出PhaseLock框架：(1) 从2步推理中提取有效的运动先验；(2) 通过Latent Delta Guidance将运动先验施加到完整去噪轨迹的高保真生成上，无需额外训练。

Result: 在多种模型上平均提升物理一致性6.2分，时间开销仅为1.06倍，内存开销1.02倍，相比外部引导方法节省约5倍时间。

Conclusion: 相位侵蚀是导致多步生成物理一致性下降的关键因素，PhaseLock通过保留少步推理的运动先验有效缓解该问题，为提升视频扩散模型物理合理性提供了轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Image-to-Video diffusion models leverage input images to generate visually stunning content, yet frequently produce motion that violates physical laws. We reveal a surprising finding: a 2-step generation often exhibits better physical consistency than a 50-step output from the same model. Through spectral analysis, we trace this to phase erosion during denoising; the phase degrades significantly (dropping by $\approx 18\%$ from step 2 to step 50), whereas the magnitude remains relatively stable. Building on this insight, we propose PhaseLock, a training-free framework that preserves the valid motion priors from few-step inference throughout the denoising trajectory. Rather than relying on full-step inference for physical consistency, PhaseLock extracts a motion prior from just 2 steps and enforces it onto high-fidelity generation via Latent Delta Guidance. Our approach effectively mitigates phase degradation, improving physical consistency by an average of 6.2 points across diverse models while largely maintaining visual fidelity, with negligible overhead ($1.06\times$ time, $1.02\times$ memory) and reduced reliance on expensive external guidance methods ($\sim5\times$ time).

</details>


### [106] [GMBFormer: An NDVI-Guided Global Memory Bank Transformer for Urban Green-Space Extraction from Ultra-High-Resolution Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06363)
*Hao Lei,Xi Cheng,Chenlu Shu,Zhiheng Chen,Zhengjie Duan,Haoyu Wang,Zhanfeng Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GMBFormer是一种基于SegFormer的框架，通过解耦NDVI作为物理信息门控、构建全局记忆库进行相似性驱动的原型检索，替代传统邻域驱动的特征传播，在超高分遥感影像城市绿地提取任务上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超高分遥感影像城市绿地提取采用逐块处理方式，限制了视觉相似但空间分离的植被图案间的语义复用；直接将NDVI注入RGB骨干网络会模糊视觉外观学习与物理植被置信度的角色分工。

Method: 提出GMBFormer，基于SegFormer框架：1) RGB通道单独进入骨干网络和解码器；2) NDVI作为物理信息门控，仅允许高置信度植被描述符通过动量更新进入紧凑的全局记忆库；3) 当前块通过记忆介导的交叉注意力查询存储的原型，检索响应以有限开销集成。

Result: 在自建成都UHR数据集（7700张512×512标注块）和ISPRS Potsdam数据集两个减少标注设定下，GMBFormer分别获得89.25%/94.31%、92.17%/95.92%和83.72%/90.86%的mIoU/mDice分数，均优于对照的SegFormer-B4基线。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明，解耦的NDVI准入机制、记忆检索、记忆容量和动量更新共同决定了最终性能。

Abstract: Urban green-space extraction from ultra-high-resolution (UHR) imagery is commonly performed patch by patch, which limits semantic reuse among spatially separated but visually similar vegetation patterns. Directly injecting the Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI) into red-green-blue (RGB) backbones can also blur the roles of visual appearance learning and physical vegetation confidence. We propose GMBFormer, a SegFormer-based framework that replaces adjacency-driven feature propagation with selective, similarity-driven prototype retrieval. Only RGB channels enter the backbone and decoder, while NDVI is decoupled as a physics-informed gate that admits high-confidence vegetation descriptors into a compact global memory bank through momentum updates. During training and inference, the current patch queries stored prototypes through memory-mediated cross-attention, and the retrieved response is integrated with bounded overhead. Experiments use a self-constructed Chengdu UHR dataset with 7,700 labeled 512 x 512 patches and two reduced-label settings derived from the public International Society for Photogrammetry and Remote Sensing (ISPRS) Potsdam dataset. Under the same training and evaluation protocol, GMBFormer obtains mean intersection over union (mIoU)/mean Dice (mDice) scores of 89.25%/94.31%, 92.17%/95.92%, and 83.72%/90.86%, respectively, improving the controlled SegFormer-B4 baseline in each setting. Ablation studies indicate that decoupled NDVI admission, memory retrieval, capacity, and momentum jointly shape the final performance.

</details>


### [107] [Visual Commonsense Driven Knowledge Refinements for Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06369)
*Maëlic Neau,Salim Baloch,Jakob Suchan,Zoe Falomir,Mehul Bhatt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种模型无关的、基于语义引导的知识精炼框架，通过从训练数据中挖掘常识约束（空间、功能、定性关系），在推理时使用声明式常识推理来修正和精炼场景图预测，无需人工规则、无需重新训练，在三个基准上一致提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的场景图生成（SGG）模型在频繁关系类型上表现良好，但在标注稀疏的情况下性能显著下降，无法捕获可靠的视觉常识知识。

Method: 提出一种模型无关的语义引导知识精炼框架：从训练数据中系统挖掘常识约束（空间、功能、定性关系规律），并在推理时利用通用声明式常识推理来修正和精炼排序后的SGG预测结果。

Result: 在三个标准基准数据集上，相比于强基线方法取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 结构化视觉常识推理可以作为纯数据驱动的场景图生成方法的有效补充，证明了在深度场景语义上进行结构化常识推理是实用且有效的。

Abstract: Learning-driven Scene Graph Generation (SGG) models excel on frequent relation types but degrade sharply under annotation sparsity, failing to capture reliable visual commonsense knowledge. We propose a model-agnostic, semantically-guided knowledge refinement framework that systematically mines commonsense-grounded constraints from training data - capturing spatial, functional, and qualitative relational regularities - and uses general declarative commonsense reasoning to correct and refine ranked SGG predictions at inference time. The framework requires no manual rule authoring, no model retraining, and transfers across datasets and architectures. On three standard benchmarks, we obtain consistent improvements over strong baselines, demonstrating that structured visual commonsense reasoning over deep scene semantics is a practical and effective complement to purely learning-based scene graph generation.

</details>


### [108] [EasyLens: A Training-Free Plug-and-Play Subtle-Lesion Representation Amplifier for Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06379)
*Qiwei Zeng,Hao Wang,Jinghao Lin,Shuchang Ye,Yuezhe Yang,Yige Peng,Haoyuan Che,Jinman Kim,Lei Bi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EasyLens是一种无需训练的即插即用型微小病变表示增强模块，通过构建病理-解剖原型空间（EasyBank）、反事实原型推理的病变补丁选择（EasyTag）以及形态学引导的残差增强（EasyAmplifier），有效提升冻结医学视觉语言模型对微小病变的检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VLM对微小病变（稀疏、低对比度、嵌入复杂解剖背景）的敏感性不足，而现有增强方法需要额外训练或模型特定适配，且可能过拟合特定病变形态，无法直接应用于冻结的医学VLM。

Method: 提出EasyLens框架，包含三个组件：1) EasyBank：构建病理-解剖原型空间，提供病变原型和解剖感知的正常参考；2) EasyTag：通过反事实原型推理选择病变相关补丁；3) EasyAmplifier：通过形态学引导的残差增强强化所选病变补丁表示，增加其在全局图像嵌入中的贡献。

Result: 在多个医学图像数据集和冻结医学VLM骨干上，EasyLens提升了微小病变检测性能，并优于现有的编码器增强基线方法。

Conclusion: EasyLens作为一种无需训练的即插即用模块，能有效增强冻结医学VLM对微小病变的敏感性，具有良好的泛用性和实用性。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) have shown increasing potential for clinical image interpretation, including lesion detection and report generation. However, their practical utility remains limited by insufficient sensitivity to subtle lesions, whose visual evidence is often sparse, low-contrast, and embedded within complex anatomical context. As local visual tokens are aggregated, these weak lesion cues can become underrepresented in global image representations, making them difficult for medical VLMs to recognize. Existing efforts to improve lesion sensitivity mainly rely on medical-domain vision-encoder pre-training, clinical-term-guided alignment, or trainable pathological representation enhancement. Although effective, these approaches usually require additional training or model-specific adaptation and may overfit to particular disease morphologies, limiting their applicability to frozen medical VLMs. To address these limitations, we propose EasyLens, a training-free plug-and-play subtle-lesion representation amplifier for medical VLMs. EasyLens first constructs EasyBank, a pathology-anatomy prototype space that provides lesion-related prototypes and anatomy-aware normal references for comparing suspicious patches against both pathological and normal anatomical patterns. To avoid blindly amplifying normal tissues, EasyTag selects lesion-relevant patches through counterfactual prototype reasoning. To counteract the dilution of subtle lesion cues in global image representations, EasyAmplifier strengthens the selected lesion-relevant patch representations through morphology-guided residual enhancement, thereby increasing their contribution to the global image embedding. Experiments on multiple medical image datasets and frozen medical VLM backbones show that EasyLens improves subtle-lesion detection and outperforms existing encoder-enhancement baselines.

</details>
