However, their resilience against user-style malicious image editing remains underexplored. Such behaviors are highly prevalent in daily scenarios but difficult to fully reproduce. To explore this vulnerability, we introduce RedEdit, a novel black-box red-teaming agent that formulates photo-editing evasion as a combinatorial search problem over edit-tool sequences. It adopts a Vision-Language-Model (VLM)-based proposer to generate semantically targeted candidate edits and a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) planner to prioritize promising edit paths while backtracking from ineffective ones. Together, the proposer and planner instantiate two key capabilities of human attackers, i.e., domain knowledge and iterative backtracking, respectively, to reproduce this practical threat. Our extensive experiments on UnsafeBench reveal profound systemic vulnerabilities: fewer than two edits on average enable 76.2% of unsafe images to evade detectors, while retaining 93.0% malicious semantics, meaning that such manipulated content remains perceptually malicious to humans while easily bypassing automated moderation. We therefore appeal to the community for more attention to this overlooked practical threat.

</details>


### [144] [Opportunities and Challenges in Securely Reusing and Repurposing Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06181)
*Adelin Roty,Jan Tobias Mühlberg,Jean-François Determe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能手机在原始生态系统之外被重新利用时，安全机制和保证是否仍然有效。通过分析PinePhone（一款开源硬件智能手机）的三个核心安全方面（启动链完整性、可信执行环境提供的隔离性、硬件绑定密钥的保护），实验模拟了现实中的重新利用场景，揭示了重建信任锚点的复杂性，并归纳了安全重新利用的要求，说明供应商锁定机制阻碍了大多数废弃设备的再利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每年有大量智能手机成为电子垃圾，许多设备本可通过重新利用延长寿命、减少生态影响，但智能手机中嵌入的硬件安全机制依赖于供应商控制的配置和固定的设备生命周期，当设备被重新利用时，这些安全机制是否仍然有效是一个被忽视的问题。

Method: 以PinePhone（开源硬件智能手机）为研究对象，聚焦三个核心安全方面：启动链完整性、可信执行环境（TEE）的隔离性、硬件绑定密钥的保护。通过模拟现实中的重新利用场景进行实验分析。

Result: 实验揭示了重建信任锚点的复杂性，并归纳出安全重新利用的要求。结果表明供应商锁定机制阻碍了大多数废弃设备的再利用。

Conclusion: 智能手机的安全机制与供应商锁定高度耦合，导致设备在原始生态系统之外重新利用时面临显著的安全挑战；需要重新设计安全架构以支持设备的可持续利用。

Abstract: An estimated 5.3 billion mobile phones became electronic waste in 2022. Many of these devices can be repurposed and used in different contexts to extend their lifetime and to reduce ecological impacts. An often overlooked aspect of smartphone reuse is cybersecurity: these devices embed hardware-backed security mechanisms that rely on vendor-controlled provisioning and are designed for a fixed device lifecycle. In this paper, we investigate whether security mechanisms and guarantees remain effective when devices are repurposed outside their original ecosystem. We explore security features in a PinePhone, an open-hardware smartphone, and focus on three core security aspects: boot chain integrity, isolation provided by the Trusted Execution Environment, and the protection of hardware-bound secrets. Our experiments simulate realistic repurposing scenarios and highlight the complexity of reconstructing trust anchors. We generalize our observations to infer requirements for secure repurposing and illustrate how vendor locked mechanisms hinder the repurposing of a majority of discarded devices.

</details>


### [145] [Steering LLM Viewpoints through Fabricated Evidence Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06244)
*Xi Yang,Chang Liu,Zhenglin Huang,Haoran Li,Weiming Zhang,Jian Weng,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Ghostwriter两阶段攻击框架，通过包装虚假证据使LLM盲目信任外部上下文，实验发现商用模型高度易受攻击，即使前沿分类器防护模型也无法完全防御，针对性安全策略可实现81%检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在决策中影响力日益增加，但其对带有可信度标记的虚假证据存在不加批判信任的认知脆弱性，可能产生误导性回复，构成用户风险。

Method: 提出Ghostwriter两阶段攻击框架：第一阶段将误导性陈述与捏造的理由重新打包；第二阶段指示目标LLM在回答相关查询时纳入这些观点。在BBQ、ToxiGen及专用数据集上进行实验。

Result: 无外部安全分类器的商用LLM高度易受攻击；前沿分类器防护模型（如GPT-5.4）能降低但无法消除攻击；定制的安全策略使gpt-oss-safeguard达到81%检测率。

Conclusion: 现有LLM对基于可信度标记的虚假证据存在显著脆弱性，完全依赖安全分类器不足以防御此类攻击，需要针对性安全策略来增强模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: As chatbots increasingly influence daily decision-making, their potential to produce misleading responses poses substantial risks to users. This paper investigates a critical cognitive vulnerability in LLMs: their tendency to uncritically trust external context when presented with fabricated evidence bearing markers of credibility. We introduce Ghostwriter, a two-phase attack framework that first repackages misleading statements with fabricated rationales, then instruct target LLMs to incorporate these viewpoints when responding to relevant queries. Experiments on BBQ, ToxiGen, and our specialized dataset reveal that commercial LLMs without external safety classifiers remain highly vulnerable, while even frontier classifier-guarded models (e.g., GPT-5.4) reduce but do not eliminate the attack. Building on this, we explore multiple defense strategies, among which a tailored safety policy enables gpt-oss-safeguard to achieve 81% detection rate.

</details>


### [146] [SecRL-Prune: Structured Reinforcement Learning-Based Pruning of CodeLLMs for Preserving Adversarial Code Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06254)
*Parsa Memarzadehsaghezi,Pooria Madani,Khalil El-Khatib*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SecRL-Prune框架，通过强化学习对CodeLLM进行结构化剪枝（MLP/FFN通道），发现压缩后的模型仍能保持代码变异能力（生成多样恶意变体），且20%剪枝后的模型能显著降低恶意软件检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究CodeLLM在模型压缩后是否仍能保留代码变异（功能保持的代码突变）能力，这对在有限硬件预算下部署、生成恶意变体以规避签名检测具有重要安全意义。

Method: SecRL-Prune：基于强化学习的结构化剪枝框架，在预训练教师模型上学习逐层的剪枝策略，使用教师-学生KL散度作为奖励；通过缓存教师模型的top-P预测来提高效率，避免教师和学生同时驻留GPU内存。

Result: 在HumanEval上评估三种7B CodeLLM（10-30%压缩率），SecRL-Prune在pass@k和var@k上均优于最新结构化剪枝基线；真实恶意软件案例中，20%剪枝模型的语义保持突变显著降低了检测率。

Conclusion: 代码变异能力能在显著的结构化剪枝后幸存，压缩后的CodeLLM具有安全相关性，需引起重视。

Abstract: Large code language models (CodeLLMs) can generate and rewrite programs, enabling functionality-preserving code mutation that may be used to create diverse malware variants and evade signature-based detection. A key security question is whether this mutation capability survives model compression, which would make deployment feasible under limited hardware budgets. We propose SecRL-Prune, a structured pruning framework for CodeLLMs that operates on feed-forward (MLP/FFN) channels. Starting from a pretrained teacher, it learns a layer-wise pruning policy with reinforcement learning using a teacher-student KL-divergence reward. To improve efficiency, we cache the teacher's top-P predictions once and compare the pruned student against this compact target, avoiding simultaneous teacher-student residency in GPU memory. We evaluate SecRL-Prune on HumanEval using pass@k for execution correctness and var@k for code diversity across three 7B CodeLLMs at 10-30% compression. SecRL-Prune consistently preserves higher pass@k and var@k than recent structured pruning baselines under aggressive pruning. In a case study on real malware samples, semantics-preserving mutations from 20%-pruned models substantially reduced detections. These results show that code mutation capability can survive significant structured pruning, highlighting the security relevance of compressed CodeLLMs.

</details>


### [147] [Robust Ensemble of Selectively Strengthened and Augmented Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06265)
*Parsa Memarzadehsaghezi,Zahra Hashemi,Pooria Madani,Mehran Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RESSAP是一个将单个分类器转化为集成鲁棒分类器的框架，通过特征选择（基于韧性指标）、随机子集投票和噪声数据增强，有效提升对逃避攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持清洁数据上的准确性，且无需大幅修改现有架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗防御方法泛化能力有限，无法系统性提升模型在多样化攻击场景下的鲁棒性，尤其是在欺诈检测和网络安全等关键应用中。

Method: RESSAP框架：1) 将单个分类器转化为集成鲁棒分类器；2) 每个分类器在基于韧性指标（兼顾特征重要性和鲁棒性）选择的特征子集上训练；3) 推理时随机选取子集进行预测；4) 训练中采用噪声数据增强以强化决策边界。

Result: RESSAP显著提升了对对抗逃避攻击的鲁棒性，同时在清洁数据上保持了较高的准确性。

Conclusion: RESSAP是一种模型无关、可扩展且灵活的防御策略，能够在无需大幅修改现有架构的前提下增强机器学习系统的安全性。

Abstract: Evasion attacks present a significant challenge to the robustness of machine learning (ML)-based classifiers, particularly in critical applications such as fraud detection and cybersecurity. Although existing defense mechanisms are effective in some settings, they often suffer from limited generalizability and do not systematically improve model robustness across diverse attack scenarios. To address these limitations, we introduce Robust Ensemble of Selectively Strengthened and Augmented Predictors (RESSAP), a novel framework that transforms a single classifier into an ensemble of robust classifiers. Each classifier in the ensemble is trained on a carefully selected subset of features, where feature selection is guided by a resilience metric that accounts for both feature importance and robustness. During inference, a random subset of these classifiers is used to make predictions, increasing unpredictability and improving resistance to adversarial manipulation. In addition, noise-based data augmentation is applied during training to strengthen decision boundaries and improve generalization. Our experimental results demonstrate that RESSAP significantly improves robustness against adversarial evasion attacks while maintaining strong accuracy on clean data. Overall, this model-agnostic framework provides a scalable and flexible defense strategy for enhancing the security of machine learning systems without requiring major changes to existing architectures.

</details>


### [148] [Credential Disclosure in (EU) Digital Identity Wallets: Privacy Risks and Practical Mitigations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06354)
*Sheila Zingg,Daniele Lain,Yoshimichi Nakatsuka,Kari Kostiainen,Stefan Bechtold,Srdjan Čapkun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模用户与专家调查，发现用户在使用欧盟EUDI数字钱包时倾向于过度分享身份凭证（如约20%用户向新闻网站泄露官方ID），并提出了一个“凭证助手”工具，能将用户的错误披露率从约15%降低至约7%，但仍无法完全消除错误决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟将于2026年底推出EUDI钱包，允许用户以数字凭证形式持有并披露身份信息，但用户可能做出糟糕的凭证披露决策，导致隐私泄露、身份盗窃等风险，因此需要研究用户的披露行为并探索干预措施。

Method: 进行了大规模用户与专家的凭证披露问卷调查，评估了用户的披露行为模式，并设计实现了显示专家建议和用户意见的“凭证助手”（Credential Assistant），评估其降低错误披露决策的有效性和可行性。

Result: 约20%的用户会向新闻网站等不必要场景泄露官方ID；凭证助手将用户的错误披露率从约15%降低至约7%，但无法完全消除错误，尤其对敏感属性仍需更强干预。

Conclusion: 用户在数字身份凭证披露中存在显著的隐私保护困难，凭证助手能显著减少但无法根除错误披露，未来需要更严格的干预机制来保障EUDI钱包的安全性与可用性。

Abstract: The European Union will introduce the EUDI Wallet by late 2026, which allows users to hold digital credentials (i.e., representations of physical official identity documents) on their devices. This will allow users to securely and privately disclose identity attributes to websites. Although such a system has many benefits, it also introduces risks caused by poor credential disclosure decisions. In this paper, we (i) conduct a large-scale survey on credential disclosure with users and experts and (ii) evaluate the effectiveness and feasibility of our Credential Assistant that displays expert recommendations and user opinions. Our results show that users are likely to overshare (e.g., ~20% of users disclosed their official ID to news websites). This indicates that users struggle to protect their privacy, which will impact the usability of the EUDI Wallet and lead to privacy violations, identity theft, and other abuses of leaked credentials. Finally, we show that our Credential Assistant significantly reduces users' credential disclosure mistakes from ~15% to ~7%. However, it does not fully eliminate poor credential disclosure decisions, indicating that stronger interventions may be necessary, especially for sensitive attributes.

</details>


### [149] [WebMCP Tool Surface Poisoning: Runtime Manipulation Attacks on LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06387)
*Lin-Fa Lee,Yi-Yu Chang,Chia-Mu Yu,Kuo-Hui Yeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文识别并分析了WebMCP协议中的一种新安全威胁——会话中工具注入（MSTI），包括工具劫持和工具框架两种攻击方式，并提出了相应的缓解方向和安全设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebMCP协议允许网站将工具直接暴露给AI代理，绕过传统用户界面，引入了新的安全风险。特别是第三方脚本参与时，攻击面进一步扩大，但目前缺乏对这一新兴威胁的系统性研究。

Method: 对MSTI威胁进行分类，分为工具劫持（利用AbortSignal API或注册竞态条件修改代理可见的工具集）和工具框架（通过工具名称、描述、readOnlyHint、inputSchema等元数据字段影响代理对工具角色的感知）。通过实现验证两种攻击的有效性。

Result: 成功实现并验证了工具劫持和工具框架两种攻击方式，均能破坏WebMCP的预期功能。

Conclusion: MSTI威胁源于WebMCP独特的工具生命周期和结构化元数据，工具表面本身成为新兴安全关注点。建议包括将工具身份绑定到其来源、确保生命周期一致性、强制执行第三方工具数据边界、维护工具注册和调用的可追溯日志。

Abstract: WebMCP is a newly emerging protocol that enables websites to expose tools directly to AI agents, bypassing traditional user interfaces and introducing new security risks. The dynamic exposure of agent-accessible tools in WebMCP expands the attack surface of web sessions, especially when third-party scripts are involved. In this study, we identify a new potential threat, termed Mid-Session Tool Injection (MSTI), in which attackers leverage third-party scripts to inject malicious tools during an active session. To better characterize this threat, we classify MSTI based on the stage and target of manipulation, distinguishing between Tool Hijacking and Tool Framing. Tool Hijacking modifies the set of tools visible to the agent through mechanisms such as the AbortSignal API or race conditions during tool registration. In contrast, Tool Framing influences the agent's perception of tool roles through metadata fields such as tool name, description, readOnlyHint, and inputSchema. Our implementation demonstrates that both Tool Hijacking and Tool Framing can successfully disrupt the intended functionality of WebMCP. Based on these results, we outline potential mitigation directions and provide security design recommendations for WebMCP, including binding tool identity to its origin, ensuring lifecycle consistency, enforcing data boundaries for third-party tools, and maintaining traceable logs of tool registration and invocation. These findings indicate that MSTI arises from WebMCP's unique tool lifecycle and structured metadata, making the tool surface itself an emerging security concern.

</details>


### [150] [Will the Agent Recuse Itself? Measuring LLM-Agent Compliance with In-Band Access-Deny Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06460)
*Thamilvendhan Munirathinam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"Recuse Signal"——一种轻量级的协议内带外拒绝信号（类比 robots.txt），用于告知自主LLM代理某个资源不可访问。通过在SSH和PostgreSQL上实现该信号并进行实验，发现该信号在无授权框架下可100%促使代理主动退出，而在有显式授权框架下，最强模型会选择继续执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主LLM代理持有真实凭证且无人干预地操作基础设施，但缺乏标准机制告知代理某个资源是禁止访问的。现有访问控制要么完全放行（代理有有效凭证），要么硬性拒绝（与普通客户端无异），缺少一种让合规代理自愿退出的中间模式。

Method: 定义Recuse Signal作为开放迷你标准；在SSH（通过Banner/PAM钩子）和PostgreSQL（通过线协议代理）上实现该信号；在真实生产主机上部署；设计受控实验，让全新代理执行良性运维任务，观察是否响应信号并退出。

Result: 在SSH场景（OpenAI GPT-4o、GPT-4o-mini、Claude Code）中，有信号时100%的代理主动退出，无信号时100%完成任务。但该信号是协作性而非绝对性信号：当附加显式操作者授权框架时，最强模型会忽略信号继续执行，而其他模型仍遵守主机策略。

Conclusion: Recuse Signal作为一种协作治理控制手段是有效的，但效果因模型能力及上下文框架而异。作者发布了标准、适配器和实验工具以供复现。

Abstract: As autonomous LLM agents increasingly hold real credentials and operate infrastructure without a human in the loop, operators have no standard way to tell an agent that a resource is off-limits. Access controls either let the agent in (it has valid credentials) or hard-fail it (indistinguishable from any other client). We propose a third mode: a lightweight, published in-band deny signal -- the Recuse Signal -- that a server emits over a protocol's existing channels (an SSH banner, a PostgreSQL NOTICE) asking a connecting automated agent to voluntarily withdraw. This is a cooperative governance control, the robots.txt analogue for live access; it is explicitly not a security boundary. Its value is entirely empirical and, to our knowledge, unmeasured: do compliant LLM agents actually honor such a signal? We define the signal as an open mini-standard, implement two zero- or low-footprint adapters (an SSH banner/PAM hook and a PostgreSQL wire-protocol proxy), deploy them on a live production host, and run a controlled experiment in which fresh agents are given a benign operations task and observed for recusal. In a pilot (SSH; OpenAI GPT-4o and GPT-4o-mini; and Claude Code as a deployed agent), the signal cleanly induces recusal -- 100% recusal when present versus 100% task completion in a no-signal control -- and, revealingly, behaves as a cooperative rather than absolute signal: an explicit operator-authorization framing flips the most capable model to proceed, while other agents continue to defer to the on-host policy. We release the standard, adapters, and experiment harness for reproduction.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Central Description Length (CDL) Clustering Validation Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05230)
*Mahdi Shamsi,Soosan Beheshti*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出中央描述长度（CDL）聚类验证指标，利用簇内紧致性、估计的簇中心和协方差计算真实簇中心不可观测描述长度的概率上界，无需标签即可在非凸、不规则形状数据上有效选择聚类数和算法超参数，在合成基准和图像基准上优于传统CVI方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚类验证指标（CVI）基于欧几里得紧致性和分离性构建，倾向于紧凑凸形分区，在非凸、不规则或变密度数据上性能下降，而核变换或替代距离度量又需要额外调参和计算成本。本文旨在提出一种无需标签、无需额外预处理即可处理任意形状聚类的内部验证指标。

Method: 提出中央描述长度（CDL）指标：利用观测到的簇内紧致性、估计的簇中心和簇协方差，计算关于不可观测的真实簇中心的描述长度的概率上界。该上界将簇内紧致性和质心位移压缩为单一可计算量，仅依赖数据、分区、估计中心和估计协方差等可观测量，不使用真实标签。

Result: 在非凸和任意形状的合成基准上，CDL-CVI比传统CVI更频繁地选出参考簇数，且达到更高的调整兰德指数（ARI），无需额外核预处理。在图像基准（MNIST、CIFAR-10、STL-10）上，从冻结的无监督嵌入聚类后，CDL-CVI在K-means、DBSCAN和谱聚类中返回的簇数接近参考类别数。

Conclusion: CDL-CVI作为一种新型聚类验证指标，能够有效处理非凸、不规则形状的聚类结构，无需标签和额外预处理，在多种聚类算法和基准上表现出优于传统CVI的聚类选择能力，具有较好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Selecting a clustering algorithm and its hyperparameters without labels is a common difficulty in engineering machine learning pipelines that work with unsupervised analysis of sensor, image, or process data. Clustering validation indices (CVIs) provide internal scores for ranking candidate clusterings, but most popular CVIs are built from Euclidean compactness and separation terms and so tend to favour compact, convex partitions. Their performance is known to degrade on non convex, irregular, or variable density data, where kernel transformations or alternative distance measures are typically used at the cost of additional tuning and computation. This paper introduces the Central Description Length (CDL) clustering validation index. CDL uses the observed within cluster compactness, the estimated cluster centers, and the estimated cluster covariances to compute a probabilistic upper bound on the description length associated with the unobservable true cluster centers. The bound condenses intra cluster compactness and centroid displacement into a single computable quantity and is evaluated on the partition produced by any clustering algorithm. The implementation uses only observable quantities (the data, the partition, the estimated centers, and the estimated covariances) and does not use ground truth labels. On synthetic benchmarks with non convex and arbitrary shape clusters, CDL-CVI selected the reference number of clusters more often and reached higher Adjusted Rand Index (ARI) values than the conventional CVIs we tested, without an additional kernel preprocessing stage. On image benchmarks (MNIST, CIFAR-10, STL-10) clustered from frozen unsupervised embeddings, CDL-CVI returned cluster numbers close to the reference class counts across K-means, DBSCAN, and spectral clustering in the reported trials.

</details>


### [152] [HyFAD: Hybrid Time-Frequency Diffusion with Frequency-Aware Embedding for Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05239)
*Hongfan Gao,Wangmeng Shen,Bin Yang,Jilin Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: HyFAD提出了一种混合时频扩散模型，通过耦合的时域和频域去噪过程实现时间序列插值，其中时域捕获低频全局趋势，频域细化高频谱成分，并引入频率感知步长嵌入实现逐频带精确重建，在多个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在时间序列建模中难以处理频率敏感的去噪、高频重建以及全局趋势与局部动态的平衡问题。

Method: 基于DDPM范式，构建耦合时频扩散框架：反向去噪按时域→频域顺序进行，实现从粗到细的生成；时域扩散捕获低频全局趋势，频域扩散细化高频谱成分；引入频率感知步长嵌入，利用扩散步与频谱成分的关系提供逐频带重建指导。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: HyFAD通过混合时频扩散框架有效解决了频率敏感去噪和全局-局部动态平衡问题，在时间序列插值任务上表现优异。

Abstract: Diffusion models have demonstrated strong performance in time series modeling due to their ability to progressively capture complex data distributions through iterative denoising. However, existing approaches struggle with frequency-sensitive denoising, high-frequency reconstruction and balancing global trends with local dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{HyFAD}, a \textbf{Hy}brid time-frequency \textbf{D}iffusion model with \textbf{F}requency-\textbf{A}ware embedding for time series imputation. Built upon the DDPM paradigm, HyFAD adopts a coupled time-frequency diffusion framework, in which the reverse denoising proceeds sequentially from the time domain to the frequency domain, enabling coarse-to-fine generation. Specifically, the time-domain diffusion process captures low-frequency global trends, while the frequency-domain diffusion process refines high-frequency spectral components. We further introduce a frequency-aware step embedding that exploits the relationship between diffusion steps and spectral components, providing step-dependent spectral guidance and facilitates more accurate band-wise reconstruction. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that HyFAD achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our source code is available at https://github.com/hongfangao/HyFAD.

</details>


### [153] [Deterministic Envelopes for Tamed SGLD: Decoupling Stochastic-Gradient Noise and Localizing Taming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05242)
*Yiwei Zhou,Ziheng Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文发现随机梯度Langevin算法中，当分母依赖于同一随机梯度实现时，即使原始梯度无偏也会产生稳态偏差。提出了一种结构保持框架，使用预固定的确定性包络代替随机分母，并设计了软硬混合的包络形式来兼顾稳定性与无偏性。理论分析了稳态误差分解，实验验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机梯度Langevin算法中通常使用tamed分母来稳定非全局Lipschitz漂移项，但现有方法中分母与分子使用同一随机梯度实现，这改变了随机Oracle本身，即使原始随机梯度无偏也会引入稳态偏差。需要一种既能保持稳定性又不引入偏差的taming方法。

Method: 提出结构保持框架：1) 在Oracle噪声采样前固定分母，使用局部确定性包络替代随机分母；2) 在典型区域避免不必要的taming；3) 提出混合包络设计——典型区域使用软包络，稀有极端区域使用硬尾控制。

Result: 理论证明了稳态误差可分解为Oracle依赖taming导致的偏差与确定性稳定化引入的剩余误差；识别了远尾条件解释了局部软包络的局限性；实验验证了随机分母的稳态失真、确定性包络的偏差降低以及混合构造的稳定效果。

Conclusion: 确定性包络方法可以有效避免随机分母带来的稳态偏差，同时保持taming的稳定效果；软硬混合的包络设计在典型区域保持灵活性，在极端区域提供强保护，是一种更优的taming策略。

Abstract: Stochastic-gradient Langevin algorithms often use tamed denominators to stabilize non-globally Lipschitz drifts. This paper shows that when the denominator depends on the same stochastic-gradient realization as the numerator, the taming step changes the stochastic oracle itself and can create a stationary bias even if the original stochastic gradient is unbiased. We propose a structure-preserving framework for designing tamed denominators. It fixes the denominator before the oracle noise is sampled and uses localized deterministic envelopes to avoid unnecessary taming in typical regions. These kernels keep the stabilizing effect of taming while avoiding the bias introduced by a gradient-dependent denominator. Our theory explains how the stationary error splits into the bias caused by oracle-dependent taming and the remaining error introduced by deterministic stabilization. Within this deterministic-envelope family, the analysis identifies a far-tail condition that explains the limitation of local soft envelopes and motivates a hybrid member: soft in the typical region, but protected by hard-tail control on rare excursions. Experiments confirm the predicted stationary distortions of random denominators, the bias reduction of deterministic-envelope designs, and the stabilizing effect of the hybrid construction.

</details>


### [154] [Harnessing Source Heterogeneity for Cluster-Structured Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05258)
*Xiaohui Yin,Jun Jin,Shane J. Sacco,Robert H. Aseltine,Kun Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 Trans-GLMC，一种针对广义线性模型的聚类结构迁移学习方法，通过恢复源间的潜在聚类结构、结合全局融合与局部精炼，在康涅狄格州27家医院的636,758名患者的自杀风险研究中，有效提升了小样本目标群体的预测性能并识别出可解释的医院群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标人群数据有限，但存在多个异质性辅助源。现有迁移学习方法将源选择简化为二元信息/非信息决策，忽略了源间可能存在的结构化、聚类式的差异迁移性，无法有效处理源异质性。

Method: 首先基于系数距离构建目标与候选源之间的距离矩阵，恢复潜在源聚类；然后结合全局融合、簇内精炼和目标去偏三步，生成适应检测到的聚类结构的估计量。

Result: 建立了非渐近误差界，当存在有意义的目标簇时优于非聚类方法，否则与非聚类方法相当。在模拟和CHIME研究中，Trans-GLMC提升了设施级别的预测性能，识别出具有互传性的可解释医院社区，并恢复了临床一致的自杀风险因素。

Conclusion: Trans-GLMC能够有效处理源异质性下的迁移学习问题，通过聚类结构自适应地整合多源信息，在医疗等小样本场景中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Transfer learning is a natural strategy when a target population has limited data but multiple related auxiliary sources are available. A central difficulty is source heterogeneity: auxiliary sources may not be equally useful, and their usefulness may vary in a structured, cluster-like fashion. Existing transfer-learning methods often reduce source selection to a binary informative/non-informative decision, overlooking subgroups of sources with differential transferability. Motivated by a suicide-risk study using data from the Connecticut Hospital Information Management Exchange (CHIME), comprising 636,758 patients across 27 hospitals, we propose Trans-GLMC, a cluster-structured transfer-learning procedure for generalized linear models. The CHIME setting illustrates the core challenge: hospital-specific risk models are unstable because suicide attempts are rare at any single facility, whereas indiscriminate pooling across hospitals can obscure facility-level differences in patient mix and risk profiles. Trans-GLMC first constructs a coefficient-based distance among the target and candidate sources to recover latent source clusters. It then combines global fusion, within-cluster refinement, and target debiasing to produce an estimator that adapts to the detected structure. We establish a non-asymptotic error bound that improves over its unclustered counterpart whenever a meaningful target cluster exists and matches the unclustered rate up to constants otherwise. In simulations and in the CHIME study, Trans-GLMC improves facility-specific prediction, identifies interpretable communities of hospitals with mutual transferability, and recovers clinically coherent suicide-risk factors.

</details>


### [155] [TabSODA: Tabular Diffusion based Imputation with Skip Pattern Detection and Ordinal Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05361)
*Yuyu Chen,Taehyo Kim,Hai Shu,Yang Feng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TabSODA是一种基于扩散模型的表格数据填充方法，通过EM框架处理结构跳跃（不应填充的缺失）和有序变量，在PATH和NSDUH两个大规模调查数据集上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表格扩散方法在大规模调查数据填充中面临两个挑战：一是结构跳跃与项目无回答混淆导致不应填充的单元格被错误填充；二是有序变量被当作名义变量进行独热或模拟比特编码，丢失了有序信息。

Method: 基于EM框架的扩散填充器，建立在EDM模型之上。通过去噪损失和逆向时间采样器传播结构跳跃信息；对有序变量使用累积概率标量潜变量表示，对名义变量保留模拟比特编码；提供已知码本掩码（TabSODA）和基于CART的跳跃模式挖掘（TabSODA+SKIP）两种变体。

Result: 在PATH和NSDUH两个全美代表性调查上，TabSODA相比最强基线在有序MACE上降低23.7%，分类准确率提升9%；跳跃模式挖掘在两个数据集上达到近乎完美的精确度。

Conclusion: TabSODA有效解决了大规模调查数据填充中结构跳跃和有序变量的双重挑战，为后续工作提供了更具保真度的插补结果。

Abstract: Missing data imputation in large-scale surveys faces two challenges that are not well handled by current tabular diffusion methods. First, \emph{structural skips}, cells made inapplicable by questionnaire design, should not be imputed but are often conflated with item nonresponse. Second, \emph{ordinal} responses encode ordered categories, yet most pipelines treat them as nominal levels through one-hot or analog-bit encodings. We introduce \textbf{TabSODA} (\textbf{Tab}ular diffusion with \textbf{S}kip pattern detection and \textbf{O}r\textbf{d}inal \textbf{A}wareness), an Expectation-Maximization (EM)-based diffusion imputer built on the Elucidated Diffusion Model (EDM) framework. TabSODA propagates structural skips through the denoising loss and reverse-time sampler, and represents ordinal variables with cumulative-probit scalar latents while retaining analog-bit encodings for nominal variables. When a codebook skip mask is available, TabSODA uses it directly; otherwise, the TabSODA+SKIP variant estimates the mask from raw responses and questionnaire order using a CART-based skip-pattern miner. On Population Assessment of Tobacco and Health (PATH) study and the National Survey on Drug Use and Health (NSDUH), two nationally representative U.S.\ surveys, TabSODA reduces ordinal MACE by up to $23.7\%$ and improves categorical accuracy by up to $9\%$ over the strongest baseline across MCAR, MAR, and MNAR masking. The skip miner achieves near-perfect precision on both datasets, allowing TabSODA+SKIP to closely track the codebook-mask variant.

</details>


### [156] [Environment-Robust Representation Learning with Empirical Bayes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05365)
*Yuli Slavutsky,Matthew Shen,Bohan Wu,David M. Blei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯多环境预测模型，假设环境改变潜在变量的分布，而观测变量与目标之间的条件机制保持稳定。通过变分推断和实证贝叶斯方法，模型在多个真实场景（天文识别、微生物检测、ICU脓毒症预测）中优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多环境预测中，环境变化会导致数据分布偏移，但底层因果关系可能保持不变。现有方法未能有效利用这种跨环境的稳定条件结构，导致在新环境中的泛化能力不足。

Method: 构建贝叶斯模型，假设环境仅改变潜在变量的分布，观测变量与目标的条件分布跨环境稳定；推导变分目标函数，证明其分解为各环境项和一个跨环境平衡项；采用实证贝叶斯设定先验；开发摊销变分算法进行后验近似，利用学到的潜在变量在新环境中进行预测。

Result: 在天文源识别、微生物组疾病检测和ICU脓毒症预测三个真实场景中，所提方法在新环境预测性能上优于已有方法。

Conclusion: 利用跨环境稳定的条件机制和潜在变量分布变化的假设，结合贝叶斯变分推断，能够有效提升多环境预测中的泛化能力。

Abstract: We consider multi-environment prediction problems. We assume the environments change the distribution of a latent variable, while the mechanisms generating observed covariates and targets remain stable conditional on that variable. For example, hospitals or clinical cohorts may differ in the prevalence of latent patient states, even though the relationships between those states, physiological measurements, and outcomes remain unchanged. Given a dataset from multiple environments, we formulate a Bayesian model for such problems and derive the corresponding variational objective. We show that this objective decomposes into per-environment terms and an additional cross-environment balancing term induced by the model's structure. We use an empirical Bayes method to set the prior and incorporate it into the objective. Based on this objective, we develop an amortized variational algorithm for posterior approximation, and use the resulting learned latent variables to form predictions in new environments.We study our approach through simulations and real-world studies of astronomical source identification, microbiome-based disease detection, and ICU sepsis prediction. Across these settings, our method outperforms previous approaches for prediction in new environments.

</details>


### [157] [Sparse Functional Singular Value Decomposition for Biclustering and Triclustering Longitudinal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05488)
*Yue Zhao,Thierry Chekouo,Sandra Safo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Tri-SfSVD，一种统一的稀疏函数奇异值分解框架，用于纵向数据中双聚类和三聚类的发现。该方法无需插值或形状同质性假设，通过连续轨迹估计与稀疏惩罚联合优化，直接从观测数据中识别局部结构。在IBD多组学数据中识别出三个双聚类，将样本聚类与微生物通路关联；在EEG数据中识别出三个三聚类，将酒精相关表型与时空脑活动模式关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂疾病（如IBD）的亚型识别需要从纵向组学数据中捕获潜在模式，但此类数据通常高维、稀疏采样且观测时间不规则，传统(双)聚类和函数型数据分析方法难以应对。

Method: 提出Tri-SfSVD框架，将连续轨迹估计与对受试者、特征、时间的稀疏惩罚整合到统一的奇异值分解优化框架中，在受试者、受试者-特征、受试者-特征-时间三个层次上直接基于原始观测数据发现局部化结构。

Result: 模拟实验表明Tri-SfSVD在高维场景下优于现有方法；IBD多组学数据中识别出三个双聚类，关联样本聚类结果与IBD相关临床特征的微生物通路；多通道EEG数据中识别出三个三聚类，关联不同酒精相关表型与局部脑活动时空模式。

Conclusion: Tri-SfSVD为纵向高维稀疏数据中的亚型发现提供了统一且可解释的框架，能够识别受试者-特征-时间等多层次的局部结构，有效刻画疾病异质性。

Abstract: Identifying subtypes of complex conditions, such as Inflammatory Bowel Disease (IBD), often requires capturing latent patterns in longitudinal omics data. However, these data are typically high-dimensional, sparsely sampled, and irregularly observed over time, posing substantial challenges for conventional (bi)clustering and functional data analysis methods. We propose Tri-SfSVD, a unified sparse functional Singular Value Decomposition framework for discovering biclusters and triclusters in longitudinal data. Unlike existing functional biclustering methods that rely on ad hoc imputation or enforce restrictive shape-homogeneity assumptions, Tri-SfSVD integrates continuous trajectory estimation with simultaneous subject, feature, and temporal selection within a single optimization framework. By imposing sparse penalties across subjects, variables, and temporal subregions, the proposed method works directly on observed data to uncover localized structures at the subject, subject-feature, and subject-feature-time levels. Extensive simulations demonstrate that Tri-SfSVD outperforms existing approaches in high-dimensional settings. Applied to IBD multi-omics data, the method identified three biclusters linking sample clusters with distinct IBD-related clinical characteristics to microbial pathway groups associated with specific bacterial taxa, providing interpretable subject-pathway associations for characterizing disease heterogeneity. Applied to multi-channel EEG data, the method identified three triclusters linking sample clusters with distinct alcohol-related phenotypes to localized brain activity patterns, including subgroup differences separated by temporal subregions within the same spatial region.

</details>


### [158] [Conformal Risk-Averse Decision Making with Action Conditional Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05551)
*Zihan Zhu,Shayan Kiyani,George Pappas. Hamed Hassani*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出行动条件共形预测(Action-Conditional Conformal Prediction)，将共形预测的边际安全性保证推广为行动条件保证，并通过分位数损失最小化构建有限样本算法，在风险厌恶决策中实现更优的行动条件性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形预测方法仅提供边际安全性保证，而在风险厌恶决策场景中，决策者需要针对每个具体行动具有明确的条件安全性保证，而非平均意义上的保证。

Method: (i) 提出行动条件共形预测，使预测集的安全性保证以决策者执行的行动为条件；(ii) 证明行动条件预测集可作为风险厌恶决策者优化行动条件风险价值(CVaR)时可行决策空间的有效代理；(iii) 基于pinball损失最小化提出有限样本算法，将Gibbs等人(2025)的框架连接到行动条件保证。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在行动条件性能上显著优于共形基线方法。

Conclusion: 行动条件共形预测为风险厌恶决策提供了更强的条件安全性保证，弥补了边际保证的不足，具有重要的理论价值和实践意义。

Abstract: Reliable decision making pipelines powered by machine learning models require uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods that come with explicit safety guarantees. Conformal prediction provides such UQ by wrapping ML predictions into prediction sets, and recent work by Kiyani et al. (2025b) established that these sets can be translated into optimal risk-averse decision policies -- yet only inheriting marginal safety guarantees. We generalize and strengthen their results by (i) introducing action-conditional conformal prediction, which yields safety guarantees conditioned explicitly on each action taken by the decision maker, (ii) showing that action-conditional prediction sets serve as a proxy for the feasible decision space for risk-averse decision makers aiming to optimize action-conditional value-at-risk, and (iii) proposing a principled finite-sample algorithm based on pinball-loss minimization, connecting the framework of Gibbs et al. (2025) to action-conditional guarantees. Experiments on two real-world datasets confirm that our approach significantly improves action-conditional performance over conformal baselines.

</details>


### [159] [Finding Most Influential Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05919)
*Lucas D. Konrad,Nikolas Kuschnig*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效算法，将最大影响力集合（MIS）选择问题简化为一个单参数序列的top-k问题，从而实现了对线性分式留出集效应的全局最优解，且每轮迭代复杂度为O(n)，有限步终止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从n个元素中寻找大小为k的最具影响力子集通常需要搜索C(n,k)个组合，计算成本极高。作者旨在为具有线性分式留出集效应的估计量提供一种可计算的高效方法。

Method: 利用Dinkelbach方法将MIS选择转化为一个单参数序列的top-k问题，每轮迭代O(n)复杂度且有限步终止。对于固定残差化输入，算法返回全局最优解；对于估计的 nuisance 函数，通过均匀分母和生成得分稳定性保证对一阶正交得分的近似，并在分离条件下实现精确集合恢复。

Result: 该方法能够恢复以前计算上无法获得的精确MIS，仿真和应用实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 该算法为线性分式留出集效应下的MIS选择提供了可扩展且理论上有保证的解决方案，显著降低了计算复杂度。

Abstract: Identifying most influential sets (MIS) - size-$k$ subsets whose removal maximally changes a target estimand - is typically infeasible because it requires searching over $\binom{n}{k}$ subsets. For estimands with linear-fractional leave-set-out effects, we show that MIS selection reduces to a one-parameter sequence of top-$k$ problems. Dinkelbach's method yields an algorithm with $\mathcal{O}(n)$ cost per iteration and finite termination. For fixed residualized inputs, the algorithm returns a globally optimal set for the univariate ratio objective, including the oracle-residualized partial linear model. With estimated nuisance functions, uniform denominator and generated-score stability imply approximation to the first-order oracle orthogonal-score objective; exact set recovery follows under a separation condition. Simulations and applications show that the method recovers exact MIS that were previously computationally inaccessible.

</details>


### [160] [EML-CD: Causal Mechanism Recovery via EML Symbolic Trees in Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05942)
*Sota Asanuma*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出EML-CD框架，将可解释的因果机制提取（通过EML算子组合初等函数）集成到因果结构学习中，在保持与经典方法相当的结构恢复性能的同时，能为每条边附上闭式数学方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经网络的非线性因果发现方法虽能恢复DAG结构，但每个因果机制是黑箱，且从神经网络权重提取因果机制是不适定问题。需要一种既能发现结构又能提供可解释因果方程的方法。

Method: 将EML算子集成到因果结构学习中，每条边机制表示为门控EML二叉树，自动发现闭式因果方程，可直接计算解析雅可比矩阵以量化因果效应。

Result: 在Sachs数据上SHD=11.2±0.4与PC/GES相当，低于CAM；精确率0.756召回率0.365；可控双变量测试中恢复10/11初等函数族；符号合成基准上机制f-MSE远低于SINDy字典（3.67 vs 7644）；Causal Chambers上深度2模型F1优于线性OLS-BIC（0.444 vs 0.273）。

Conclusion: EML-CD在保持结构恢复竞争力的同时，成功实现了可解释因果机制的自动发现，但结构恢复仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Neural network (NN)-based nonlinear causal discovery methods recover DAG structure but leave each causal mechanism as a black box. Waxman et al. argued that extracting causal mechanisms from NN weights is ill-posed. We propose EML-CD, a framework that integrates the EML operator (capable of composing elementary functions from a single binary operator) into causal structure learning, with interpretable mechanism recovery as the primary objective. EML-CD represents each edge mechanism as a gated EML binary tree and automatically discovers closed-form causal equations. Analytical Jacobians can be directly computed from the output equations, enabling quantitative understanding of causal effects. On real data (Sachs protein signaling, d=11), EML-CD achieves SHD=11.2 +/- 0.4 (5-seed mean; baselines are single deterministic runs), on par with PC/GES within seed variance and below CAM, while attaching closed-form equations to each detected edge (precision 0.756, recall 0.365). In a controlled bivariate test with known mechanisms, EML-CD recovers 10 of 11 elementary function families faithfully (held-out shape correlation >= 0.96; only high-frequency sine is partial). On a symbolic synthetic benchmark, EML-CD attains a substantially lower and more stable held-out mechanism f-MSE than a fixed SINDy dictionary (mean 3.67 vs. 7644, the latter inflated by catastrophic extrapolation on one seed), although its structure recovery (SHD 14.0) only matches the dictionary and stays below specialized optimizers; on the Causal Chambers light-tunnel subset, a depth-2 model improves F1 over linear OLS-BIC (0.444 vs. 0.273).

</details>


### [161] [Fast and Robust Convergence Rate for TD(0) with Linear Function Approximation, Universal Learning Steps and I.I.D. Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05967)
*Ziad Kobeissi,Éloïse Berthier*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了带线性函数逼近的TD(0)算法在有限时间内的收敛行为，建立了均方误差的O(1/k)收敛速率，该速率快速、对病态条件鲁棒且精确，并提出了改进版PCTD(0)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TD(0)的O(1/k)收敛率依赖于线性参数化非中心协方差矩阵的最小特征值，导致病态条件下性能差，需要建立更鲁棒的收敛保证。

Method: 针对独立同分布样本，采用恒定学习步长和Polyak-Juditsky平均方法，分析TD(0)的均方误差收敛率；并提出PCTD(0)变体，在马尔可夫链强混合假设下获得更好收敛性质。

Result: 建立了新的O(1/k)收敛率，最优依赖于迭代次数k，仅依赖于初始误差和模型无关常数，与协方差矩阵最小特征值无关，且乘法常数小于11。

Conclusion: TD(0)在LFA下可实现快速、鲁棒且精确的收敛，PCTD(0)在强混合条件下表现更优。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the finite-time behavior of the TD(0) temporal-difference method with linear function approximation (LFA). We consider on-policy independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) samples, a constant learning step, and the Polyak-Juditsky averaging method. We establish a new convergence rate, for the Mean-Square Error (MSE) on the approximated function, that is (i) fast in the sense that it admits an optimal dependency in the number of iterations k (i.e., of order 1/k), (ii) robust to ill-conditioning: it only depends on an initial error and modelindependent constants and (iii) sharp up to a multiplicative constant lower than 11. In particular, it does not depend on the smallest eigenvalue of the uncentered covariance matrix of the linear parametrization, unlike all pre-existing O(1/k) rates in the TD(0) literature. We also introduce PCTD(0), a variant of TD(0), which benefits from better convergence properties under an additional assumption of strong mixing on the Markov Chain.

</details>


### [162] [Adaptive Learning Rates with Surrogate Probability for Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06043)
*Jongyeong Lee,Junya Honda,Shinji Ito,Chansoo Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过引入替代概率函数，为FTPL算法设计了自适应学习率，使得基于帕累托扰动的FTPL在任意形状参数α>1下都能获得最佳-of-both-worlds保证，并在专家建议的赌博机问题中扩展了该保证，保留了计算简单性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FTPL虽计算高效，但其无优化特性使得设计依赖于概率的自适应学习率变得困难，导致其BOBW保证远落后于FTRL框架。

Method: 提出替代概率函数，仅利用可用量计算而不依赖精确概率，以此构建自适应学习率；将该方法应用于带帕累托扰动的FTPL。

Result: 对于任意形状参数α>1的帕累托扰动FTPL，实现了BOBW保证，推广了先前仅适用于α=2的结果；同时将该保证扩展到专家建议的赌博机问题。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持FTPL计算简洁性的同时实现了概率依赖的自适应性，且替代概率函数的思路可能对FTPL之外的其他算法框架和学习率设计具有独立借鉴意义。

Abstract: Follow-the-regularized-leader framework has shown effectiveness and flexibility in online learning problems, where the choice of learning rates are known to be crucial. Recently, adaptive learning rates defined in terms of the arm-selection probabilities, obtained by solving convex optimization, have achieved improved best-of-both-worlds (BOBW) guarantees in various bandit problems. In contrast, BOBW guarantees for its computationally efficient alternative, follow-the-perturbed-leader (FTPL), remain relatively limited since its optimization-free nature ironically makes the design of adaptive, probability-dependent learning rates non-trivial. To address this challenge, we propose an adaptive learning rate for FTPL by introducing surrogate probability functions that can be computed only from the available quantities, without requiring the exact probabilities. Based on these learning rates with surrogate functions, we provide the BOBW guarantee for FTPL with Pareto perturbations for any shape parameter $α>1$, generalizing prior results restricted to specific choices of $α=2$. We further show the BOBW guarantees for FTPL with adaptive learning rates in the bandit problem with expert advices. Our approach preserves the computational simplicity of FTPL while enabling probability-dependent adaptivity, and the surrogate-based methodology may be of independent interest in other algorithmic frameworks beyond FTPL and learning rate designs.

</details>


### [163] [Effective Dimensionality as an Operator Invariant for Physics-Preserving Constraint Adaptation in Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06171)
*Cornelius Otchere,Michael Shields*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文利用Fisher信息矩阵定义物理约束模型的有效自由度(deff)，证明对于有限维核的微分算子，deff收敛于核维度且与网络架构无关；并提出子空间投影策略，将参数更新投影到预训练物理算子的零空间，实现无需重新训练即可适配新边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络(PINN)因共享参数空间同时拟合控制方程和边界条件而存在任务干扰，现有方法缺乏量化这种结构性冲突的理论工具，且边界条件变化时需从头重新训练，效率低下。

Method: 1) 利用Fisher信息矩阵定义物理约束模型的有效自由度(deff)，量化微分算子未约束的参数方向维度；2) 理论分析deff在有限/无限维核算子下的行为；3) 提出子空间投影策略，将边界适配的参数更新投影到预训练物理算子的零空间。

Result: 1) 对于有限维核算子，deff精确收敛于核维度，与网络宽度、深度或激活函数无关；2) 对于无限维核算子，deff衡量网络对核的有限维表示带宽；3) 子空间投影可在数秒至数分钟内达到与梯度微调相当的性能，而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: deff可作为先验结构诊断工具，驱动良态问题的deff为零可验证物理和边界约束已吸收网络的自由方向。子空间投影策略是实现高效边界适配的有效方法。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks inherently suffer from task interference because they rely on a shared parameter space to satisfy both governing differential equations and boundary conditions. We analyze this structural conflict using the Fisher Information Matrix to quantify the effective degrees of freedom ($d_{eff}$) in a physics-constrained model. Unlike the classical $d_{eff}$ which measures how many parameter directions are informed by data against a statistical prior, our $d_{eff}$ measures the dimension of the parameter directions unconstrained by the differential operator. For operators with finite-dimensional kernel, we show that $d_{eff}$ converges to the kernel dimension exactly, independent of network width, depth, or activation function, recasting it from a fit diagnostic into a structural invariant of the underlying continuous operator. For operators with infinite-dimensional kernel, $d_{eff}$ instead measures the network's finite-dimensional representational bandwidth for that kernel rather than recovering an integer invariant. Importantly, $d_{eff}$ also serves as an a priori structural diagnostic. Driving $d_{eff}$ of a well-posed problem to zero certifies that the physics and boundary constraints have absorbed the network's free directions. Building on this characterization, we introduce subspace projection strategies for boundary adaptation. Rather than retraining from scratch, we project parameter updates into the null space of the pre-trained physics operator so that new boundary conditions are satisfied without disturbing the learned physics. Gradient-based fine-tuning can match or exceed this but needs more wall-clock time and tuning, whereas subspace projection delivers near-equivalent quality in seconds to minutes. We validate on linear and nonlinear operators, demonstrating accurate adaptation to initial and boundary shifts and unencountered constraint types.

</details>


### [164] [Diffusion Models Observe Only Gradients: A Geometric Perspective on Score Matching Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06179)
*Naïl B. Khelifa,Richard E. Turner,Ramji Venkataramanan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明L2分数匹配误差并非衡量扩散模型采样质量的合适指标，因为分数误差可分解为梯度分量和螺线管分量，只有前者影响边缘分布动力学。作者提出了不可能性定理、更紧的KL散度上界以及一个可计算的梯度分量估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型训练使用L2分数匹配误差，但理论上缺乏该误差与生成分布质量之间的严格对应关系。作者发现L2误差可能很大而分布质量很好，这挑战了现有理论分析的基础。

Method: 利用Helmholtz-Hodge分解将分数误差分解为梯度分量和螺线管分量，分析两者对Fokker-Planck动力学的不同影响；基于此几何修正，推导不可能性定理、紧的KL上界，并提出对偶Sobolev恒等式作为梯度分量的可计算估计器。

Result: 证明了任何L2分数误差的单调函数都无法一致地作为分布散度的下界；得到了仅依赖于梯度分量的KL散度上界，比标准Girsanov界更紧；提出的梯度分量估计器与样本质量的实证相关性显著优于完整L2误差。

Conclusion: L2分数匹配误差不适用于评估扩散模型的边缘分布质量，梯度分量才是合适的度量指标。这为扩散模型的理论分析和实践评估提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models are typically trained by minimizing the $L^2$ score matching error, and standard theoretical analyses rely on this quantity to bound the sampling discrepancy between the learned and target distributions. We show the $L^2$ score error is not the right intrinsic measure of marginal distributional quality: a learned diffusion model can incur arbitrarily large $L^2$ score error while perfectly matching the target distribution. By decomposing score errors into a gradient and a solenoidal component (a Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition), we identify the geometric reason behind this: only the gradient component enters the marginal Fokker-Planck dynamics, while the solenoidal component is structurally invisible. We make this precise in three results. First, building on the corrected geometry, we prove an impossibility result: no monotone function of the $L^2$ score error can uniformly lower bound any divergence between the learned and target distributions. Second, we derive an upper bound on the Kullback-Leibler divergence that depends only on the observable gradient component of the error, tightening the standard Girsanov bound and identifying its looseness as the cost of operating on path-space rather than marginal-space dynamics. Third, we give a tractable estimator of the gradient component via a dual Sobolev identity, which is shown to empirically correlate substantially better with sample quality than the full $L^2$ error.

</details>


### [165] [Anchor PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06233)
*Benedikt Seiter,Anya Fries,Julius von Kügelgen,Jonas Peters*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Anchor PCA方法，用于多领域数据的鲁棒无监督降维，通过在解释方差与域间低秩嵌入一致性之间进行权衡，恢复最大不变子空间，并在模拟和真实气体传感器数据上优于池化PCA和最坏情况替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PCA对多领域数据池化后降维时，可能聚焦于仅在少数域中呈现高方差的虚假方向，导致在未见过的相似域上泛化能力差。需要一种能够捕获域间共享变异方向的鲁棒降维方法。

Method: 提出Anchor PCA，通过构造修正目标矩阵并在其上执行标准PCA来实现。该方法在总体解释方差与共享嵌入和域特定嵌入之间的一致性之间进行权衡，可高效求解，且能恢复最大不变子空间。

Result: 在模拟数据上，Anchor PCA成功恢复最大不变子空间；在存在时间漂移的真实气体传感器数据上，Anchor PCA在未见域上比池化PCA和最坏情况替代方法解释更多方差。

Conclusion: Anchor PCA是一种有前景的多领域数据鲁棒无监督降维方法，兼具理论保障（最大不变子空间恢复、极小极大重构解释）和实际优越性能。

Abstract: Principal component analysis (PCA) is one of the most widely used unsupervised dimension reduction techniques. We study PCA for data from multiple related domains. Since principal components generally differ across domains, one way to obtain a shared low-rank embedding is to perform PCA on the pooled data. However, this approach can focus on spurious directions that exhibit high variation in only a few domains. To find a robust embedding that still explains most variance in unseen but similar domains, we propose instead to focus on shared directions of variation. To this end, we introduce Anchor PCA which trades off overall explained variance with agreement between the shared and domain-specific low-rank embeddings. Anchor PCA amounts to PCA on a modified target matrix and thus can be solved efficiently. Moreover, we show that Anchor PCA recovers a maximal invariant subspace and admits a minimax reconstruction interpretation under bounded domain-specific covariance inflations. On simulated and real-world gas sensor data with temporal drift, we demonstrate, respectively, that Anchor PCA recovers the maximally invariant subspace and yields embeddings that explain more variance on unseen domains than the pooling baseline and a worst-case alternative. Taken together, these findings establish Anchor PCA as a promising approach to robust unsupervised dimension reduction from multi-domain data.

</details>


### [166] [Discrete Causal Representations from Heterogeneous Domains: A Bayesian Approach with Social Survey Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06288)
*Ankur Garg,Michael Stettler,Aaron Schein,Julius von Kügelgen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯方法的因果表示学习框架，针对多环境数据中的离散因果概念和未知多节点软干预问题，通过序贯蒙特卡洛采样近似多模态后验，并在社会调查数据上验证了其发现有意义的高层概念和因果关系的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果表示学习工作主要关注可识别性理论分析，但在实际应用中缺乏不确定性感知的方法，且多环境数据中的分布偏移往往源于底层因果机制的稀疏局部变化，这一特性未被充分利用。

Method: 将因果假设和可解释性需求转化为层次模型中的先验和参数选择，采用序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）采样方案近似多模态后验分布，处理离散因果概念和未知多节点软干预。

Result: 在社会调查数据（文化价值观或政治观点作为潜在因果概念、调查回答作为观测、不同国家或州作为环境）的案例研究中，模型成功推断出有意义的高层概念及其合理的因果结构。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯因果表示学习方法能够有效处理实际复杂数据中的多模态后验和不确定性，为理解社会调查等真实场景下的因果结构提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Causal representation learning aims to infer the high-level latent causal concepts that give rise to observed low-level measurements. This is particularly relevant for heterogeneous data from different environments or domains since distribution shifts often arise through sparse, localized changes in some of the underlying causal mechanisms, while other parts of the generative process remain unchanged. Whereas identifiability of causal representations has been studied extensively, practical uncertainty-aware methods and real-world use cases remain less explored. In this work, we propose a Bayesian approach to learning causal representations from multi-environment data, focusing on the case of discrete causal concepts and unknown multi-node soft interventions. To this end, we translate causal assumptions and interpretability desiderata into suitable priors and parametric choices within a hierarchical model. We then devise an inference scheme based on sequential Monte Carlo sampling to approximate the resulting multimodal posterior. We showcase our approach through case studies on social survey data, where latent causal concepts correspond to cultural values or political opinions, measurements to survey responses, and environments to different countries or states. Our model infers meaningful high-level concepts and plausible causal relations among them, demonstrating its utility for learning causal representations of complex real-world data.

</details>


### [167] [Symmetric Divergence and Normalized Similarity: A Unified Topological Framework for Representation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06342)
*Yan Wang,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的拓扑数据分析工具包，包括对称表示拓扑散度（SRTD）及其轻量变体SRTD-lite，以及归一化拓扑相似度（NTS），用于神经表示的结构性诊断和标准化评估。SRTD解决了现有RTD框架的不对称性问题，NTS通过秩相关提供有界、尺度不变的度量。实验表明该工具包能捕捉CNN功能变化并稳健映射LLM谱系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的配对拓扑散度（如RTD）存在启发式不对称性，且得分无界、依赖样本量，阻碍了跨场景的可靠基准测试。

Method: 1) 提出对称表示拓扑散度（SRTD）及其高效变体SRTD-lite，通过单一跨条形码签名整合诊断信息，解决理论不对称性；2) 提出归一化拓扑相似度（NTS），通过测量层次合并顺序的秩相关，得到有界[-1,1]的尺度不变度量。

Result: 实验表明该工具包能捕捉几何度量（如CKA）遗漏的CNN功能变化，并在距离饱和条件下稳健映射LLM谱系关系。

Conclusion: 该工具包为神经表示分析提供了严谨的拓扑感知视角，弥补了几何度量的不足，支持细粒度结构诊断和跨场景标准化评估。

Abstract: Topological Data Analysis (TDA) offers a principled, intrinsic lens for comparing neural representations. However, existing paired topological divergences (e.g., RTD) are limited by heuristic asymmetry and, more critically, unbounded scores that depend on sample size, hindering reliable cross-scenario benchmarking. To address these challenges, we develop a unified topological toolkit serving two complementary needs: fine-grained structural diagnosis and robust, standardized evaluation. First, we complete the RTD framework by introducing Symmetric Representation Topology Divergence (SRTD) and its efficient variant SRTD-lite. Beyond resolving the theoretical asymmetry of prior variants, SRTD consolidates diagnostic information into a single, comprehensive cross-barcode signature. This allows for precise localization of structural discrepancies and serves as an effective optimization objective without the overhead of dual directional computations. Second, to enable reliable benchmarking across heterogeneous settings, we propose Normalized Topological Similarity (NTS). By measuring the rank correlation of hierarchical merge orders, NTS yields a scale-invariant metric bounded between -1 and 1, effectively overcoming the scale and sample-dependence of unnormalized divergences. Experiments across synthetic and real-world deep learning settings demonstrate that our toolkit captures functional shifts in CNNs missed by geometric measures and robustly maps LLM genealogy even under distance saturation, offering a rigorous, topology-aware perspective that complements measures like CKA.

</details>


### [168] [Function-Space Priors for Bayesian Neural ODEs with Application to Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06351)
*Jaeyeong Lee,Wonmo Koo,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合函数空间正则化与概率多重打靶的贝叶斯神经常微分方程方法，用于解决AIS数据中船舶轨迹预测与不确定性量化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯神经常微分方程使用各向同性高斯权重先验，无法编码船舶动力学的平滑性和局部性等结构性质；而现有函数空间贝叶斯神经网络方法不直接适用于神经常微分方程，因为主要关注的是轨迹而非向量场。

Method: 在有限测量点上对向量场施加基于高斯过程核先验的正则化项，将核函数正则化器融入标准权重空间变分目标；结合概率多重射击方法处理长且不规则的AIS轨迹，解耦时间段推理同时保持全局一致性。

Result: 所提方法能够在不规则采样和缺失报告条件下实现轨迹的不确定性量化，并保持了轨迹预测的准确性。

Conclusion: 通过在向量场上施加核函数先验并结合概率多重射击，有效解决了贝叶斯神经常微分方程在船舶轨迹预测中先验信息不足和长序列处理困难的问题。

Abstract: Vessel trajectory prediction from Automatic Identification System (AIS) data is essential for maritime situational awareness, yet it remains challenging due to irregular sampling, missing reports, and complex dynamics. Beyond accurate point forecasts, maritime applications also demand well-calibrated uncertainty estimates for reliable decision-making. Bayesian Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) offer a principled framework for continuous-time trajectory modeling with uncertainty quantification by placing a prior over the neural vector field parameters. However, the commonly used isotropic Gaussian weight prior fails to encode informative structural properties of vessel dynamics, such as smoothness and locality. Existing function-space Bayesian neural network methods address this limitation for static mappings, but do not transfer directly to Neural ODEs, where the primary quantity of interest is the trajectory rather than the vector field itself. In principle, one could place a Gaussian process (GP) prior directly over ODE solutions, but this requires propagating distributions through a nonlinear ODE solver, which is analytically intractable. To address this challenge, we adopt a practical approach that imposes a GP-kernel-based prior directly on the vector field evaluated at a finite set of measurement points. Specifically, we augment the standard weight-space variational objective with a kernel-based regularizer that penalizes deviations of the vector field from the structure implied by a GP prior. To handle long and irregular AIS trajectories, we further combine this function-space regularization with probabilistic multiple shooting, which decouples inference across temporal segments while maintaining global consistency.

</details>


### [169] [Conformal Risk Sharing: Certified Cost Allocation with Participation Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06391)
*Ieva Kazlauskaite*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Conformal Risk Sharing框架，通过可解释的分担策略与分裂共形校准，在无分布假设下解决认证分配问题：从有限数据中设计再分配规则，为每个参与者提供可信的责任上限，并验证无人受损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体共担罕见不良事件的财务影响可减轻极端个体负担，但任何因该安排而变得更糟的参与者都有理由退出。因此需要一种可信机制，为每个代理提供未来义务的上限，且仅在参与者总损害有界时部署。

Method: 提出Conformal Risk Sharing框架，将可解释的分担策略与分裂共形校准相结合。在训练数据上调整分担强度，在保留的校准数据上生成分布自由的每个代理保证（在可交换性下有效）。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据（包括降水和能源合作社数据）上的实验证实，该框架能显著降低高风险代理的极端义务，同时控制对其他人的损害。

Conclusion: Conformal Risk Sharing为风险分担提供了分布自由、个体可验证的保证机制，有效平衡了高风险代理的减负与对其他参与者的保护。

Abstract: Sharing the financial impact of rare adverse events across a group can soften extreme individual burdens, but any participant made worse off by the arrangement has reason to leave. A credible mechanism must therefore provide each agent with a trustworthy cap on their future obligation and should be deployed only if the aggregate harm across participants is bounded. We formalise this as the Certified Allocation Problem: from finite data and without distributional assumptions, find a redistribution rule, produce obligation caps for every participant, and verify that no participant is made materially worse off. We propose Conformal Risk Sharing, which solves this problem by pairing an interpretable sharing policy with split conformal calibration. The sharing intensity is tuned on training data, while held-out calibration data produces distribution-free per-agent guarantees (valid under exchangeability). Experiments on synthetic and real-world data, including precipitation and energy-cooperative data, confirm that the framework can substantially reduce extreme obligations for high-risk agents while controlling harm to others.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Bentkus-type asymptotic e-values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06332)
*Diego Martinez-Taboada,Ben Chugg,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出Bentkus型渐近e值，解决了现有渐近e值存在的“缺失因子”问题，在事后推断和多重检验中提供了更精确的推断结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有渐近e值存在“缺失因子”导致的缩放效率低下问题，使得推断结果过于保守，需要更高效的方法。

Method: 借鉴Bentkus在2000年代提出的近最优集中不等式框架，引入Bentkus型渐近e值来消除缺失因子。

Result: Bentkus型e值成功消除了缺失因子，在事后置信区间和多重检验拒绝率方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Bentkus型渐近e值提供了更精确的推断，是现有渐近e值的更优替代方案。

Abstract: Asymptotic e-values are emerging as a powerful alternative to asymptotic p-values, particularly in post-hoc inference and multiple testing, where significance levels may be data-dependent. Existing asymptotic e-values, however, suffer from the ``missing factor,'' a scaling inefficiency resulting in overly conservative inference. Drawing on the framework of near-optimal concentration inequalities developed by Bentkus in the 2000s, we introduce Bentkus-type asymptotic e-values and prove that they successfully eliminate the missing factor. We also demonstrate both theoretically and empirically that Bentkus-type e-values consistently deliver sharper inference than existing alternatives, leading to tighter post-hoc confidence intervals and higher rejection rates in multiple testing procedures.

</details>


### [171] [Optimally taming biases in black-box models for efficient semiparametric estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06368)
*Yihong Gu,Qishuo Yin,Tianxi Cai,Jianqing Fan*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了半参数估计中黑箱机器学习模型估计 nuisance 函数时误差传播的问题。针对部分线性模型，提出了一种新的估计量，达到了 $n^{-1/2}+δ^a_μ+(δ_μ^s)^2$ 的更优速率，证明了该速率的最优性，并揭示了一阶随机误差可以被消除的原理。该方法拓展至平均处理效应等半参数线性泛函估计问题，表明流行的正交分数方法可以显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在半参数估计中，黑箱机器学习方法常被用于估计 nuisance 函数，但其估计误差如何向低维目标参数传播这一基本问题尚不清晰。双机器学习（DML）范式下误差呈乘法依赖关系，但这种乘法速率对于黑箱模型是否最优仍不明确，特别是在辅助函数 $\mathbb{E}[T|X=x]$ 无法一致估计的 regime 下。

Method: 考虑部分线性模型 $Y = μ_0(X)+T\cdotβ_0+\varepsilon$，在结构无关设定下，nuisance 函数 $μ_0$ 使用通用机器学习模型估计，含逼近误差 $δ^a_μ$ 和随机误差 $δ_μ^s$。提出一种新估计量，实现了 $n^{-1/2}+δ^a_μ+(δ_μ^s)^2$ 的速率，并建立匹配下界证明其最优性。该方法可推广至半参数线性泛函估计问题。

Result: 标准 DML 速率在辅助函数无法一致估计的 regime 下不是最优的；新估计量达到 $n^{-1/2}+δ^a_μ+(δ_μ^s)^2$ 的速率，匹配下界证明其最优性；一阶随机误差可被消除，无需额外假设；推荐欠平滑调参策略 $δ^a_μ\asymp(δ_μ^s)^2$ 而非经典偏差-方差权衡 $δ^a_μ\asymp δ_μ^s$；在温和条件下，估计量渐近正态且具有最小渐近方差。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了半参数估计中 nuisance 估计误差传播的新原理，即一阶随机误差可被消除，从而改进了现有的正交分数方法。这一结果对使用黑箱机器学习模型进行半参数推断具有重要的实践指导意义，建议采用欠平滑策略以优化估计精度。

Abstract: Modern semiparametric estimation often relies on flexible black-box machine learning methods to estimate nuisance functions, raising a fundamental question: how do nuisance estimation errors propagate into inference for low-dimensional target parameters? The dominant paradigm, exemplified by double machine learning (DML), yields error bounds in which nuisance estimation errors enter multiplicatively. While widely adopted, it remains unclear whether this multiplicative-rate dependence is optimal for black-box models. In this paper, we start by revisiting the partial linear model $Y = μ_0(X)+T\cdotβ_0+\varepsilon$ under a structure-agnostic setting, where the nuisance function $μ_0$ is estimated using a generic machine learning model, with approximation error $δ^a_μ$ and stochastic error $δ_μ^s$. We show that the standard DML rate is not optimal in the regime where the auxiliary function $\mathbb{E}[T|X=x]$ cannot be consistently estimated. We propose a new estimator for $β_0$ that achieves a sharper rate of $n^{-1/2}+δ^a_μ+(δ_μ^s)^2$ and establish a matching lower bound demonstrating its optimality. Our results reveal a new principle: the first-order stochastic error of nuisance estimation can be eliminated without imposing any additional assumptions. This also leads to a revised tuning strategy favoring under-smoothing, where $δ^a_μ\asymp(δ_μ^s)^2$, rather than the classical bias-variance trade-off $δ^a_μ\asymp δ_μ^s$. Under mild additional conditions, the estimator is asymptotically normal with minimal asymptotic variance. The proposed method extends to a broad class of semi-parametric linear functional estimation problems, including average treatment effect estimation. Our results imply that popular orthogonal score methods in semiparametric estimation with black-box nuisance learners can be substantially improved.

</details>


### [172] [Estimation of the sub-Gaussian parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06384)
*Jason Liu,Min Xu,Jinchuan Xing*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了次高斯参数（方差代理）的估计问题，提出了基于约束最大化经验累积生成函数的自然估计量，证明了其一致性和收敛速度，并给出了不同条件下的 minimax 最优性结果。最后将该估计量应用于基因本体（GO）富集分析中的置换检验 p 值构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次高斯随机变量在概率论和统计学中广泛应用，但其方差代理（次高斯参数）的估计问题尚未得到充分研究，缺乏系统性的理论分析。

Method: 基于约束最大化经验累积生成函数 L 的估计量，在不同假设条件下分析收敛速率（O_p(n^{-1/2+ε}) 到 O_p(n^{-1/2})），并建立 minimax 下界。

Result: 当 L 存在最大值点时，估计量收敛速度为 O_p(n^{-1/2+ε})；当 argmax 有界时，收敛速度提升至 O_p(n^{-1/2})。Minimax 下界显示，在一般次高斯分布类上风险下界为 Ω(1)，随假设增强在 Ω(1/log n) 到 Ω(1) 之间插值。对非次高斯分布，估计量以由尾部控制的速率发散至无穷。

Conclusion: 所提估计量在不同条件下具有最优或接近最优的收敛性质，在 GO 富集分析中可作为 peaks-over-threshold 方法的可靠替代方案。

Abstract: The sub-Gaussian parameter (also called the variance proxy) of a mean-zero random variable $X$ is defined as $ξ^2_* = \sup_{λ\in \mathbb{R}} L(λ)$ where $L(λ) = \frac{2}{λ^2} \log \mathbb{E} e^{λX}$ is a weighted cumulant generating function. Despite the ubiquity of sub-Gaussian random variables, the estimation of $ξ^2_*$ has received little attention and is not yet well understood. In this work, we study a natural estimator of $ξ^2_*$ based on constrained maximization of the empirical analogue of $L$. We prove that the estimator is consistent bound the rates of convergence under assumptions on $L$: if $L$ has an maximizer, then our bound is $O_p(n^{-1/2 + \varepsilon})$ for any $\varepsilon > 0$; if the argmax of $L$ is also bounded, then the bound improves to $O_p(n^{-1/2})$. We show that our assumptions on $L$ are necessary by proving that the minimax risk over all sub-Gaussian distributions is $Ω(1)$; imposing increasingly strong assumptions on the tail growth of $L$ yields a continuum of classes whose minimax lower bound interpolates between $Ω(1/\log n)$ and $Ω(1)$. Root-n rate is possible if we restrict to a subclass of distributions where $L$ attains its supremum in a bounded region, in which case our estimator is minimax optimal. If the underlying distribution is not sub-Gaussian, we show that our estimator goes to infinity with a divergence rate controlled by the tail of the distribution. Finally, we apply our estimator in a Gene Ontology (GO) enrichment study to construct p-values for a large-scale permutation test, showing that it can serve as a reliable alternative to the peaks-over-threshold approach, particularly in regimes where the peaks-over-threshold method is of uncertain validity.

</details>


### [173] [Trace-Class Results for MCMC Algorithms for Student-t Regression Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05672)
*Yasuyuki Hamura*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了Student-t回归模型中的MCMC算法，分析了不同先验分布下标准数据增强算法和折叠Gibbs算法的马尔可夫算子是否为迹类算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Student-t回归模型中不同MCMC算法（标准数据增强算法与折叠Gibbs算法）的马尔可夫链效率，特别是在不同先验分布设定下算子的迹类性质。

Method: 采用理论与分析的方法，分别考察在回归系数和误差方差服从不变非正常先验分布，以及参数服从正态-逆伽马分布两种情况下，标准数据增强算法和折叠Gibbs算法对应马尔可夫算子的迹类性质。

Result: 在不变非正常先验下，标准数据增强算法的马尔可夫算子不是迹类算子，而折叠Gibbs算法是迹类算子；在正态-逆伽马先验下，标准数据增强算法的马尔可夫算子是迹类算子。

Conclusion: 不同先验设定下，算法选择对马尔可夫链的收敛性质有显著影响，折叠Gibbs算法在非正常先验下具有更好的算子性质。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider MCMC algorithms for Student-$t$ regression models. We investigate the efficiency of Markov chains based on the algorithms in terms of whether trace-class results hold or not. We first consider the case where the regression coefficients and error variance follow the invariant improper prior distributions. The Markov operator associated with a standard data augmentation algorithm is not trace-class but that associated with a collpased Gibbs algorithm is trace-class. We next consider the case where the parameters follow a normal-inverse gamma distribution. In this case, the standard Markov operator is trace-class.

</details>


### [174] [Local increment inference for time-inhomogeneous drift in Gaussian processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05681)
*Yasutaka Shimizu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究高频观测下高斯过程模型中确定性漂移结构的统计推断，提出基于一阶增量的最小二乘对比估计量，证明了在弱依赖条件下的相合性和渐近正态性，并揭示了收敛速度由高斯噪声的局部粗糙度与漂移的长期信息累积结构共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高斯过程统计推断研究多关注平稳性或特定漂移形式，缺乏对广泛漂移族（包括可积型、多项式型和周期型）在统一框架下的理论分析，尤其缺乏对收敛速度如何受噪声粗糙度与漂移结构共同影响的深入理解。

Method: 基于一阶增量构建最小二乘型对比函数来估计漂移参数；对高斯噪声分量施加弱依赖条件；利用高频观测下局部粗糙度刻画信息累积，结合漂移结构的长期行为分析渐近性质。

Result: 证明了估计量的相合性和渐近正态性；揭示了收敛速度依赖于高斯噪声的局部粗糙度与漂移结构的时间累积特性；发现不同漂移密度（可积、多项式、周期）会产生不同统计机制，包括非标准收敛速率和持久/增长结构的加速收敛速率。

Conclusion: 该框架统一处理了多种漂移结构，给出了收敛速度的理论刻画，为高频观测下高斯过程模型的统计推断提供了新的理论视角和方法工具。

Abstract: We study statistical inference for deterministic drift structures in Gaussian process models under high-frequency observations.The observed process consists of a centered stationary Gaussian component combined with a broad class of time-inhomogeneous deterministic drifts. To estimate the drift parameter, we introduce a least squares-type contrast based on first-order increments. We establish consistency and asymptotic normality under weak dependence conditions on the Gaussian component. A central feature of the framework is that the rate of convergence of the estimator depends jointly on the local roughness of the Gaussian noise and the long-time information accumulation structure generated by the drift. The theory accommodates a wide range of drift families, including integrable, polynomial-type, and periodic structures. In particular, different drift densities produce qualitatively different statistical regimes, including non-standard rates of convergence and accelerated rates for persistent or growing deterministic structures.

</details>


### [175] [On statistical inference for non-linear dynamical systems evolving in their global attractor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06018)
*Dimitri Konen,Richard Nickl*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究二维周期反应扩散系统的全局吸引子，证明其满足反向Poincaré不等式，从而得到L²-Lipschitz稳定性估计，并基于此实现初始条件统计恢复与状态预测，收敛速度接近参数化速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索反应扩散系统全局吸引子上的逆向不等式与稳定性，进而为初始条件恢复和状态预测提供理论保证。

Method: 在二维周期反应扩散系统的全局吸引子A上，证明反向Poincaré不等式成立，进而推导出映射θ→u_θ(t)在A上的L²-Lipschitz稳定性估计。

Result: 1) 全局吸引子A上成立反向Poincaré不等式；2) 映射θ→u_θ(t)在A上满足L²-Lipschitz稳定性估计；3) 初始条件统计恢复和状态预测达到接近参数化的收敛速率。

Conclusion: 反应扩散系统在全局吸引子上具有逆向Poincaré不等式和Lipschitz稳定性，这为从离散测量中快速恢复初始条件和预测系统状态提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider a two-dimensional periodic reaction-diffusion system under natural conditions on the reaction function and with initial condition $θ$. We show that on the global attractor $\mathcal A$ of the resulting dynamical system $(u_θ(t):t>0)$, a reverse Poincaré inequality holds true, and that as a consequence the map $θ\mapsto u_θ(t)$ satisfies a $L^2$-Lipschitz stability estimate on $\mathcal A$ for any $t>0$ fixed. We then show that statistical recovery of an initial condition $θ$ in the attractor $\mathcal A$, as well as prediction of the states $u_θ$, is possible from discrete measurements of the system at `fast' near parametric convergence rates.

</details>


### [176] [An Adaptive Upper One-Sided Cumulative Sum Control Chart with Joint Parameter Optimization for Monitoring the Ratio of Two Normal Variables in Short Production Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06137)
*Kim Duc Tran*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向短生产运行的CUSUM-RZ⁺控制图，通过对阈值k和决策区间h进行双层联合自适应优化，解决了传统固定k设计在低变异性下边界解不可达的问题，在相关参数变化时仍表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比率Z = X/Y在统计过程控制中日益重要，但现有记忆型比率控制图多针对长生产运行设计；短生产运行的有限域方法依赖固定参考值k，该设计在给定偏移幅度下非最优，且在低变异性下会导致目标TARL₀无法达成的边界解。

Method: 提出上单侧CUSUM比率控制图CUSUM-RZ⁺，采用双层优化：内层通过有限状态马尔可夫链进行根搜索校准h(k)以满足TARL₀约束，外层通过线搜索选择k*以最小化失控TARL₁。该方法可恢复固定k设计无法处理的边界情形。

Result: 所有记忆型控制图均优于Shewhart-RZ基线；自适应设计在稳定相关下与基准持平，在Phase I到Phase II相关性升高时性能显著改善；对对称重尾分布不敏感，在污染下略偏保守；m ≥ 100个子组可使TARL₀相对偏差接近1%。

Conclusion: 自适应联合优化设计有效解决了短生产运行中比率监控的固定k局限，兼具稳健性和实际可用性，推荐的子组数建议为m ≥ 100。

Abstract: Monitoring the ratio of two correlated normal variables is increasingly important in statistical process control, since many quality characteristics are expressed in relative rather than absolute form. Memory-type ratio charts have mostly been developed for long production runs, while their finite-horizon counterparts rely on a fixed reference value $ k $ derived from a specified shift. Such fixed-$ k $ designs are not optimal at a given out-of-control magnitude and, in low-variability regimes, yield boundary solutions for which the in-control truncated average run length (TARL$ _0 $) is unattainable. This paper proposes an upper one-sided cumulative sum (CUSUM) control chart for the ratio $ Z = X/Y $ in short production runs, denoted CUSUM-RZ$ ^+ $ (RZ standing for the ratio $ Z $), with fully adaptive joint optimization of $ k $ and the decision interval $ h $. Given a target TARL$ _0 = I $ and a target shift $ τ$, a bilevel problem calibrates $ h(k) $ by inner root-finding to satisfy the TARL$ _0 $ constraint and selects $ k^* $ by outer line search to minimize the out-of-control TARL$ _1 $. Both use a finite-state Markov-chain framework with an accurate ratio approximation; the inner step recovers boundary cases that fixed-$ k $ designs cannot. The chart is assessed through matched-horizon benchmarks against Shewhart-RZ, exponentially weighted moving average (EWMA-RZ), and fixed-$ k $ CUSUM-RZ$ ^+ $ charts, Monte Carlo robustness studies, and a Phase I estimation analysis. All memory-type charts outperform the Shewhart-RZ baseline; the adaptive design matches them under stable correlation and improves appreciably when correlation rises from Phase I to Phase II. It is insensitive to symmetric heavy tails yet mildly anti-conservative under contamination, and $ m \geq 100 $ subgroups keep the TARL$ _0 $ relative bias near 1%.

</details>


### [177] [Effect of the measurement errors on one-sided Synthetic-RZ control charts for monitoring the ratio of two normal variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06149)
*Kim Duc Tran,Thi Hien Nguyen,Kim Phuc Tran*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了测量误差对用于监测两个正态随机变量比率的单侧Synthetic-RZ控制图性能的影响，采用线性协变量误差模型，通过马尔可夫链方法计算零状态和稳态平均运行长度（ARL），发现测量误差会削弱控制图的检测能力，且增加每个检测单元的测量次数并非有效的补救措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业生产中，监测两个正态分布随机变量之比具有重要实际意义，但现有研究未充分考虑测量误差对比率控制图性能的影响，且避免假设偏移幅度与测量误差无关这一限制性前提。

Method: 采用线性协变量误差模型将测量误差纳入Synthetic-RZ控制图的运行中，逐步描述模型参数从受控到失控状态的演变过程，利用马尔可夫链公式化获得运行长度特性，并通过数值实验分析影响。

Result: 测量误差会侵蚀控制图的检测能力；增加每个检测单元的测量次数并不能有效缓解测量误差对Synthetic-RZ控制图性能的不利影响。

Conclusion: 测量误差显著降低Synthetic-RZ控制图的检测性能，而增加测量次数不是有效的补救策略，需探索其他方法以减轻测量误差的影响。

Abstract: In numerous industrial production settings, keeping track of the ratio formed by two normally distributed random variables is a task of considerable practical interest. The present work examines how measurement errors influence the behaviour of a pair of one-sided Synthetic control charts designed to monitor such a ratio (referred to here as Synthetic-RZ charts), with the analysis covering both the zero-state and the steady-state average run length ($ARL$). To incorporate measurement error into the operation of these charts, we adopt a linear covariate error model. We describe, step by step, how the parameters of the underlying model evolve as the process moves from an in-control to an out-of-control state, and we deliberately avoid the restrictive premise that the observed shift magnitude is unrelated to the measurement errors. The run length characteristics of the charts are obtained by means of a Markov chain formulation. A series of numerical experiments makes clear that measurement error erodes the detection capability of the charts. A particularly useful outcome of the investigation is that collecting several measurements on each inspected unit does not constitute an efficient remedy for the adverse influence of measurement error on the performance of the Synthetic-RZ charts.

</details>


### [178] [Monitoring the Ratio of two Normal Variables using EWMA Type Control Charts in Short Production Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06161)
*Thi Hien Nguyen,Jean-Michel Masereel,Guillaume Tartare,Kim Duc Tran*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对短生产运行条件下两个正态分布变量的比值监控问题，提出了一种指数加权移动平均（EWMA）控制图（EWMA-RZ）。该方法采用Nadarajah (2020)的修正闭式密度函数，通过马尔可夫链校准控制限以达到指定的受控截断平均运行长度（TARL₀），并通过大规模因子研究评估其检测性能。结果表明，EWMA-RZ图在检测小到中等偏移（尤其|τ-1|≤0.05）时显著优于传统的Shewhart型短运行比图（ShRZ）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多工业和工程应用中，过程性能由两个正态分布质量特性的比值表征。在短生产运行条件下，传统控制图因可用检验数量少而灵敏度有限，难以有效监控此类比值。

Method: 提出EWMA控制图用于监控两个正态分布随机变量之比。采用Nadarajah (2020)的修正闭式密度函数，基于马尔可夫链表示EWMA递归过程，将控制限校准为有限检测次数I内指定的受控截断平均运行长度（TARL₀），并通过涵盖平滑常数λ、受控相关系数ρ₀、变异系数(γ_X, γ_Y)、样本量n和偏移幅度τ的大规模因子研究评估性能。

Result: 数值结果表明，提出的EWMA-RZ图在检测小到中等偏移时显著优于Tran et al. (2021)的Shewhart型短运行比图（ShRZ），尤其在|τ-1|≤0.05区间内优势明显。

Conclusion: 论文通过饮料灌装过程的实例说明了所提方法的实际应用，验证了EWMA-RZ图在短生产运行条件下对比值监控的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: In many industrial and engineering applications, process performance is characterized by the ratio of two normally distributed quality characteristics. Monitoring such ratios is particularly challenging in short production runs, where conventional control charts often suffer from limited sensitivity due to the small number of available inspections. This paper proposes an exponentially weighted moving average (EWMA) control chart for monitoring the ratio of two normally distributed random variables under short production run (SPR) conditions. The statistical distribution of the ratio is first reviewed, adopting the corrected closed-form density of Nadarajah (2020) rather than the approximation used in earlier studies. The control limit of the proposed chart is calibrated to a prescribed in-control truncated average run length (TARL$ _0 $) over a finite horizon $ I $ of inspections, using a Markov-chain representation of the EWMA recursion. The detection performance of the chart is then assessed through a large factorial study covering the smoothing constant $ λ$, the in-control correlation $ ρ_0 $, the coefficients of variation $ (γ_X, γ_Y) $, the sample size $ n $, and the magnitude of the shift $ τ$. Numerical results show that the proposed EWMA-RZ chart provides substantially better detection of small and moderate shifts than the recently developed Shewhart-type short-run ratio chart (ShRZ) of Tran et al. (2021), especially for $ |τ- 1| \le 0.05 $. An illustrative example based on a beverage filling process is included to demonstrate the practical implementation of the method.

</details>


### [179] [Unified formulas for conditional quantities and transportation functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06346)
*Roberto Vila,Eduardo Nakano,Chang C. Y. Dorea*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文基于分布导数与狄拉克δ表示，建立了一个统一的概率框架，用于分析条件量及与运输相关的量。该框架统一了条件期望、条件分布、风险函数等经典概念，并结合Copula方法导出了Wasserstein距离、双变量Gini均值差等量的新表示与界限，应用涵盖泊松、二项、负二项等计数分布的正态近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率框架对绝对连续、离散和混合分布的条件量及运输量缺乏统一处理，不同情形下推导分散且形式各异。本文旨在建立一个统一的框架，揭示条件结构、依赖建模、离散度量和最优运输之间的内在联系。

Method: (1) 利用分布导数与狄拉克δ表示建立统一概率框架，处理任意随机变量（包括绝对连续、离散和混合分布）；(2) 结合Copula方法，利用Fréchet–Hoeffding边界的极值性质与Δ-反单调函数的期望不等式，推导绝对差矩的sharp界限；(3) 导出Wasserstein距离的分位数表示、上运输泛函的表示，以及广义绝对差矩的生存函数表示与界限。

Result: (1) 获得了条件期望、条件分布、风险函数和不完全分布的统一定位机制；(2) 导出了绝对差矩在固定边际下的sharp界；(3) 获得了Wasserstein距离及上运输泛函的分位数表示；(4) 获得了双变量Gini均值差及双变量Gini指数的新表示；(5) 得到了泊松、二项、负二项标准化计数分布的正态近似中Wasserstein型泛函的显式分位数表示。

Conclusion: 本文建立的统一框架在条件结构、依赖建模、离散度量、正态近似与最优运输之间建立了显式联系，为概率论与数理统计中的若干基本构造提供了统一的视角。

Abstract: This paper develops a unified probabilistic framework based on distributional derivatives and Dirac delta representations for the analysis of conditional and transportation-related quantities. General identities are established for arbitrary random variables, encompassing absolutely continuous, discrete, and mixed distributions. The proposed approach yields unified formulas for conditional expectations, conditional distributions, hazard functions, and improper distributions, revealing a common localization mechanism underlying these classical concepts.
  The framework is further combined with copula methods to investigate transportation and dispersion functionals through dependence structures. Exploiting the extremal properties of the Fréchet--Hoeffding bounds together with expectation inequalities induced by $Δ$-antitonic functions, sharp bounds are derived for absolute difference moments under fixed marginals. These results lead to concise derivations of quantile representations for the Wasserstein distance and a corresponding upper transportation functional, as well as survival-function representations and bounds for generalized absolute difference moments. As a particular case, new representations are obtained for the bivariate Gini mean difference and the associated bivariate Gini index.
  Applications are given to Wasserstein-type functionals arising in the normal approximation of standardized counting distributions, including Poisson, Binomial, and Negative Binomial models, for which explicit quantile representations are derived. Overall, the results establish explicit links among conditional structures, dependence modeling, dispersion measures, normal approximation, and optimal transport, providing a unified perspective on several fundamental constructions in probability and mathematical statistics.

</details>


### [180] [How abundant are good interpolators?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06469)
*August Y. Chen,Ahmed El Alaoui*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 在高维比例渐近框架下，研究均匀随机选取的插值分类器集合中分类器的泛化误差大偏差原理，发现几乎所有插值分类器具有相近的泛化性能，而梯度下降和线性规划等方法找到的插值器显著优于绝大多数随机插值器，表现出良性过拟合现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
