Leveraging these results, we derive non-asymptotic uniform convergence rates for smooth DNN estimators across multiple statistical contexts, including Huber, least-squares, quantile, and logistic regression. We prove that smooth DNNs can mitigate the {curse of dimensionality} in uniform convergence by adaptively exploiting the low-dimensional hierarchical composition structure of the target function. Supported by both simulation studies and a real-world application, our results position smooth DNNs as a theoretically grounded and practically viable alternative to ReLU networks for statistical learning tasks requiring uniform guarantees.

</details>


### [216] [Sharp First-Order Lower Bounds for Higher-Order Smooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05438)
*Dongruo Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在高阶光滑非凸优化中，一阶确定性oracle复杂度存在维度无关的匹配下界：Lipschitz Hessian下为Ω(ε^{-7/4})，Lipschitz三阶导数下为Ω(ε^{-5/3})，通过一种“块链”机制构造困难实例，填补了此前仅有上界而无下界的理论空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典非凸优化在仅Lipschitz梯度条件下ε^{-2}的复杂度下界已被证明是最优的，但当目标函数具有更高阶光滑性（如Lipschitz Hessian或三阶导数）时，虽然已有加速的一阶方法上界（ε^{-7/4}和ε^{-5/3}），但匹配的下界一直缺失，这一理论空白亟待填补。

Method: 提出一种“块链”（block-chain）机制构造困难实例：通过块状结构强制oracle按块顺序逐步揭示信息，同时保持标量困难实例所需的光滑性结构，使得一阶oracle无法利用高阶光滑性加速超越所证下界。

Result: 证明了维度无关的匹配下界：Lipschitz Hessian情形下为Ω(ε^{-7/4})，三阶光滑情形下为Ω(ε^{-5/3})，对所有有限光滑阶数均成立。

Conclusion: 完全解决了高阶光滑非凸优化中一阶oracle复杂度的上下界匹配问题，确认了已知加速上界的最优性；同时提及构造过程借助了ChatGPT 5.5 Pro辅助并经过作者验证。

Abstract: We study the deterministic first-order oracle complexity of finding \(ε\)-stationary points in smooth nonconvex optimization when the objective satisfies higher-order smoothness assumptions. While the classical \(ε^{-2}\) rate is optimal under only Lipschitz gradients, higher-order smoothness leads to accelerated first-order upper bounds, most notably the \(ε^{-7/4}\) rate under Lipschitz Hessians and the \(ε^{-5/3}\) rate under Lipschitz third derivatives. The matching lower bounds, however, have remained open. We resolve this gap by proving a new dimension-free first-order lower bound for higher-order smooth nonconvex functions, valid for every finite smoothness order. In particular, our construction gives a matching \(Ω(ε^{-7/4})\) lower bound in the Hessian-Lipschitz case and a matching \(Ω(ε^{-5/3})\) lower bound in the third-order-smooth regime. The hard instance is based on a \emph{block-chain} mechanism that enforces blockwise oracle revelation while preserving the smoothness structure needed for the scalar hard instance. The lower-bound construction was discovered with the assistance of ChatGPT 5.5 Pro and subsequently verified by the authors.

</details>


### [217] [Zero-Copy Semantic Contagion: An In-Memory Streaming Architecture for Evolving Attention Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05733)
*Kabir Murjani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种异构Rust-Python流式架构，通过连续时间图注意力机制捕获跨公司信息传播，基于新闻文本驱动的多元神经霍克斯过程实现端到端延迟约13ms，在FNSPID语料库上相较于随机基线提升1.70倍精度，并证实图拓扑是跨公司信号的唯一驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单资产（per-ticker）预测模型无法捕获跨公司信息传播，例如台湾晶圆厂供应链中断这类事件在苹果股价变动之前不会被单资产模型感知，亟需一种能动态建模跨公司注意力传播的架构。

Method: 异构Rust-Python流式架构：1）零拷贝Rust解析器以~100ns解析新闻记录、~1.2μs扫描目标股票池；2）多元神经霍克斯过程，每节点配备连续时间LSTM状态与双线性潜在投影以传播定向激励；3）自适应剪枝规则约束动态邻域更新的计算成本。

Result: 单CPU上端到端延迟~13ms；在FNSPID语料库（47只股票、638篇文章）一个月时间留出集上，在90分位数次日回报阈值下，精度较随机基线提升1.70倍，较同行业基线提升3.36倍；移除图拓扑后精度降为零。

Conclusion: 动态注意力网络是架构中跨公司信号的唯一驱动来源，验证了图拓扑在跨资产金融预测中的必要性。

Abstract: Per-ticker forecasting models dominate financial time-series work yet remain blind to cross-company propagation: a foundry disruption in Taiwan does not register in a single-asset model until Apple's own price has already moved. To address this limitation, we introduce a heterogeneous Rust-Python streaming architecture that maps cross-company attention as a continuous-time graph driven directly from text. We show that on the ingestion side, a zero-copy Rust edge parses news records in $\sim$100 ns and scans the target equity universe in $\sim$1.2 $μ$s. On the inference end, a multivariate Neural Hawkes Process featuring per-node continuous-time LSTM states and a bilinear latent projection propagates directed excitation, while an adaptive pruning rule bounds the computational cost of dynamic neighborhood updates. Combining these stages, we demonstrate an end-to-end processing latency of $\sim$13 ms per incoming news record on a single commodity CPU. Evaluated on a one-month temporal holdout of the FNSPID corpus (638 articles across 47 tickers), the system delivers a $1.70\times$ precision lift over random at the 90th-percentile next-day return threshold, and $3.36\times$ over a same-sector baseline. Crucially, removing the graph topology collapses precision to zero, confirming that the dynamic attention network is the sole driver of cross-company signal in this architecture.

</details>


### [218] [Towards Unified and Data-Efficient Prognostics and Health Management with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05481)
*Raffael Theiler,Lev Telyatnikov,Leandro Von Krannichfeldt,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用表格基础模型（Tabular Foundation Models）结合上下文学习（in-context learning）来处理工业时间序列数据，解决PHM任务中的数据碎片化、弱标注等问题。通过将单元级信号转换为表格形式，模型在诊断和预后任务上表现优异，尤其在低数据场景下竞争力强，平均排名优于序列模型、Transformer和梯度提升树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业PHM数据通常碎片化、部分观测、标签稀疏，难以直接使用监督学习；现有时间序列基础模型多面向预测任务，要求长序列、规则采样，不适应PHM实际数据特点。因此需要一种更通用、高效且可复用的建模方法。

Method: 将原始单元级信号转换为表格行（tabular rows），利用表格基础模型（如PFN-based模型）结合上下文学习进行PHM任务的诊断与预后；与序列模型、Transformer基线、梯度提升树在统一评估协议下进行对比实验。

Result: 表格基础模型在预后和诊断任务上取得了最佳平均排名；PFN模型在低数据场景下具有竞争力；表格表示能够保留时序上下文信息；模型性能依赖于代表性上下文构建和子采样策略。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型为异构PHM问题提供了一种实用且通用的接口，能够有效应对工业时间序列数据中的挑战。

Abstract: Data-driven Prognostics and Health Management (PHM) uses time-varying condition-monitoring data to diagnose system states and estimate remaining useful life in engineered assets. These tasks are central to maintenance planning, but industrial PHM data are often fragmented, partially observed, and poorly labeled, which hinders supervised learning. Foundation models offer a route toward reusable predictive systems, yet most time-series foundation models are designed for forecasting and assume long, coherent, regularly sampled sequences. To address this gap, we propose a framework for applying Tabular Foundation Models to industrial time series using in-context learning, and we evaluate them on a variety of PHM tasks. By converting raw unit-level signals into tabular rows, we show that these models perform well across multiple tasks - including prognostics, and diagnostics - and are highly data efficient. We compare them directly with sequence models, transformer baselines, and gradient-boosted trees under a common evaluation protocol. The results indicate that tabular foundation models achieve the best average ranks across prognostic and diagnostic tasks. Our findings further show that PFN-based models are competitive in low-data regimes, that temporal context can be preserved in the tabular representation, and that performance depends on representative context construction under subsampling. These results demonstrate that tabular foundation models provide a practical and general interface for heterogeneous PHM problems.

</details>


### [219] [Causal Longitudinal Prior-Fitted Networks for Counterfactual Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05797)
*Amirhossein Zare,Amirhessam Zare,Herlock Rahimi,Reza Salarikia,Mohammad Kashkooli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CausalLongPFN，一种基于预训练-冻结范式的时间序列因果预测模型，在合成因果时序数据上预训练后，无需梯度更新或倾向性模型拟合即可进行多步反事实预测，在癌症、HIV等基准上达到领域专门训练模型的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向治疗决策需要预测未来治疗序列下的潜在结局，但面临时变混杂、患者异质性、领域数据有限等挑战，现有方法需要为每个队列或模拟器训练新模型，成本高昂且不实用。

Method: 基于Prior-Fitted Networks范式，在广义先验分布的合成时序结构因果模型上预训练，使模型学会上下文中的因果预测；测试时冻结模型，通过条件支持轨迹、查询历史和未来治疗序列，递归应用单步预测器得到多步预测分布。

Result: 在分支癌症、HIV、华法林三个具有真实反事实标签的基准上，与领域专门训练的基线方法竞争力相当；在MIMIC-III ICU轨迹的事实预测任务上表现强劲。

Conclusion: 广泛的合成因果预训练可以提供一种有用的冻结替代方案，适用于重复领域特定训练成本高昂或不切实际的场景。

Abstract: Longitudinal treatment decisions require predicting potential outcomes under future treatment sequences in the presence of time-varying confounding, heterogeneous patient dynamics, and limited domain-specific data. Existing longitudinal causal estimators typically train a new model for each cohort or simulator. We introduce Causal Longitudinal Prior-Fitted Networks (CausalLongPFN), a prior-fitted in-context predictor for longitudinal causal prediction. The model is pretrained entirely on synthetic episodes sampled from a broad prior over temporal structural causal models, exposing it to treatment-confounder feedback, latent heterogeneity, nonlinear state evolution, delayed effects, and cumulative treatment responses. At test time, CausalLongPFN is frozen: it conditions on support trajectories, a query history, and a proposed future treatment sequence, and returns a predictive distribution over future outcomes without gradient updates or propensity-model fitting. Multi-step predictions are obtained by recursively applying the one-step predictor under the specified treatment sequence. We evaluate on branchable cancer, HIV, and warfarin benchmarks with ground-truth counterfactual labels, and on factual-only rolling-origin prediction in MIMIC-III ICU trajectories. CausalLongPFN is competitive with domain-trained longitudinal baselines on counterfactual benchmarks and performs strongly on factual MIMIC-III prediction, suggesting that broad synthetic causal pretraining can provide a useful frozen alternative when repeated domain-specific training is costly or impractical.

</details>


### [220] [Learned Subspace Compression for Communication-Efficient Pipeline Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05484)
*Paul Janson,Edouard Oyallon,Eugene Belilovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MAPL方法，通过流形约束的learnable正交投影实现流水线并行中的激活压缩，在低带宽网络中训练大语言模型时大幅减少通信开销，同时保持模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流水线并行中跨阶段的激活通信在低带宽网络上成为主要瓶颈；现有固定正交投影方法性能下降明显且需要非标准约束；学习低秩投影又难以保持正交性约束。

Method: 1) 将跨阶段压缩建模为Stiefel流形（正交矩阵）约束下的可学习正交投影，通过流形约束最速下降实现每阶段自适应压缩子空间学习；2) 引入分阶段因子化anchor embeddings用于全秩激活重建，通信开销可忽略；3) 结合残差向量量化与流式码本同步协议，摊销字典通信成本。

Result: 在LLaMA 150M到1B参数模型上，MAPL可轻松应用于现有流水线，实现高压缩比且性能损失可忽略，相比Subspace Networks在性能-压缩权衡上显著提升。

Conclusion: MAPL通过显式的流形约束解决了learnable正交投影的学习问题，相比固定投影方法提供了更优的性能-压缩tradeoff，是一种实用的流水线并行通信压缩方案。

Abstract: Pipeline parallelism enables training of large language models that exceed single-device memory, yet inter-stage activation communication becomes the dominant bottleneck when trained on low-bandwidth networks. Recent work in this area has proposed using fixed orthogonal projections to compress activations. However, this still results in a significant performance degradation and requires a number of non-standard adaptations to constrain the optimization. A natural alternative is to learn a low rank projection for each pipeline stage, however maintaining the necessary orthogonality of these projectors during training remains a challenge. We present Manifold Aware Projection Learning (MAPL), a method that treats inter-stage compression as a learnable orthogonal projection under explicit Stiefel manifold (orthogonal matrices) constraints. Rather than prescribing a fixed global subspace, MAPL lets each pipeline stage discover and continuously adapt its own task-optimal compression subspace via manifold-constrained steepest descent. To recover token-specific signals at stage boundaries, we introduce per-stage factorized anchor embeddings that allow for full-rank activation reconstruction with negligible communication overhead. We further show that we can incorporate residual vector quantization after projection with a streaming codebook synchronization protocol that amortizes dictionary communication. Across LLaMA models from 150M to 1B parameters we show that MAPL can be easily applied to the existing pipeline and can achieve high compression with neglibile performance degradation with a drastically improved tradeoffs in performance vs. compression compared to Subspace Networks.

</details>


### [221] [Dead Directions: Geometric Singular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05957)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过“死方向”（dead direction）这一基本概念，将奇异学习理论（singular learning theory）和信息几何（information geometry）统一起来。该方向既是Fisher度规退化的单位向量，也是解析奇点集的切向量，具有明确的KL阶数。研究表明，在原始参数坐标下，Fisher曲率沿死方向趋近奇点的衰减率可以恢复KL阶数，并进一步推导出Watanabe现实对数规范阈值（RLCT）的单方向贡献、多重交叉、多重度、奇异涨落等不变量。该分析被扩展到深度网络：通过多层K-FAC分解，Fisher块被分解为激活侧和梯度侧衰减率的乘积，并验证了残差流、层归一化、注意力等现代网络原语。此外，本文建立了梯度流下商空间Θ/G的等价定理，证明了SGD适用而标准Adam不适用，并提出了G-等变Adam族预条件子（DDCAdam）。最终，该方法只需单个检查点的前向和后向传播即可直接读取Watanabe三元组(λ, m, ν)，无需后验采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异学习理论和信息几何虽然研究相同的参数空间，但使用不同的词汇体系：前者在消解坐标下计算贝叶斯不变量，后者在原始坐标下工作但假设非退化性，而过参数化模型通常违反该假设。本文旨在通过一个统一的基本概念桥接这两个领域。

Method: 定义“死方向”为Fisher度规退化的单位向量（等价于解析奇点集的切向量），其在两个理论中对应同一向量。核心创新：在原始参数坐标下，无需Hironaka消解，通过Fisher曲率沿死方向趋近奇点的衰减率恢复KL阶数。通过光滑纤维上的选择规则将此衰减率转化为Watanabe的RLCT单方向贡献，并扩展至多分量交叉、多重度m、奇异涨落ν、先验RLCT偏移和退火后验。对于深度网络，采用多层K-FAC分解将Fisher块分解为激活侧和梯度侧衰减率乘积，并验证残差流、层归一化、注意力机制。提出商空间Θ/G下的等价定理，证明SGD适用而标准Adam不适用，构造DDCAdam预条件子。

Result: 成功将死方向的KL阶数恢复为原始参数坐标下Fisher曲率的衰减率；建立了从衰减率到Watanabe三元素(λ, m, ν)的完整映射；在深度网络中实现了Fisher块的激活-梯度双侧重构；证明了SGD在商空间下的适用性及Adam的不适用性，并提出等变替代方案DDCAdam；实现了从单个检查点前向和后向传播直接读取奇异几何不变量。

Conclusion: 本文通过死方向这一基本概念成功桥接了奇异学习理论和信息几何，提供了一种在原始参数坐标下处理奇异几何的工具，能够在无后验采样的情况下从单个检查点读取Watanabe不变量，并给出了每架构的闭式预测，为深度学习的几何理解开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Singular learning theory and information geometry have studied the same parameter spaces in mostly separate vocabularies: the former computes Bayesian invariants in resolved coordinates, the latter works in original coordinates under a non-degeneracy assumption that overparameterised models routinely violate. We bridge them through one primitive, the dead direction: a unit vector along which the Fisher metric degenerates, equivalently a tangent to the analytic singular set with a definite KL order, set by how fast the KL divergence vanishes. The two readings name the same vector; our central move shows its KL order is recoverable as the decay rate of the directional Fisher curvature approaching the singularity, in original parameter coordinates and without a Hironaka resolution. A selection rule on smooth fibres translates this rate into Watanabe's single-direction contribution to the real log canonical threshold, and we extend the recovery to multi-component crossings, multiplicity $m$, the singular fluctuation $ν$ (universal in the KL order for 1D directions), prior-RLCT shifts, and tempered posteriors. We then lift this rate to a deep network: a multi-layer K-FAC factorisation writes each Fisher block as a product of activation- and gradient-side rates with a duality between them, instantiated at modern-network primitives (residual streams, layer normalisation, attention). A quotient theorem carries the rate to the gauge quotient $Θ/G$ under gradient flow on a $G$-invariant metric; SGD qualifies, standard Adam does not, and we construct a $G$-equivariant Adam-family preconditioner (DDCAdam) that does. The bridge yields a parameter-coordinate handle on singular geometry, closed-form per-architecture predictions, and a trajectory-rate readout of Watanabe's triple $(λ, m, ν)$ from one checkpoint's forward and backward passes, without posterior sampling.

</details>


### [222] [LEVANTE-bench: Multi-Scale Comparison of VLMs to Children Using Cognitive Tasks (or, "Is Your VLM Smarter Than a 5th Grader?")](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05497)
*Alvin Wei Ming Tan,David Cardinal,Tania Lorido-Botran,Laura Bravo-Sanchez,Sunny Yu,Michael C. Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LEVANTE-bench基准测试，用于评估视觉语言模型(VLM)与5-12岁儿童在六项认知任务上的一致性。研究发现，模型与儿童的对齐在不同尺度上存在异质性：能力更强的模型在任务和项目层面与人类对齐更好，但在错误分布匹配上差异显著，且小模型在某些任务上更能匹配年幼儿童的错误模式。即使是表现最好的VLM也在矩阵推理和心理旋转任务上表现不佳，表明当前VLM架构与儿童认知能力仅部分对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类经验本质上是多模态的，视觉语言模型(VLM)有潜力模拟人类认知的发展过程。然而，缺乏系统比较VLM与不同年龄、任务和人群中儿童认知发展的工具，因此需要建立相应的基准测试来评估这种对齐程度。

Method: 基于LEVANTE项目（涵盖三个国家、1547名5-12岁儿童）的开放任务和数据，构建LEVANTE-bench基准测试，在六项认知任务上系统评估VLM。从多个尺度比较模型：整体准确率、任务和项目级别与儿童的对齐程度、以及试次级别错误分布的匹配度。

Result: 对齐在不同尺度上呈现异质性：任务和项目层面，更强的模型与人类对齐更好；但错误分布匹配度因任务而异，某些任务上小模型与年幼儿童的错误模式更匹配。即使最佳VLM在矩阵推理和心理旋转任务上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 当前VLM架构与儿童认知能力仅部分对齐，尚不能全面模拟人类认知发展。

Abstract: Given the inherently multimodal nature of human experience, vision-language models (VLMs) hold substantial promise for modeling human cognition as it grows and develops with experience. Realizing their potential requires tools for comparing VLMs with human cognitive development across tasks, ages, and populations. We present LEVANTE-bench, a benchmark based on tasks and data from the Learning Variability Network (LEVANTE), which distributes open-source tasks and data measuring children's cognition across languages and cultures. In LEVANTE-bench, we systematically assess VLMs on six tasks, comparing their alignment with children aged 5-12 ($N$ = 1547) across three countries. We compare models at multiple scales, assessing their overall accuracy, their task- and item-level alignment with children, and how well they match children's trial-level error distributions. Alignment was heterogeneous across scales: at the level of tasks and items, more capable models aligned better with humans. However, match to human error distributions varied widely across tasks, and for several tasks, smaller models matched younger children's errors better. In addition, even the best-performing VLMs struggled on matrix reasoning and mental rotation tasks. Thus, current VLM architectures align only partially with the cognitive abilities of children.

</details>


### [223] [Adaptive state-action abstractions via rate-distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06123)
*Fernando E. Rosas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种动态调整强化学习中状态-动作抽象粒度的原则：当学习误差与抽象误差相当时，应细化抽象。通过率失真原理构建软状态-动作抽象，并利用性能证书（分解为Bellman残差与双模拟度量）指导切换策略，在表格型任务中以高压缩比实现近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽有多技术构建简化版本任务，但缺乏动态调整任务抽象粒度的通用原则。受婴儿学步启发（先解决粗粒度问题，待收益递减时再细化），需要一种能自动决定何时细化抽象的方法。

Method: 提出基于性能证书的原则：将值误差分解为学习误差界（Bellman残差）和抽象误差界（双模拟度量），当二者可比时触发抽象细化。通过率失真原理构建可沿状态和动作轴连续调整分辨率的软状态-动作抽象，实现该切换策略。

Result: 在多种表格型设置中验证，表明在状态和动作信息的高损耗压缩下仍能实现近最优性能。

Conclusion: 提出的动态抽象细化原则有效，通过率失真软抽象结合性能证书，可在高压缩比下保持近最优性能，为自适应任务抽象提供了通用原则。

Abstract: When learning to walk, infants seem to address a coarse version of the problem first - stay upright, reach the caregiver - and refine it only when further practice at that resolution stops paying off. Reinforcement learning offers multiple techniques for building simple versions of complex tasks, but lacks general principles for how to dynamically adjust the granularity of these abstractions during learning. This paper proposes one such principle: refine the abstraction as soon as the learning error within it becomes comparable to the error induced by the abstraction itself. Here, we investigate one way of formalising this principle via a performance certificate that decomposes value error into two terms: a learning error bound captured by a Bellman residual, and an abstraction error bound given by a bisimulation metric. The resulting switching strategy is implemented by soft state-action abstractions built from rate-distortion principles, whose resolution along state and action axes can be continuously adjusted. We validate this construction in a range of tabular settings, showing that near-optimal performance can be achieved under substantial lossy compression of state and action information.

</details>


### [224] [Dominant-Layer ZO: A Single Layer Dominates Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05516)
*Wanhao Yu,Ziyan Wang,Zheng Wang,Abeer Matar Almalky,Yihang Zuo,Shuteng Niu,Sen Lin,Adnan Siraj Rakin,Deliang Fan,Li Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现零阶优化微调大语言模型时，模型性能主要由单个解码层主导，且该层可以通过推理时的激活异常值简单识别。仅微调该主导层即可匹配甚至超越全模型零阶微调，并带来高达4.52倍的训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化（ZO）在LLM微调中具有内存高效的优点，但各层对微调效果的贡献分布尚不明确。本文旨在揭示ZO微调中不同层的作用差异，并探索能否仅微调关键层来提升效率。

Method: 通过系统实验发现ZO微调中单个解码层主导性能；通过激活异常值分析在训练前识别该主导层；分析扰动传播机制解释该现象：主导层兼具高扰动敏感性和残差流中的早期位置，使扰动效应能在后续层传播累积。

Result: 在LLaMA2-7B和Qwen3-8B上，仅微调主导层的ZO方法在九个基准上平均性能优于全模型MeZO和基于LoRA的ZO微调，同时实现最高4.52倍训练加速。

Conclusion: ZO微调的性能主要由单个解码层贡献，该层具有任务无关但模型特定的特性，可通过激活异常值在训练前识别。这一发现为高效LLM微调提供了新的理论理解和实用方法。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization enables memory-efficient fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) using only forward passes, but it remains unclear how useful adaptation is distributed across layers. In this work, we reveal a surprising phenomenon: ZO fine-tuning is sharply dominated by a single decoding layer. Across multiple LLM families and downstream tasks, fine-tuning this dominant layer alone consistently matches or even exceeds full-model ZO fine-tuning. We further show that the dominant layer is task-agnostic but model-specific, and can be identified before training through a simple inference-only analysis of activation outliers. Specifically, the dominant layer consistently aligns with the first activation-outlier layer in the pre-trained model. To explain this phenomenon, we analyze how perturbation effects propagate under ZO optimization. We find that the dominant layer combines two key properties: high perturbation sensitivity and early placement in the residual stream, allowing perturbation-induced effects to propagate and accumulate through remaining subsequent decoding layers. As a result, this layer produces disproportionately strong and stable optimization signals under forward-only updates. Extensive experiments on LLaMA2-7B and Qwen3-8B across nine benchmarks show that dominant-layer ZO fine-tuning improves average performance over full-model MeZO and LoRA-based ZO fine-tuning while achieving up to 4.52$\times$ training speedup.

</details>


### [225] [PAC-Bayesian Adversarially Robust Generalization for Message Passing Graph Neural Networks: A Sensitivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06293)
*Ziling Liang,Xinping Yi,Qingsong Wen,Shi Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了敏感性感知的PAC-Bayesian框架到消息传递图神经网络（MPGNNs），通过利用Jacobian矩阵的低秩性质（秩≤K，K为类别数）构造各向异性高斯后验，将鲁棒泛化界从隐藏宽度依赖优化为类别数K依赖，得到了更紧的对抗鲁棒泛化界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN对抗鲁棒泛化分析依赖各向同性高斯后验，在完整参数空间控制权重扰动，无法捕获异构参数敏感性，且受隐藏宽度依赖的复杂度项制约，导致泛化界不够紧。

Method: 1) 推导输出关于权重参数的Jacobian矩阵，量化不同参数块的敏感性；2) 利用K类图分类中Jacobian矩阵秩≤K的性质，构造Jacobian对齐的敏感性矩阵；3) 使用具有优化协方差的各向异性高斯后验，紧致地上界KL散度。

Result: 将主导维度因子从隐藏宽度依赖项缩减为类别数K，得到了更紧的MPGNNs鲁棒泛化保证。

Conclusion: 本文分析能够为MPGNNs提供更紧的鲁棒泛化界，从而指导其对抗鲁棒性的设计增强。

Abstract: Whilst the vulnerability of graph neural networks (GNNs) to adversarial attacks poses a critical threat to graph representation learning, the understanding of the robust generalization behavior remains a fundamental challenge in the adversarial setting. Recently, PAC-Bayesian margin-based generalization analysis substantially advances this line of research by providing a flexible and data-dependent analytical framework. However, existing robust analyses often rely on isotropic Gaussian posteriors and control weight perturbations in the full parameter space, which limits the ability to capture heterogeneous parameter sensitivity yet hinges on hidden-width-dependent complexity terms, resulting in not-tight-enough generalization bounds. In this paper, we extend a recently proposed sensitivity-aware PAC-Bayesian framework from deep neural networks to message passing GNNs (MPGNNs) and derive a tighter robust generalization bound in the adversarial setting. Specifically, we first quantify how sensitive the perturbations across different parameter blocks are to the network outputs by deriving the output Jacobians with respect to the weight parameters. Exploiting the fact that these Jacobian matrices have rank at most $K$ in $K$-class graph classification, we then construct Jacobian-aligned sensitivity matrices and use anisotropic Gaussian posteriors with optimized covariances to upper bound the KL divergence in a tight way. Notably, by refining the spectral-norm dependence on the learned weights and reducing the leading dimension factor from hidden-width-dependent terms to the number of classes $K$, our analysis yields much tighter robust generalization guarantees for MPGNNs, thereby guiding their designs to enhance adversarial robustness.

</details>


### [226] [What Objects Enable, Not What They Are: Functional Latent Spaces for Affordance Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05533)
*Rohan Siva,Neel P. Bhatt,Yunhao Yang,Seoyoung Lee,Nishant Gadde,Christian Ellis,Alvaro Velasquez,Zhangyang Wang,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出A4D，通过将视觉观测映射到以功能（affordance）为核心的共享潜空间，替代传统基于外观的推理，实现机器人规划中对任务相关功能性的推理，并引入affordance发现机制处理未见场景。A4D在多项规划任务中显著优于现有方法，推理速度提升100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人规划系统依赖基于外观的推理，将视觉观测编码到围绕物体外观组织的潜空间中，无法捕捉任务相关的功能性（如物体是否"可移动"），导致难以泛化到新的机器人-物体交互场景。本文旨在通过affordance推理解决这一泛化性局限。

Method: 提出A4D框架：1) 将视觉观测映射到以affordance（如"可移动"）结构化的共享潜空间；2) 通过测量观测在该功能潜空间中的投影与affordance原型的邻近度推断功能属性；3) 引入affordance发现机制，利用潜空间中的邻近度量化推理不确定性，选择性地扩展潜空间以处理未见场景。

Result: A4D在现有affordance上推理准确率达94%，超SOTA 15+%；新affordance推理准确率从70%提升至90%+，且仅需不到10%原始训练数据；推理速度提升100倍。

Conclusion: A4D通过功能驱动的潜空间表示和affordance发现机制，有效解决了基于外观推理的泛化瓶颈，实现了高效、准确的机器人规划中功能性推理。

Abstract: Existing robot planning systems rely on appearance-based reasoning, where visual observations are encoded into latent spaces organized around object appearances (e.g., recognizing a "cart" based on how it looks). However, planning requires reasoning about task-relevant functionalities of objects (e.g., whether an object is "movable"), which appearance-based latent spaces do not capture. As a result, existing approaches struggle to generalize to novel robot-object interactions. We address this limited generalizability through affordance reasoning, enabling planning based on task-relevant object functionalities instead of appearance alone. We introduce A4D, which maps visual observations into a shared latent space structured around affordances (e.g., "movable"). By projecting visual observations into this functional latent space and measuring their proximity to affordances, A4D infers functionalities relevant to the observed object. Furthermore, we introduce an affordance discovery mechanism that expands the latent space to handle unseen scenarios where existing affordances are insufficient. A4D uses proximity in the functional latent space to quantify uncertainty in affordance inference and selectively triggers affordance discovery. We evaluate A4D across several planning tasks involving diverse and unseen affordances. A4D achieves 94% inference accuracy on existing affordances outperforming state-of-the-art approaches by over 15% points, improves new-affordance inference accuracy from 70% to over 90% with fewer than 10% of the original training data, and enables 100x faster inference. Code, videos, and data available at: https://A4Dance-reasoning.github.io.

</details>


### [227] [Efficient Mean Curvature Computation on High-Dimensional Data Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06329)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对高维数据局部平均曲率计算中$O(m^4)$的昂贵成本，提出两项互补贡献：一是利用代数恒等式将每点代价降至$O(m^2)$，二是利用截断SVD和零空间解析近似将总代价降至$O(k^2 m + k m p^2)$，实验表明加速50-300倍且精度损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据集的局部平均曲率估计是几何感知机器学习（如MCBP方法）的关键，但朴素实现基于k近邻的局部形状算子构造，每点需显式构建一个$m \times m$的矩阵$H$，计算迹形式的代价高达$O(m^4)$，当特征数$m$较大时完全不可行。因此需要大幅降低计算复杂度。

Method: 1) 利用协方差矩阵特征向量的正交性和迹算子的循环性，推导精确代数恒等式，消除矩阵$H$的显式构建，使每点代价降至$O(m^2)$。2) 针对局部协方差矩阵秩最多$k-1 \ll m$的特点，用$k \times m$中心化数据矩阵的截断SVD替代完整特征分解（$O(k^2 m)$），并基于Haar测度下零空间特征向量外积的期望值，导出其贡献的解析近似。最终总代价为$O(k^2 m + k m p^2)$，其中$p=k-1$。

Result: 在真实数据集上，相比原始实现获得50-300倍加速；快速估计器替代原始版本时精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 所提方法使局部曲率估计变得可扩展且数据驱动，将曲率确立为从经典到现代深度学习管道等多种机器学习任务中实用的几何特征。

Abstract: Estimating local mean curvature at each point of a high-dimensional dataset is a key ingredient of geometry-aware machine learning algorithms, such as the Mean Curvature Boundary Points (MCBP) method. The naive implementation of this computation, based on a local shape operator approximated from k-nearest neighbor patches, involves an explicit construction of a matrix $H$ whose trace form yields an $O(m^4)$ cost per point, rendering the approach intractable for datasets with more than a few dozen features. This paper introduces two complementary contributions that together reduce this cost by several orders of magnitude. The first contribution is an exact algebraic identity. This identity, derived from the orthogonality of the eigenvectors of the covariance matrix and the cyclicity of the trace operator, eliminates $H$ entirely and reduces the per-point cost to $O(m^2)$ after the eigendecomposition. The second contribution addresses the remaining $O(m^3)$ bottleneck of the full eigendecomposition. Since the local covariance matrix has rank at most $k-1 \ll m$, we replace it with a truncated SVD of the $k \times m$ centered data matrix, an $O(k^2 m)$ operation, and derive an analytical approximation for the contribution of the null-space eigenvectors based on the expected value of their outer product under the Haar measure. The resulting estimator has total cost $O(k^2 m + k m p^2)$, where $p = k-1$. Experiments on real-world datasets confirm speedups of 50 to 300 times relative to the original implementation, with negligible loss when the fast estimator is used to replace the original version. By providing a scalable and data-driven estimate of local curvature, the proposed method establishes curvature as a practical geometric feature for a broad range of machine learning tasks, from classical to modern deep learning pipelines.

</details>


### [228] [Less is MoE: Trimming Experts in Domain-Specialist Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05538)
*Haoze He,Xinkai Zou,Xuan Jiang,Xingyuan Ding,Ao Qu,Juncheng Billy Li,Heather Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现MoE模型的关键能力集中在FFN稀疏中间维度，提出基于Fisher重要性的Fisher-MoE压缩方法，在50%压缩比下保留模型能力，减少约45%权重内存并提升21%推理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE压缩方法在通用基准测试上灾难性失效，原因是压缩粒度不当——重要能力分布在专家间但集中在FFN稀疏中间维度，需要更细粒度的压缩策略。

Method: 使用Fisher重要性分数识别任务关键维度（优于激活值、路由器分数和幅度基线），在FFN内部按Fisher重要性排序移除中间维度，实现Fine-grained压缩。

Result: 在Qwen1.5-MoE上，仅移除1.35M路由FFN中间维度中的12个就导致GSM8K准确率崩溃；50%压缩比下，Fisher-MoE保留模型能力，减少~45%权重内存，推理吞吐量提升21%。

Conclusion: 中间维度粒度是MoE模型压缩和重要性排序的有效单元，关键能力在此集中，为MoE高效部署提供了新方向。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models achieve strong performance through conditional computation, but their large parameter footprint poses deployment challenges. Prior MoE compression approaches catastrophically fail when evaluated on general-purpose benchmarks beyond commonsense reasoning. We trace this failure to the granularity of compression: important capabilities are distributed across experts but concentrated in FFN sparse intermediate dimensions. To identify these dimensions, we use Fisher importance which outperforms activation-, router-score-, and magnitude-based alternatives, and identifies tiny sets of task-critical dimensions: in Qwen1.5-MoE, removing as few as 12 of 1.35M routed-FFN intermediate dimensions collapses GSM8K accuracy while largely preserving factual-knowledge performance. Building on this, we propose Fisher-MoE, which operates within FFN to remove intermediate dimensions ranked by Fisher importance. At the same 50% MoE compression ratio, Fisher-MoE preserves model capability, while reducing weight memory by ~45% and improving inference throughput by 21%. These findings suggest intermediate dimension granularity is an effective unit for both compression and ranking where capability concentrates in MoE models.

</details>


### [229] [Representation Learning Enables Scalable Multitask Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05555)
*Johan Obando-Ceron,Lu Li,Scott Fujimoto,Pierre-Luc Bacon,Aaron Courville,Pablo Samuel Castro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MR.Q算法，证明了在多样化多任务强化学习中，预测性表征学习（而非基于模型的规划）是可扩展性的关键驱动因素。该无模型方法结合高容量价值函数与辅助预测目标，在连续控制任务上超越基于世界模型的方法，同时显著降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于模型的强化学习方法在多任务设置中表现优异，但其依赖规划和复杂训练流程，导致难以厘清哪些组件对可扩展性真正必要。本文旨在探究多任务RL可扩展性的核心驱动力。

Method: 提出MR.Q算法，一种结合预测性辅助目标（基于模型的表征学习）与高容量价值函数近似的无模型actor-critic架构，完全摒弃了规划过程。

Result: 在多样化的多任务连续控制任务套件上，MR.Q优于基于世界模型的方法及多种深度RL基线；显著降低计算开销并提升墙钟效率；增加模型容量带来一致性改进。

Conclusion: 多任务RL可扩展性的核心驱动力是表征学习而非基于模型的控制；预测性表征学习对性能至关重要，且无模型方法结合高质量表征足以达到甚至超越基于模型的方法。

Abstract: Scaling reinforcement learning (RL) to diverse multitask settings remains a central challenge. While recent advances in model-based RL achieve strong performance, they rely on planning and complex training pipelines, making it unclear which components are essential for scalability. We revisit this question and argue that the primary driver of scalable multitask RL is not model-based control, but \emph{representation learning}. In particular, we show that combining predictive, model-based representations with high-capacity value function approximation is sufficient to achieve strong performance, even without planning. We evaluate a simple model-free algorithm, MR.Q, coupled with auxiliary predictive objectives into a scalable actor-critic architecture. This approach outperforms a recent world-model-based method and a range of deep RL baselines across a diverse suite of multitask continuous control tasks, while significantly reducing computational overhead and improving wall-clock efficiency. We observe consistent improvements with increased model capacity and show through ablations that predictive representation learning is critical for performance.

</details>


### [230] [From Prediction to Self: Developmental Conditions for Agency in Minimal Neural Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05605)
*Evan Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过40个受控实验追踪一个192维GRU系统从纯预测到区分自我因果与世界因果的转变过程，发现四个严格有序的条件（持久状态、因果行动回路、本体感觉反馈、异步觉醒），并提出代理增益（A=Err_world-Err_self）作为量化指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一个仅通过预测世界运行的系统如何逐步发展出区分自身因果影响与外部世界变化的能力，即自我表征的涌现机制。

Method: 在192维GRU上进行40个受控实验，按发展序列逐步添加组件，追踪系统能否区分自我因果与世界因果；提出代理增益（A=Err_world-Err_self）作为量化指标；对比前向采样与两种梯度方法的效果。

Result: 发现四个严格有序的必要条件：(1)持久状态形成稳定吸引子，(2)因果行动回路连接输出到输入，(3)本体感觉反馈使隐含因果知识显式化，(4)异步觉醒（感知学习先于行动学习）。自感知预测器在周期和混沌环境中均优于自盲预测器。仅前向采样行动选择产生有意义代理增益，梯度方法退化。识别出12个被证伪的假设。

Conclusion: 系统从纯预测到自我因果区分需要特定的发展序列和组件，代理增益是有效的量化指标，预测编码本身不足以产生自我表征。

Abstract: How does a system that merely predicts the world come to distinguish its own causal influence from everything else? We trace this transition in a minimal 192-dimensional GRU through 40 controlled experiments arranged as a developmental sequence, adding components one at a time and tracking whether the system can distinguish self-caused from world-caused changes.
  The developmental path reveals four conditions that must be satisfied in strict order: (1) persistent state forming stable attractors, (2) a causal action loop linking output to input, (3) proprioceptive feedback that makes implicit causal knowledge explicit, and (4) asynchronous awakening - perceptual learning must consolidate before action learning begins. We propose agency gain (A = Err_world - Err_self), the predictive advantage of knowing one's own action, as a metric to track this process. The self-aware predictor consistently outperforms the self-blind predictor across periodic (sinusoidal) and chaotic (Lorenz) environments, and the metric survives ablation of all auxiliary components. Only forward-sampled action selection produces meaningful agency gain; two gradient-based alternatives degenerate.
  Equally significant are 12 falsified hypotheses mapping where development stalls: predictive coding alone does not produce self-represent

</details>


### [231] [Cross-Epoch Adaptive Rollout Optimization for RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05606)
*Yiming Zong,Yige Wang,Jiashuo Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CERO方法，通过贝叶斯后验估计每个提示的成功概率，并利用Fenchel对偶和在线梯度下降实现自适应rollout分配，在固定预算下显著优于GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练强化学习方法对所有提示使用固定rollout预算，忽略了不同提示提供的训练信号差异，导致样本效率低下。

Method: 维护每个提示成功概率的Beta后验分布，用后验期望伯努利方差估计额外rollout的价值，构造凹饱和效用函数；通过Fenchel对偶重构将时序不可分的目标转化为可解形式，使用投影在线梯度下降更新提示级和预算级对偶变量。

Result: 在固定提示效用下证明了对离线分配基准的O(√K)遗憾界；在多个数学推理任务和开源LLM上，CERO一致优于GRPO。

Conclusion: 自适应rollout预算分配能显著提升样本效率，CERO是一种有效的在线资源分配方法。

Abstract: LLM post-training often relies on reinforcement learning methods that sample multiple rollouts per prompt, yet most existing approaches use a fixed rollout budget for every prompt, despite large differences in the training signal different prompts provide. In this paper, we study adaptive rollout allocation under a fixed global budget and formulate the problem as online resource allocation with prompt-level diminishing returns. Our method, CERO, maintains a Beta posterior over each prompt's success probability and uses the posterior expected Bernoulli variance as a Bayesian estimate of the value of additional rollouts. We use this estimate to construct a concave, saturating utility over cumulative allocations, yielding an objective in which decisions across prompts and epochs are coupled by the global budget. Since the resulting objective is temporally nonseparable, we derive a Fenchel-dual reformulation and update both prompt-level and budget-level dual variables via projected online gradient descent. Under fixed prompt utilities, we prove an $O(\sqrt{K})$ regret bound against the offline allocation benchmark. Experiments on mathematical-reasoning problems show that CERO consistently outperforms GRPO across multiple open-weight LLMs and benchmarks, demonstrating that adaptive rollout budgeting can improve sample efficiency.

</details>


### [232] [StableRCA: Robust Graph-Agnostic Mechanism-Level Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05636)
*Xiaoyu Lin,Nicholas Tagliapietra,Kehan Li,Lavdim Halilaj,Juergen Luettin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StableRCA 提出了一种无需全局因果图的局部机制级根因分析方法，通过估计局部马尔可夫边界并检测其中的条件分布偏移来识别干预目标，在理论上保证了指数级收敛的识别概率，并在多个真实场景中验证了其鲁棒性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RCA 方法面临瓶颈：基于图的因果方法需要已知或准确估计的因果图，而无图统计方法要么定位的是边缘异常而非结构成因，要么依赖对图结构或函数形式的严格假设。

Method: StableRCA 通过估计局部马尔可夫边界并检测其中的条件分布偏移来识别干预目标，利用独立因果机制原理，在忠实马尔可夫边界恢复和非退化机制偏移条件下，保证干预目标识别概率随样本量指数级收敛。

Result: 在合成基准和五个真实数据集上的实验表明，StableRCA 对图误设定具有鲁棒性，在多个干预目标下有效，可扩展至大规模系统，并在多样化应用领域中表现可靠。

Conclusion: StableRCA 是一种无需全局图发现、鲁棒且可扩展的局部机制级 RCA 框架，有效克服了现有方法的瓶颈。

Abstract: Root-Cause Analysis (RCA) seeks to identify the variables responsible for abnormal system behavior in complex domains such as manufacturing, cloud computing, and healthcare. Existing approaches face a critical bottleneck: graph-based causal methods can identify intervention targets but typically require a known or accurately estimated causal graph, while graph-free statistical methods either localize marginal anomalies rather than structural causes, or rely on restrictive assumptions about graph structure or functional form. We propose StableRCA, a local mechanism-level RCA framework that avoids global graph discovery by estimating local Markov boundaries and detecting conditional distribution shifts within them. Leveraging the Independent Causal Mechanism principle, we show that intervention targets can be identified with probability converging exponentially in sample size under faithful Markov boundary recovery and non-degenerate mechanism shifts. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks and five real-world datasets demonstrate that StableRCA is robust to graph misspecification, effective under multiple intervention targets, scalable to large systems, and reliable across diverse application domains. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/StableRCA-E362

</details>


### [233] [Q-GNN: Query-Conditioned Graph Neural Networks with Type Awareness for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05639)
*Dongxiao He,Ruqiong Zhang,Zhizhi Yu,Ling Ding,Di Jin,Guangquan Xu,Zhiyong Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-GNN，通过引入查询实体的结构上下文（邻居结构与关系模式）和语义类型（由LLM推断），在知识图谱补全中同时利用查询实体和查询关系引导推理，克服了传统GNN方法仅依赖查询关系而忽略查询实体信息的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的KGC方法仅使用查询关系作为引导信号，忽略了查询实体本身携带的信息，导致推理过程中实体仅作为子图提取的结构锚点，未能充分利用实体层面的结构信息和语义类型约束。

Method: 从两个角度引入查询实体信息：1）结构上下文：通过专用上下文编码器编码实体周围的邻居结构和关系模式，用于调制消息传递；2）语义类型：利用大语言模型推断实体的语义类型，融入注意力计算和最终评分，提供类型级别的先验约束。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上的实验结果表明，Q-GNN方法有效提升了知识图谱补全性能。

Conclusion: 通过同时利用查询关系和查询实体信息引导推理过程，能够更充分地利用知识图谱中的信息进行三元组补全。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) aims at predicting missing triplets from incomplete knowledge graphs, which is crucial for downstream applications. Recently, Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based methods have achieved remarkable success by performing message passing over query-centered local subgraphs. However, in practice, a query is jointly defined by both the entity and the relation, with both carrying information indispensable for reasoning, yet these methods rely solely on the query relation as the guiding signal, while the information inherent in the query entity is not leveraged to guide inference - the entity serves merely as a structural anchor for subgraph extraction. To this end, we incorporate query entity information into the reasoning process from two perspectives: the first is structural context, i.e., the neighboring structure and relation patterns around the entity, which is encoded by a dedicated context encoder and used to modulate messages; the second is semantic type of the entity, inferred by a large language model, which is incorporated into attention computation and final scoring to provide type-level prior constraints. Together, these two sources of information enable the reasoning process to be guided by both the query relation and the query entity. Experimental results on standard benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed Q-GNN.

</details>


### [234] [Two-Way Is Better Than One: Bidirectional Alignment with Cycle Consistency for Exemplar-Free Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05675)
*Hongye Xu,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BiCyc是一种双向投影对齐方法，通过循环一致性目标联合优化新旧映射，解决原型类增量学习中单向投影导致的系统性偏差问题，有效减少灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有原型类增量学习中的单向投影方法存在系统性偏差：要么扭曲当前特征几何结构，要么仅局部对齐旧类别，导致跨任务循环不一致性累积，加剧表示漂移和灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出BiCyc双向投影器对齐方法，联合优化old-to-new和new-to-old两个映射，采用stop-gradient门控机制使传输与表示协同演化；通过循环一致性损失在白化空间中收缩奇异谱，改善类均值和协方差的传输。

Result: 在标准无样本类增量学习基准上，BiCyc显著减少了遗忘并提高了从头训练设置的准确率，在预训练细粒度场景中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 双向投影对齐与循环一致性损失能有效缓解原型漂移导致的系统性偏差，通过保持旧类别决策边界来减轻灾难性遗忘，为无样本类增量学习提供了高效且理论保障的解决方案。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) seeks models that acquire new skills without erasing prior knowledge. In exemplar-free class-incremental learning (EFCIL), this challenge is amplified because past data cannot be stored, making representation drift for old classes particularly harmful. Prototype-based EFCIL is attractive for its efficiency, yet prototypes drift as the embedding space evolves; therefore, projection-based drift compensation has become a popular remedy. We show, however, that existing one-directional projections introduce systematic bias: they either retroactively distort the current feature geometry or align past classes only locally, leaving cycle inconsistencies that accumulate across tasks. We introduce BiCyc, a bidirectional projector alignment approach with a cycle-consistency objective. BiCyc jointly optimizes two maps, old-to-new and new-to-old, with stop-gradient gating so that transport and representation co-evolve. Analytically, we show that the cycle loss contracts the singular spectrum toward unity in whitened space, and that improved transport of class means and covariances yields smaller perturbations of classification log-odds, preserving old-class decisions and mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Empirically, across standard EFCIL benchmarks, BiCyc substantially reduces forgetting and improves accuracy in from-scratch settings, while remaining competitive in the pretrained fine-grained regime.

</details>


### [235] [Causal Modeling of Selection in Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05689)
*Haoyue Dai,Zeyu Tang,Peter Spirtes,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文区分了两种选择偏差：静态选择（一次性过滤）和进化选择（迭代适应性筛选），指出现有方法仅适用于前者，并为后者提出了新的因果发现模型与识别方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法将数据选择偏差统一定义为同一种图模型，然而进化场景中的选择（如免疫适应、抗生素耐药性）是迭代、历史累积的过程，与静态选择（如调查志愿者偏差）本质不同，导致错误发现。因此需要为进化选择建立专门的理论框架。

Method: 提出一种新的图模型来刻画进化选择的内在机制，并开发了一个在单/多环境或多代数据上进行的、完备且正确的识别算法，用于从观测数据中推断该模型结构。

Result: 实验验证了该方法能够有效从数据中揭示驱动进化过程的相关机制，优于现有混淆两种选择的方法。

Conclusion: 静态选择与进化选择本质不同，需要分别建模；本文提出的进化选择模型及其识别算法填补了这一空白，为进化场景下的因果发现提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Understanding potential selection in data is crucial for causal discovery; we argue that "selection" in common narratives takes two forms, which we term static and evolutionary selection, respectively. Static selection refers to a one-shot filtering process where observed data consist of a subset of the population of interest, as in survey volunteer bias. Evolutionary selection, in contrast, operates through repeated rounds of differential fitness in reproduction, where observed data constitute the latest generation shaped by a historical trajectory, as in immune adaptation, antibiotic resistance, and social norm emergence. Existing methods largely conflate these two forms and rely on an identical graphical model of selection. We show that this model is valid for static settings but fails to characterize data under evolution, yielding false discovery results. To address this, we introduce a new model that specifically characterizes evolutionary selection, and develop a sound and complete procedure for identifying such models from data across one or multiple environments or generations. Experimental results validate the method's ability to uncover the relevant mechanisms underlying evolution from data.

</details>


### [236] [Benchmarking Counterfactual Prediction in Epidemic Time Series with Time-Varying Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05692)
*Wenhao Mu,Facundo Yan,Anik Mumssen,Marisa Eisenberg,Alexander Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于美国150多个县的真实人口、流动、流行病学和政策数据的大规模疫情时间序列反事实预测基准，支持静态/时变治疗和单/多策略干预设置，评估发现现有因果推断方法存在显著性能差异和现实时序因果推理挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列因果推断基准要么依赖无真实反事实标签的观测数据，要么使用过于简化的模拟，无法反映真实世界的复杂因果动态，亟需一个具有可观测反事实结果的大规模现实基准。

Method: 基于校准的代理人基模型（agent-based model），融入真实的人口、流动、流行病学和政策数据，生成超过150个美国县区的反事实轨迹；设计支持静态/时变治疗、单/多策略干预的评估框架。

Result: 广泛评估了现有和最新的因果推断方法，发现方法之间在反事实预测性能上存在显著差异，揭示了现实场景下时间序列因果推理的挑战。

Conclusion: 该基准填补了缺乏大规模现实反事实数据的空白，为未来时间序列因果推断方法的发展提供了更具挑战性的评估标准。

Abstract: Deep learning has enabled significant advances in time-series causal inference, yet progress remains constrained by the lack of realistic benchmarks with observable counterfactual outcomes. Existing datasets either rely on real-world observations without ground-truth counterfactuals or on simplified simulations that fail to capture complex causal dynamics. To address this gap, we develop a large-scale benchmark for counterfactual prediction in epidemic time series under dynamic interventions. Unlike existing benchmarks, it supports static and time-varying treatments, as well as both single-policy and multi-policy intervention settings, enabling evaluation of causal inference methods across a broad range of causal inference scenarios. Leveraging a calibrated agent-based model grounded in real-world demographic, mobility, epidemiological, and policy data, we generate realistic counterfactual trajectories across more than 150 U.S. counties. Using this benchmark, we evaluate widely used and state-of-the-art causal inference methods, revealing substantial performance differences and highlighting the challenges of realistic time-series causal reasoning.

</details>


### [237] [MolE-RAG: Molecular Structure-Enhanced Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05693)
*Joey Chan,Wonbin Kweon,Ashley Shin,Niharika Bhattacharjee,Pengcheng Jiang,Yue Guo,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MolE-RAG是一个无需训练的、以分子为中心的检索增强生成框架，通过整合化学文献、分子特定信息和结构相似分子三种上下文来源，显著提升大语言模型在分子性质预测任务上的性能，无需微调即可将ROC-AUC提升多达28个百分点，回归RMSE降低多达67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在分子性质预测中表现有限，因为SMILES等分子表示与LLM预训练的自然语言存在语义和化学知识鸿沟，需要弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出MolE-RAG框架，在推理时从三个互补来源检索增强上下文：化学文献检索、分子特定信息（同义词、标识符、官能团注释、物理化学描述符）以及训练集中结构相似分子检索，无需模型微调。

Result: 在9个分子性质预测任务上，相比仅使用SMILES的基线，通用LLM的分类ROC-AUC提升最多28个百分点，回归RMSE降低最多67%。不同上下文来源的效用因模型和任务而异。

Conclusion: 以分子为中心的检索增强可以在无需微调的情况下提升LLM的分子性质预测能力，同时提供一种灵活的框架用于在推理时整合异构化学知识。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for molecular property prediction, but their ability to reason over chemical structures remains limited, as molecular representations such as SMILES differ substantially from the natural language on which LLMs are primarily trained. To bridge this semantic and chemical knowledge gap, we propose MolE-RAG, a training-free, molecule-centric retrieval-augmented generation framework for LLM-based molecular property prediction. MolE-RAG augments each prediction with three complementary sources of inference-time context: retrieved chemistry literature, molecule-specific information including compound synonyms, identifiers, functional group annotations, and physicochemical descriptors, and structurally similar molecules retrieved from the training set. We evaluate MolE-RAG across nine molecular property prediction tasks using proprietary, chemistry-specialized, and open-source LLMs. Across general-purpose LLMs, MolE-RAG improves ROC-AUC by up to 28 percentage points on classification tasks and reduces regression RMSE by up to 67% relative to a SMILES-only baseline. We further find that the utility of each context source varies across models and tasks, with different models benefiting most from textual retrieval, molecular context, or structural retrieval. These results suggest that molecule-centric retrieval can improve LLM-based molecular property prediction without model fine-tuning while providing a flexible framework for integrating heterogeneous chemical knowledge at inference time.

</details>


### [238] [Revisiting Prototype Rehearsal for Exemplar-Free Continual Learning: Manifold-Aware Boundary Sampling with Adaptive Class-Balanced Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05695)
*Hongye Xu,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了无样本类增量学习（EFCIL）中的原型回放策略，指出现有方法性能不佳的原因在于将原型视为孤立类摘要、忽略邻近类别信息且未解决类别不平衡。为此提出流形感知的原型回放方法：采用约束扩展过采样生成边界感知的样本，并设计自适应类别平衡损失进行时间加权，缩小甚至逆转了与最近漂移补偿方法之间的差距，在多个EFCIL基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无样本类增量学习中，原型回放策略被漂移补偿方法持续超越，但作者认为问题不在于原型回放本身，而在于现有实现方式将原型视为孤立类摘要，忽略了邻近敌方类别信息，且未解决合成旧类样本与真实新类样本之间的类别不平衡。

Method: 1) 约束扩展过采样（Constrained Expansive Over-Sampling）：将旧类原型向最近的新类敌方特征插值，生成遵循数据流形并保持类间分离的边界感知回放样本。2) 自适应类别平衡损失（Adaptive Class-Balanced loss）：基于时间进行类别加权，在旧原型信息最丰富时放大其梯度，随后续任务积累逐渐退火其影响。

Result: 所提方法将原型回放转变为抗漂移且感知不平衡的机制，缩小甚至逆转了与最近漂移补偿方法的差距，在多个EFCIL基准上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过改进原型回放的实现方式（边界感知采样+自适应类别平衡），可以恢复原型回放在EFCIL中的竞争力，表明该策略本身并非根本受限，关键在于如何正确实例化。

Abstract: Exemplar-free class-incremental learning (EFCIL) aims to acquire new classes over time without storing raw data. Historically, prototype rehearsal, which samples around stored class prototypes and mixes them with current-task data, has been a popular strategy to reduce catastrophic forgetting. However, recent drift-compensation methods that explicitly realign prototypes in the evolving feature space consistently outperform prototype-based rehearsal, raising the question of whether rehearsal itself is fundamentally limited. We argue that the performance gap stems not from the idea of prototype rehearsal per se, but from how it is typically instantiated: existing approaches treat prototypes as isolated class summaries that ignore information from nearby enemy classes, and fail to correct the emerging class imbalance between a handful of synthetic old-class samples and hundreds of real instances from newly introduced classes. Building on this hypothesis, we revisit prototype rehearsal and propose a manifold-aware variant that restores its competitiveness in EFCIL. First, we introduce Constrained Expansive Over-Sampling, which interpolates each old-class prototype toward its nearest enemy features from new classes, generating boundary-aware rehearsal samples that better follow the underlying data manifold while preserving inter-class separation. Second, we design an Adaptive Class-Balanced loss that performs time-based class weighting, amplifying gradients from older prototypes when they are most informative and gradually annealing their influence as richer supervision from later tasks accumulates. Together, these components turn prototype rehearsal into a drift-resilient, imbalance-aware mechanism that closes, and often reverses, the gap to recent drift-compensation methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance across multiple EFCIL benchmarks.

</details>


### [239] [Intercomparison of Machine Learning Algorithms for Remote Sensing-based In-season Crop Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05731)
*August Posch,Jitendra Kumar,Forrest M. Hoffman,Auroop R. Ganguly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过整合HLS遥感时间序列和轮作历史信息，利用SVM算法在30米分辨率下实现了6月初对玉米（爱荷华州）和杏仁（加州）的季内作物类型精准识别，F1分数分别达0.59和0.74，并量化了物候和空间分布的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作物类型制图产品（如USDA CDL）存在收获后才能获取的滞后性，缺乏能在收获前以满意精度进行近实时制图的方法，无法满足应急管理部门对作物威胁的快速响应需求；同时，已有研究未充分考虑算法在不同年份间的相对优势比较。

Method: 采用HLS地表反射率影像时间序列结合作物轮作历史信息，在30米分辨率下构建分类模型；对十种机器学习算法、数千种模型配置进行逐年交叉验证和综合指标评估，通过超参数搜索确定最优算法。

Result: SVM在五个未见过的验证年份中表现最优：加州杏仁6月初平均F1为0.74，爱荷华州玉米为0.59；年际变异是主要不确定性来源，但集成方法或辅助数据有进一步提升潜力。

Conclusion: SVM结合HLS时间序列和轮作信息可在生长季早期实现高分辨率作物类型制图；未来工作可扩展至多类别作物、全CONUS范围制图及季内产量预测。

Abstract: In-season crop type mapping is critical for food security in the face of increasingly extreme climate-related threats to crops. Currently, the USDA Cropland Data Layer provides crop type labels at 30m resolution and is available the February after harvest, but no product exists that maps crop types before harvest with satisfactory accuracy that would allow emergency managers to respond to crop threats in near real time. Furthermore, the relative advantages of a wide range of algorithms have not been evaluated in a way that accounts for interannual variability, until this study. Here, Harmonized Landsat-Sentinel surface reflectance imagery time series and crop rotation history information are combined to map corn in Iowa and almonds in California at 30m resolution accurately by early June in unseen years, with robust quantification of uncertainty due to phenology and crop distribution. Thousands of model configurations across ten machine learning algorithms were compared using a year-wise cross-validation and a suite of metrics. Hyperparameter search revealed Support Vector Machines to be the most successful algorithm overall, with a mean F1 score of 0.74 (0.59) across five unseen validation years for almonds by early June in California (corn by early June in Iowa). Interannual variation was a large source of uncertainty, but patterns showed the potential to further improve performance with ensemble approaches or ancillary data. Future work may extend these methods to include multiclass maps of all crop types, CONUS-wide maps, and in-season crop yield forecasting.

</details>


### [240] [Beyond Soft Masks: Hard-Perturbation Mixup Explainer for Robust GNN Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05756)
*Jialiang Yin,Zheng Zhao,Linsey Pang,Bo Dong,Bin Shi,Jiaxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HPME框架，通过硬扰动混合和广义图信息瓶颈，利用图池化提取离散解释子图，并通过结构级替换的混合策略生成分布内解释，解决了现有方法因软掩码无法完全消除标签无关信息导致的分布偏移和保真度下降问题，在多项任务上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN后验解释方法使用软掩码无法完全消除标签无关信息，导致冗余结构泄露到混合过程中，无法解决分布外（OOD）问题，降低了解释保真度。

Method: 提出HPME框架：1) 基于广义图信息瓶颈，利用图池化提取离散解释子图并生成信息容量边界以压缩标签无关成分；2) 引入基于结构级替换的新型混合策略，生成分布内解释以缓解分布偏移。

Result: 在多种任务上的大量实验表明，HPME在合成和真实数据集上生成鲁棒且可解释的解释方面达到了最优性能。

Conclusion: 硬扰动结合信息瓶颈与结构级混合策略能有效解决软掩码的固有问题，提升GNN解释的保真度与分布鲁棒性。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have demonstrated remarkable performance across a range of applications involving graph-structured data, particularly in high-stakes domains. However, the opaque nature of their decision-making processes limits their trustworthiness and broader adoption. Existing post-hoc explanation methods aim to improve explainability by identifying subgraphs that influence GNN predictions and adopt mixup strategies to alleviate the out-of-distribution (OOD) issue caused by using subgraphs for prediction. Yet, these approaches typically rely on soft masks, which are inherently unable to fully eliminate label-irrelevant information, allowing redundant structures to leak into the mixup process and hindering the resolution of the OOD problem, thereby degrading explanation fidelity. In this work, we propose HPME, a Hard-Perturbation Mixup Explanation framework grounded in a generalized Graph Information Bottleneck, which leverages graph pooling to extract discrete explanatory subgraphs and to yield an information-capacity bound to thoroughly compress label-irrelevant components. Furthermore, we introduce a novel mixup strategy built upon structure-level replacement, generating in-distribution explanations to effectively mitigate the distribution shift. Extensive experiments on diverse tasks demonstrate that HPME achieves state-of-the-art performance in generating robust and interpretable explanations across both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [241] [Domain-Adapted Small Language Models with Hybrid Post-Processing: Achieving Cost-Efficient, Low-Latency Multi-Label Structured Prediction via LoRA Fine-Tuning on Scarce Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05781)
*Srinivasan Manoharan,Dilipkumar Nallusamy,Sachin Kumar,Haifeng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合框架，将经过LoRA微调的小语言模型（LLaMA 3.1 8B，仅训练2.05%参数）与确定性规则后处理层相结合，在仅219个标注样本上训练，用于多标签合规评估。在53个未见过的生产数据上，实现了100%的JSON结构有效性、83.0%的整体准确率和关键字段100%准确率，同时推理速度比前沿模型API快2-5倍，成本降低46-76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署前沿大语言模型进行领域特定的结构化评估任务存在高延迟、高成本和数据隐私问题，需要一种更高效且经济的替代方案。

Method: 采用混合神经-符号分解框架：1) 使用LoRA对LLaMA 3.1 8B进行参数高效微调（仅2.05%可训练参数）；2) 结合确定性基于规则的后处理层；3) 引入针对性硬负例增强以提升关键决策边界性能。

Result: 在53个未见过的生产数据盲测中：JSON结构有效性100%；整体准确率83.0%；关键分类字段准确率100%。推理时间约2秒（单A100 GPU），比前沿模型API快2-5倍。每次评估成本$0.013，相比专有方案$0.025-$0.055，节省46-76%成本。

Conclusion: 领域适应的小语言模型结合确定性后处理，能够在结构化合规评估任务中匹配前沿模型的准确性，同时大幅降低运营成本、延迟和隐私风险，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deploying frontier large language models (LLMs) for domain-specific structured evaluation tasks often incurs substantial latency, cost, and data privacy overhead. We present a hybrid framework that combines a fine-tuned small language model (LLaMA 3.1 8B, with only 2.05% trainable parameters via LoRA) and a deterministic rule-based post-processing layer. Trained on just 219 curated examples, the system is applied to multi-label compliance evaluation of conversational transcripts spanning 18 heterogeneous output fields. In blind evaluation on 53 previously unseen production transcripts, it achieves 100% JSON structural validity, 83.0% human-validated overall accuracy, and 100% accuracy on the most critical classification field. The proposed approach formalizes a hybrid neural-symbolic decomposition and introduces targeted hard-negative augmentation to improve performance on critical decision boundaries. Running on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU, inference completes in approximately 2 seconds, which is 2-5x faster than frontier-model APIs. The system costs only $0.013 per evaluation compared with $0.025-$0.055 for proprietary alternatives, resulting in 46-76% cost savings. These results demonstrate that domain-adapted small language models, when combined with deterministic post-processing, can match frontier-model accuracy for structured compliance evaluation while substantially reducing operational cost, latency, and privacy risk.
  Keywords: small language models, parameter-efficient fine-tuning, LoRA, domain adaptation, hybrid inference, compliance evaluation, structured output.

</details>


### [242] [CaliDist: Calibrating Large Language Models via Behavioral Robustness to Distraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05799)
*Mohammad Anas Jawad,Cornelia Caragea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CaliDist方法，通过测量LLM在干扰信息下的行为稳定性来改进后验校准，在7个NLU基准上平均将ECE从23%降至7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM校准方法忽略了模型对无关或误导信息的"行为鲁棒性"这一信任维度，模型的真实置信度应反映其在认知压力下的稳定性。

Method: 提出CaliDist，一种后验校准方法。通过向输入提示注入语义干扰项，量化模型预测和不确定性的变化，利用这种稳定性信号自适应缩放模型的初始置信度分数。

Result: 在7个NLU分类基准上使用6种不同LLM进行实验，CaliDist在ECE和Brier Score上均优于强基线，平均将ECE从23%降至7%，相对改进70%。

Conclusion: 行为稳定性是校准的有效信号，CaliDist通过测量模型对干扰的敏感性显著提升了校准质量。

Abstract: Existing calibration methods for Large Language Models (LLMs) often overlook a critical dimension of trustworthiness: a model's {\em behavioral robustness} to irrelevant or misleading information. In this paper, we argue that a model's true confidence should reflect its stability under cognitive pressure. We introduce \textsc{CaliDist}, a novel post-hoc calibration approach that directly measures and penalizes a model's susceptibility to distraction. \textsc{CaliDist} quantifies how an LLM's predictions and uncertainty change when its input prompt is perturbed with semantic \textit{distractors}. This stability (or lack thereof) signal is then used to adaptively scale the model's initial confidence score. Our extensive experiments on seven Natural Language Understanding classification benchmarks using six distinct LLMs show that \textsc{CaliDist} consistently achieves lower Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and Brier Score compared with strong baselines. Remarkably, our method reduces the ECE from 23\% to 7\% on average--a relative improvement of 70\%--demonstrating that behavioral stability is a powerful signal for calibration. We make our code and datasets available at github.com/m-anas-j/CaliDist.

</details>


### [243] [SALT: When More Rollouts Don't Help in Group-Based Policy Optimization and How to Make Them Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05800)
*Powei Chang,Jinpeng Zhang,Chaoqun Sun,MiniWell Tsao,Lianrui Li,Jianxiang Xiang,Chenyu Wang,Yukang Gao,Dongying Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRPO风格的组归一化更新中，增加采样数量反而因梯度低秩和符号抵消导致有效更新减弱。SALT通过子空间自适应重加权组系数，增强残差通道以缓解符号抵消，在多尺度推理基准上提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO风格的组归一化更新中，增加rollout数量并不能可靠地增强学习，因为每个rollout的策略梯度特征会集中到低秩、有符号的几何结构中，导致聚合时大量抵消，削弱有效更新。

Method: SALT（Subspace-Adaptive geometry pLug-in componenT）利用样本梯度的几何结构重新加权组相对更新的系数。具体来说，从mini-batch的Gram几何中估计主导共享子空间，将组相对系数分解为共享通道和残差通道，并在符号抵消严重时自适应放大残差通道。

Result: 在多种面向推理的RLVR基准测试和模型规模上，SALT改善了有效更新的几何结构并提升了性能，且无需修改奖励模型或rollout采样过程。

Conclusion: SALT作为一种即插即用组件，通过几何自适应机制有效缓解了GRPO风格更新中因符号抵消导致的梯度退化问题，显著提升了RLVR训练效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) often adopts GRPO-style group-relative updates, sampling multiple rollouts per prompt to construct normalized learning signals. However, merely increasing the number of rollouts does not reliably strengthen learning: under GRPO-style group normalization, per-rollout policy-gradient features can concentrate into a low-rank, signed geometry, causing substantial cancellation during aggregation and weakening the effective update. We address this failure mode with SALT, a Subspace-Adaptive geometry pLug-in componenT that uses sample-wise gradient geometry to reweight the coefficients of group-relative updates. SALT estimates a dominant shared subspace from the mini-batch Gram geometry, decomposes group-relative coefficients into shared and residual channels, and adaptively amplifies the residual channel when signed cancellation is severe. Across diverse reasoning-oriented RLVR benchmarks and model scales, SALT improves effective update geometry and performance without modifying the reward model or the rollout sampling procedure

</details>


### [244] [Robust and sparse support vector machine via hybrid truncated loss for supervised classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05814)
*Yuliang Yang,Chen Chen,Yuxiang Liu,Huiru Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合截断损失函数L_ht，构建了单视图L_ht-SVM和多视图MvL_ht-SVM模型，通过P-驻点和ADMM工作集策略实现高效优化，在多个数据集上优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SVM损失函数中，凸损失（如hinge loss、最小二乘损失）对异常值敏感，而有界非凸损失计算成本高，亟需一种既稀疏有界又计算高效的损失函数。

Method: 提出混合截断损失L_ht（稀疏且有界），构建L_ht-SVM模型；引入P-驻点建立一阶最优性充要条件；设计带工作集策略的ADMM算法实现全局收敛；通过加入结构信息和视图权重扩展为多视图MvL_ht-SVM。

Result: 在合成、真实世界和图像数据集上，L_ht-SVM比5种单视图方法精度更高、支持向量更少、噪声鲁棒性更强；MvL_ht-SVM在准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数上优于6种多视图方法。

Conclusion: L_ht-SVM和MvL_ht-SVM在分类性能、稀疏性和鲁棒性方面均优于现有方法，验证了混合截断损失的有效性和多视图扩展的优越性。

Abstract: The support vector machine (SVM) is a widely used classifier, but choosing an appropriate loss function remains difficult. Convex losses such as the hinge loss and least-squares loss are sensitive to outliers, while bounded non-convex losses often lead to high computational cost. To address this, we propose a hybrid truncated loss function ($L_{\mathrm{ht}}$) that is both sparse and bounded, and build the $L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM model for single-view classification. We introduce the P-stationary point and use it to establish the first-order necessary and sufficient optimality conditions. Based on these conditions, we design an alternating direction method of multipliers with a working-set strategy that reduces computational cost and achieves global convergence. We further extend $L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM to multi-view learning by adding structural information and view weights, resulting in Mv$L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM, which follows both the consensus and complementarity principles. Experiments on synthetic, real-world, and image datasets show that $L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM achieves higher accuracy with fewer support vectors and better noise robustness than five single-view methods, while Mv$L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM outperforms six multi-view methods in accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score.

</details>


### [245] [Consistency Training Along the Transformer Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05817)
*Sukrati Gautam,Neil Shah,Arav Dhoot,Bryan Maruyama,Caroline Wei,Rohan Kapoor,Robert Sidey,Prakhar Gupta,Zi Cheng Huang,David Demitri Africa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了一致性训练的范畴，引入两种新的内部一致性目标（MLPCT和AttCT），并将其应用于四种新的安全威胁场景。实验表明一致性训练能有效减少模型失调，且存在跨威胁泛化能力。研究还揭示了不同一致性训练方法的机制差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一致性训练主要关注模型在不同上下文中的行为一致性，但局限于sycophancy和jailbreak等少数场景。作者希望扩展一致性训练的适用范围，探索其在更广泛安全威胁场景中的有效性。

Method: 1) 提出两种新的内部一致性目标：MLPCT（匹配MLP层后激活状态）和AttCT（匹配每头注意力分布）2) 将一致性训练应用于四种新的安全威胁：人物角色上下文学习攻击、对抗性挫折、前缀注入攻击、条件性失调 3) 在多种模型和威胁设置下进行实验评估。

Result: 一致性训练在多种威胁场景中均能有效减少模型失调，效果超越先前研究中的sycophancy和jailbreak场景。发现跨威胁泛化现象（训练一种威胁可提升对其他威胁的鲁棒性）。识别出ACT、MLPCT和AttCT共享残差流机制，而BCT在机制上有所不同。

Conclusion: 一致性训练是一个灵活可扩展的对齐框架，能够统一防御更广泛类别的模型病理行为。

Abstract: Consistency training encourages models to behave similarly across different contexts, and has shown promise for reducing misalignment. We broaden the scope of consistency training in two ways. First, we introduce two new internal consistency targets: MLP Consistency Training (MLPCT), which matches post-activation MLP states, and Attention Consistency Training (AttCT), which matches per-head attention distributions. Second, we apply consistency training to four additional safety threats: persona in-context learning attacks, adversarial frustration, prefill attacks, and conditional misalignment. Across several models and threat settings, we find that consistency training reduces misalignment well beyond the sycophancy and jailbreak settings studied in prior work. We also find cases of cross-threat generalization, where training against one failure mode improves robustness to another, and identify a shared residual-stream mechanism underlying ACT, MLPCT, and AttCT, while distinguishing BCT as mechanistically distinct. Our results suggest that consistency training is a flexible and extensible framework for alignment, capable of unifying defenses against a broader class of model pathologies.

</details>


### [246] [GenAutoML: An Agentic Framework for Dynamic Architecture Generation and Optimization in Time-Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05860)
*Oleeviya Babu Poikarayil,Cédric Schockaert,Abdulrahman Nahhas,Christian Daase,Mursal Dawodi,Jawid Ahmad Baktash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GenAutoML是一个利用大语言模型作为神经架构师的智能体框架，能够根据自然语言需求自动生成适配不同数据特征的轻量级时序预测与异常检测网络，在保持高性能的同时实现低于0.01ms的推理延迟，适用于边缘AI部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AutoML系统依赖静态预定义的搜索空间，难以适应多样化的数据特征；且神经架构设计需要大量领域专业知识与资源，亟需一种能动态生成任务特化架构的方法。

Method: 提出GenAutoML框架，核心组件包括：(1) LLM作为神经架构师，将自然语言需求转化为可执行的PyTorch代码；(2) 沙盒反射循环用于自主代码修正；(3) 签名感知运行时保障架构一致性与执行安全；(4) 动态可逆实例归一化(Dyn-RevIN)增强非平稳条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在ETTh1、ETTm1和Weather基准上，GenAutoML能动态生成任务特化的神经架构。其中WaveInterferenceNet的推理延迟低于0.01ms/样本，同时保持具有竞争力的预测性能。

Conclusion: GenAutoML通过结合计算效率、架构适应性和稳定优化行为，实现了适用于资源受限和延迟敏感的Edge AI场景的超轻量级神经网络自动生成。

Abstract: Designing neural architectures for time-series forecasting and anomaly detection remains a resource-intensive task that often requires substantial domain expertise. Traditional Automated Machine Learning (AutoML) systems typically rely on static, predefined search spaces, limiting their ability to adapt to diverse data characteristics. We present GenAutoML, an agentic framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) as neural architects to bridge natural-language requirements and executable PyTorch implementations. The framework incorporates a Sandboxed Reflection Loop for autonomous code refinement and a Signature-Aware Runtime that enforces architectural consistency and execution safety. To improve robustness under non-stationary conditions, we further introduce a Dynamic Reversible Instance Normalization (Dyn-RevIN) wrapper. Experiments on the ETTh1, ETTm1, and Weather benchmarks demonstrate that GenAutoML can dynamically generate task-specific neural architectures tailored to dataset characteristics. Among the generated models, WaveInterferenceNet achieves inference latency below 0.01 ms per sample while maintaining competitive predictive performance. By emphasizing computational efficiency, architectural adaptability, and stable optimization behavior, GenAutoML enables the creation of ultra-lightweight neural networks suitable for resource-constrained and latency-sensitive Edge AI deployments.

</details>


### [247] [Deciphering Two Training Clocks in Grokking via Deep Linear Network Theory with Conditional ReLU Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05863)
*Hu Tan,Kuo Gai,Shihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"双训练时钟"概念，形式化描述了分类损失快速衰减与表示简化缓慢发生的分离现象。在深度线性网络中，通过后间隔增长或单步尾收缩条件可在对数时间尺度上降低交叉熵损失；而在权重衰减下，结构能量在多项式时间尺度上衰减。ReLU MLP在激活模式固定时退化为线性模型，支持先拟合分类器后简化表示的两阶段机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化解释"Grokking"现象中训练数据拟合与学习底层规则发生在不同时间尺度的内在机理，分离分类损失快速衰减与表示简化的慢速过程。

Method: 提出双训练时钟框架：一个时钟对应分类损失的快速衰减（对数时间尺度），另一个对应表示简化的慢速过程（多项式时间尺度）。对深度线性网络进行严格理论分析，对ReLU MLP采用条件约化方法，在激活模式固定时退化为线性模型进行分析。

Result: 深度线性网络中，分类损失在对数时间尺度上降至epsilon水平；带权重衰减时，结构能量在多项式时间尺度上衰减。ReLU MLP中，分类器头比嵌入块获得更大有效梯度，支持两阶段机制。

Conclusion: 双训练时钟框架有效分离了拟合与表示简化过程，深度线性理论提供严格核心分析，ReLU结果作为条件约化解释实验现象，为理解grokking提供理论支撑。

Abstract: Grokking suggests that fitting the training data and learning a simple underlying rule may occur on different time scales. We formalize this phenomenon by separating the fast decay of the classification loss from the slower simplification of the learned representation, and we call the resulting pair of stopping times two training clocks. For deep linear networks, we show that a post-margin gap-growth or one-step tail-contraction condition reduces the cross-entropy loss to level epsilon on a logarithmic time scale. In contrast, when layerwise weight decay is present, the induced regularization on the end-to-end map can be expressed as a Schatten-type penalty; under a sharp late-time Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz tail, this structural energy closes on a polynomial time scale. The two clocks, therefore, separate fitting from representation simplification. We then explain how the same mechanism can appear in ReLU MLPs. In regions where the activation patterns on the training set remain fixed, the network reduces to a linear model in the active coordinates. In a two-layer ReLU embedding model, chain-rule estimates further show that the classifier head can receive larger effective gradients than the embedding block under controlled downstream norms. This supports a two-stage mechanism in which the classifier fits first, while the representation continues to simplify later. We use modular addition as the main experimental setting. The deep linear theory provides the rigorous core of the analysis. But the ReLU results are formulated as conditional reductions that account for empirical behavior without claiming a global proof for nonlinear training dynamics.

</details>


### [248] [TS-ICL: A Flexible Time-Indexed Foundation Model for Time Series via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05878)
*Etienne Le Naour,Tahar Nabil,Adrien Petralia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-ICL，一种基于概率上下文学习的编码器-回归器Transformer，统一了时间序列预测与缺失值插补任务，在插补任务上达到SOTA，同时在预测任务上与领先基础模型保持竞争力，特别是在部分观测回溯窗口的预测中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型主要专注于预测任务，但真实世界的时间序列数据往往是不规则且部分观测的，需要模型能够同时进行预测、缺失值插补并处理退化采样条件。目前缺乏能够统一解决这些挑战的通用模型。

Method: TS-ICL将时间序列任务形式化为时间戳对齐的回归问题，采用概率上下文学习的编码器-回归器Transformer架构。通过从新颖的因果数据先验生成的合成依赖结构进行训练，自然地融入协变量信息，实现预测与插补的统一框架。

Result: 在插补任务上达到新的最先进水平；在单变量和协变量感知基准测试中与领先的预测基础模型保持竞争力；在部分观测回溯窗口的预测场景中展现尤为强大的性能。

Conclusion: TS-ICL成功弥合了预测与插补之间的差距，为处理真实世界部分观测的时间序列数据提供了一种统一、通用的零样本解决方案，标志着时间序列基础模型向更实用范式的重要转变。

Abstract: Foundation models mark a profound paradigm shift in time series modeling, with task-specific models being superseded by general-purpose zero-shot models. Yet, current approaches primarily focus on forecasting, while real-world time series are often irregularly and partially observed, requiring models that can jointly forecast, impute missing values, and handle degraded sampling conditions. To address these challenges, we introduce TS-ICL, a novel probabilistic In-Context Learning encoder--regressor Transformer that unifies forecasting and imputation. TS-ICL formulates time series tasks as timestamp-aligned regression and naturally incorporates covariates by training on synthetic dependency structures generated from a novel causal data prior. Empirically, TS-ICL achieves a new state-of-the-art in imputation, while remaining competitive with leading forecasting foundation models across both univariate and covariate-aware benchmarks. It shows particularly strong performance in forecasting with partially observed look-back windows.

</details>


### [249] [When Denser Credit Is Not Enough: Evidence-Calibrated Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05885)
*Yuanfan Li,Qi Zhou,Wenjing Duan,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ECPO算法，通过证据校准的动作优势和方差门控信用加权，在稀疏奖励的长程LLM智能体任务中实现更可靠的逐步信用分配，在ALFWorld和WebShop上分别比GiGPO提升5.2和7.3个成功点，且额外计算开销仅0.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分组的策略优化方法（如GiGPO）在锚点状态下构建逐步优势时，因有限rollout下稀有但幸运的动作获得过大优势，导致发散性锚点偏差和训练后期振荡，信用分配统计上不可靠。

Method: ECPO包含两个核心组件：1) 证据校准动作优势（Evidence-Calibrated Action Advantage），将rollout按规范动作分组并对低计数估计进行收缩；2) 方差门控信用加权（Variance-Gated Credit Weighting），抑制被组内噪声主导的锚点状态。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上使用Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B进行实验，ECPO持续优于强基线，在Qwen2.5-1.5B上比GiGPO提升+5.2（ALFWorld）和+7.3（WebShop）个成功点，额外优势计算开销仅0.1%。

Conclusion: ECPO作为无需评论家的策略优化算法，能有效校准逐步信用，缓解稀疏延迟奖励下长程LLM智能体训练的统计不可靠问题。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents require reinforcement learning methods that can assign credit to intermediate decisions under sparse and delayed rewards. Recent group-based methods such as GiGPO improve over GRPO by constructing step-level advantages at repeated anchor states. However, we show that such dense credit can be statistically unreliable: under limited rollouts, rare but lucky actions may receive overly large advantages, producing divergent anchor bias and late-stage training oscillation. We propose Evidence-Calibrated Policy Optimization (ECPO), a critic-free policy optimization algorithm that calibrates step-level credit before policy updates. ECPO combines Evidence-Calibrated Action Advantage, which groups rollouts by canonical actions and shrinks low-count estimates, with Variance-Gated Credit Weighting, which suppresses anchor states dominated by within-action noise. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B show that ECPO consistently outperforms strong baselines, improving GiGPO by +5.2/+7.3 success points on ALFWorld/WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B while adding only 0.1% additional advantage-computation overhead.

</details>


### [250] [High-Dimensional Theory of LoRA Fine-Tuning in a Solvable Attention Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05899)
*O. Duranthon,F. Boncoraglio,L. Zdeborová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发展了注意力模型中低秩适配（LoRA）的高维统计理论，在可求解框架下分析了单头注意力层先在数据丰富任务上预训练、再通过秩一LoRA更新在有限数据上微调的过程。高维极限下，两阶段均可由有限序参数集精确刻画，得到测试误差和表示对齐的显式预测。分析表明预训练对LoRA的影响可归纳为有效噪声项，据此推导最优预训练策略，并发现测试误差与表示质量存在失配区域，进而提出主动微调的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA方法缺乏严格的理论理解，特别是预训练与微调之间的相互作用机制尚不清晰。本文旨在建立LoRA在注意力模型中的高维统计理论框架，从理论层面揭示预训练如何影响下游微调性能。

Method: 采用高维统计理论方法，构建单头注意力层的可求解框架：先在数据充足任务上预训练，再通过秩一LoRA更新在有限数据上微调。在高维极限下利用序参数方法对两阶段进行渐近精确刻画。

Result: (1) 预训练对LoRA的影响可简化为一个有效噪声项；(2) 基于该噪声项推导了最优预训练策略；(3) 发现测试误差与表示质量存在失配的相区；(4) 提出主动微调应用方案。

Conclusion: 建立了LoRA在注意力模型中的严格统计理论，揭示了预训练-微调相互作用的本质机制，为最优预训练设计和主动微调提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We develop a high-dimensional statistical theory of low-rank adaptation (LoRA) in attention models, capturing the interplay between pre-training and fine-tuning. We introduce a solvable framework in which a single-head attention layer is first pre-trained on a data-abundant task and subsequently adapted via a rank-one LoRA update on limited data. In the high-dimensional limit, both stages admit a sharp asymptotic characterization in terms of a finite set of order parameters, yielding explicit predictions for test errors and representation alignment. Our analysis shows that the impact of pre-training on LoRA is summarized by an effective noise term, from which we derive prescriptions for the optimal pre-training procedure. We also demonstrate a regime with a mismatch between the value of the test error and representation quality, and propose an application of our theory to active fine-tuning.

</details>


### [251] [Addressing Imbalance in Multi-Label Data via Label-Specific Distance-based Oversampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05927)
*Bin Liu,Jun Wu,Haoyu Peng,Ao Zhou,Jin Wang,QiaoSong Chen,Grigorios Tsoumakas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于标签特定距离的多标签过采样方法（LSDMLO），通过构建标签特定的加权特征空间来识别标签一致的近邻，生成更有效的合成实例，解决多标签分类中标签分布不平衡和合成实例标签不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过采样方法使用欧氏距离在整个特征空间中检索邻域信息，未考虑特征对不同标签的语义相关性差异，导致近邻间标签不一致，引入标签混淆和过拟合问题。

Method: 提出LSDMLO方法：基于加权相关特征空间推导标签特定距离，识别标签一致的近邻；选择表达更多标签相关性的边界区域种子实例；生成与原始数据标签分布一致的合成实例。

Result: 综合实验验证，在不同基分类器下，LSDMLO性能优于当前最先进的多标签采样方法。

Conclusion: LSDMLO通过标签特定距离解决了标签不一致问题，生成的合成实例更有效，显著提升了多标签不平衡分类的性能。

Abstract: The complex imbalanced label distribution poses a crucial challenge to multi-label classification, as most classifiers are biased towards the majority class and high-frequent labels. Oversampling is an efficient and flexible solution that augments instances to provide a more balanced training dataset for multi-label classifiers. Most existing oversampling methods create synthetic instances in a heuristic way that essentially relies on neighborhood information retrieved using Euclidean distance within the entire feature space. However, they fail to consider the varying semantic relevance of features to different labels, leading to label inconsistency among proximate neighbors and further introducing label confusion and overfitting to synthetic instances.
